# 阿毘達磨雜集論

## 阿毘達磨雜集論 — 1/4 ^1-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第六安慧菩薩糅大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中諦品第一之一 ^1-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2]]

復次略說決擇有四種， ^1-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-3]]

謂諦決擇、 ^1-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-4]]

法決擇、 ^1-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-5]]

得決擇、 ^1-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-6]]

論議決擇。 ^1-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-7]]

諦決擇復有四種， ^1-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-8]]

謂依苦集滅道四聖諦說。 ^1-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-9]]

苦諦云何？ ^1-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-10]]

謂有情生及生所依處， ^1-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-11]]

即有情世間、器世間，如其次第。若生若生處，俱說名苦諦。 ^1-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-12]]

有情生者， ^1-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-13]]

謂諸有情生在地獄、畜生、餓鬼、人、天趣中。 ^1-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-14]]

人謂東毘提訶、 ^1-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-15]]

西瞿陀尼、 ^1-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-16]]

南贍部洲、 ^1-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-17]]

北俱盧洲。 ^1-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-18]]

天謂四大王眾天、 ^1-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-19]]

三十三天、 ^1-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-20]]

夜摩天、 ^1-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-21]]

覩史多天、 ^1-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-22]]

樂變化天、 ^1-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-23]]

他化自在天， ^1-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-24]]

梵眾天、 ^1-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-25]]

梵輔天、 ^1-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-26]]

大梵天、 ^1-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-27]]

少光天、 ^1-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-28]]

無量光天、 ^1-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-29]]

極光淨天、 ^1-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-30]]

少淨天、 ^1-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-31]]

無量淨天、 ^1-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-32]]

遍淨天、 ^1-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-33]]

無雲天、 ^1-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-34]]

福生天、 ^1-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-35]]

廣果天、 ^1-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-36]]

無想有情天、 ^1-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-37]]

無煩天、 ^1-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-38]]

無熱天、 ^1-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-39]]

善現天、 ^1-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-40]]

善見天、 ^1-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-41]]

色究竟天， ^1-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-42]]

無邊空處天、 ^1-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-43]]

無邊識處天、 ^1-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-44]]

無所有處天、 ^1-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-45]]

非想非非想處天。 ^1-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-46]]

生所依處即器世界， ^1-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-47]]

謂水輪依風輪、 ^1-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-48]]

地輪依水輪。 ^1-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-49]]

依此地輪，有蘇迷盧山、 ^1-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-50]]

七金山、 ^1-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-51]]

四大洲、 ^1-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-52]]

八小洲、 ^1-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-53]]

內海 ^1-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-54]]

外海。 ^1-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-55]]

蘇迷盧山四外層級 ^1-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-56]]

四大王眾天， ^1-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-57]]

三十三天所居處別。 ^1-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-58]]

外輪圍山 ^1-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-59]]

虛空宮殿， ^1-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-60]]

若夜摩天、 ^1-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-61]]

覩史多天、 ^1-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-62]]

樂變化天、 ^1-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-63]]

他化自在天， ^1-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-64]]

及色界天所居處別， ^1-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-65]]

諸阿素洛所居處別。 ^1-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-66]]

及諸那落迦所居處別， ^1-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-67]]

謂熱那落迦、 ^1-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-68]]

寒那落迦、 ^1-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-69]]

孤獨那落迦， ^1-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-70]]

及一分傍生餓鬼所居處別。 ^1-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-71]]

乃至一日一月周遍流光所照方處名一世界。 如是千世界中 ^1-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-72]]

有千日千月、 ^1-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-73]]

千蘇迷盧山王、 ^1-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-74]]

千四大洲、 ^1-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-75]]

千四大王眾天、 ^1-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-76]]

千三十三天、 ^1-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-77]]

千夜摩天、 ^1-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-78]]

千覩史多天、 ^1-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-79]]

千樂變化天、 ^1-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-80]]

千他化自在天、 ^1-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-81]]

千梵世天， ^1-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-82]]

如是總名小千世界。 ^1-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-83]]

千小千界總名第二中千世界。 ^1-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-84]]

千中千世界總名第三大千世界。 ^1-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-85]]

如此三千大千世界，總有大輪圍山周匝圍繞。 ^1-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-86]]

又此三千大千世界同壞同成， ^1-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-87]]

譬如天雨滴如車軸無間無斷從空下注。 ^1-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-88]]

如是東方無間無斷無量世界，或有將壞、或有將成、 ^1-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-89]]

或有正壞、 ^1-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-90]]

或壞已住、 ^1-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-91]]

或有正成、 ^1-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-92]]

或成已住。 ^1-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-93]]

如於東方乃至一切十方亦爾。 ^1-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-94]]

如是若有情世間若器世間，業煩惱力所生故、 ^1-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-95]]

業煩惱增上所起故，總名苦諦。 ^1-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-96]]

業煩惱力所生、業煩惱增上所起者，此二句如其次第， ^1-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-97]]

顯有情世間及器世間俱是苦性。 ^1-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-98]]

復有清淨世界非苦諦攝， ^1-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-99]]

非業煩惱力所生故、 ^1-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-100]]

非業煩惱增上所起故， ^1-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-101]]

然由大願清淨善根增上所引。 ^1-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-102]]

此所生處不可思議，唯佛所覺， ^1-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-103]]

尚非得靜慮者靜慮境界， ^1-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-104]]

況尋思者。 ^1-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-105]]

復次已略辯苦諦相， ^1-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-106]]

今當廣顯諸經所說苦相差別， ^1-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-107]]

所謂生苦、 ^1-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-108]]

老苦、 ^1-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-109]]

病苦、 ^1-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-110]]

死苦、 ^1-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-111]]

怨憎會苦、 ^1-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-112]]

愛別離苦、 ^1-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-113]]

求不得苦， ^1-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-114]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦。 ^1-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-115]]

生何因苦？ ^1-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-116]]

眾苦所逼故、 餘苦所依故。 ^1-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-117]]

眾苦所逼者， ^1-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-118]]

謂曾於母胎生熟藏間具受種種極不淨物所逼迫苦， ^1-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-119]]

正出胎時復受支體逼切大苦。 ^1-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-120]]

餘苦所依者，謂有生故老病死等眾苦隨逐。 ^1-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-121]]

老何因苦？ ^1-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-122]]

時分變壞苦故。 ^1-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-123]]

病何因苦？ 大種變異苦故。 ^1-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-124]]

死何因苦？ ^1-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-125]]

壽命變壞苦故。 ^1-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-126]]

怨憎會何因苦？ ^1-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-127]]

合會生苦故。 ^1-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-128]]

愛別離何因苦？ ^1-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-129]]

別離生苦故。 ^1-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-130]]

求不得何因苦？ ^1-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-131]]

所希不果生苦故。 ^1-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-132]]

略攝一切五取蘊何因苦？ 麁重苦故。 ^1-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-133]]

如是八種略攝為六， ^1-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-135]]

謂逼迫苦、 ^1-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-136]]

轉變苦、 ^1-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-137]]

合會苦、 ^1-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-138]]

別離苦、 ^1-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-139]]

所希不果苦、 ^1-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-140]]

麁重苦。 ^1-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-141]]

如是六種廣開為八， ^1-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-142]]

轉變苦中分三種故。 ^1-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-143]]

若六若八平等平等。 ^1-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-144]]

問：如說三苦， ^1-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-145]]

此中八苦為三攝八、 ^1-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-146]]

八攝三耶？ ^1-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-147]]

答：展轉相攝。 ^1-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-148]]

所謂生苦乃至怨憎會苦能顯苦苦， ^1-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-149]]

順苦受法苦自相義故。 ^1-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-150]]

愛別離苦、求不得苦能顯壞苦， ^1-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-151]]

已得未得順樂受法壞自相義故。 ^1-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-152]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦能顯行苦， ^1-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-153]]

不解脫二無常所隨不安隱義故。 ^1-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-154]]

問：如說二苦，謂世俗諦苦、勝義諦苦。 ^1-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-155]]

何者世俗苦？ ^1-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-156]]

何者勝義苦？ ^1-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-157]]

答：生苦乃至求不得苦， ^1-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-158]]

是世俗諦苦， ^1-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-159]]

世間智境界故。 ^1-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-160]]

略攝一切五取蘊苦，是勝義諦苦， ^1-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-161]]

由安立真如門出世智境界故。 ^1-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-163]]

復次諸觀行者於苦聖諦以四種行觀察共相， ^1-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-164]]

謂無常相、 ^1-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-165]]

苦相、 ^1-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-166]]

空相、 ^1-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-167]]

無我相。 ^1-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-168]]

無常相者略有十二， ^1-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-169]]

謂非有相、 ^1-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-170]]

壞滅相、 ^1-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-171]]

變異相、 ^1-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-172]]

別離相、 ^1-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-173]]

現前相、 ^1-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-174]]

法爾相、 ^1-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-175]]

剎那相、 ^1-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-176]]

相續相、 ^1-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-177]]

病等相、 ^1-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-178]]

種種心行轉相、 ^1-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-179]]

資產興衰相、 ^1-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-180]]

器世成壞相。 ^1-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-181]]

非有相者，謂蘊界處於一切時我我所性常非有故。 ^1-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-182]]

言無常者，是非有義， ^1-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-183]]

由苦聖諦恒無有我我所自性。 ^1-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-184]]

無者，是除遣義。 ^1-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-185]]

常者，是一切時義。 ^1-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-186]]

以常無故，名曰無常。 ^1-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-187]]

壞滅相者， ^1-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-188]]

謂諸行生已即滅、 ^1-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-189]]

暫有還無故。 ^1-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-190]]

變異相者， ^1-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-191]]

謂諸行異異生， ^1-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-192]]

由不相似相續轉故。 ^1-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-193]]

別離相者，謂於諸行失增上力，或他所攝執為己有， ^1-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-194]]

以於資具等事或時自在失壞、或他陵奪為己有故。 ^1-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-195]]

現前相者， ^1-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-196]]

謂正處無常， ^1-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-197]]

由因隨逐今受無常故。 ^1-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-198]]

法爾相者， ^1-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-199]]

謂當來無常，由因隨逐定當受故，死無常性決定當受。 ^1-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-200]]

剎那相者， ^1-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-201]]

謂諸行剎那後必不住， ^1-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-202]]

諸行念念劣得自體無間必壞故。 ^1-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-203]]

相續相者， ^1-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-204]]

謂無始時來諸行生滅相續不斷， ^1-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-205]]

由無始生死展轉相乘輪迴不絕故。 ^1-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-206]]

病等相者，謂四大時分壽命變異， ^1-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-207]]

由四大乖違齒髮疎落等，住時勢盡故。 ^1-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-208]]

種種心行轉相者， ^1-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-209]]

謂於一時起有貪心、 ^1-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-210]]

或於一時起離貪心， ^1-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-211]]

如是有瞋 ^1-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-212]]

離瞋、 ^1-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-213]]

有癡 ^1-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-214]]

離癡， ^1-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-215]]

若合若散、 ^1-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-216]]

若下若舉、 ^1-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-217]]

若掉 ^1-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-218]]

離掉、 ^1-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-219]]

若不寂靜 ^1-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-220]]

若寂靜、 ^1-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-221]]

若定 ^1-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-222]]

不定， ^1-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-223]]

如是等心行流轉， ^1-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-224]]

由住能治所治位差別故。 ^1-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-225]]

資產興衰相者， ^1-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-226]]

謂諸興盛終歸衰變， ^1-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-227]]

由諸世間富貴榮盛不可愛樂非究竟故。 ^1-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-228]]

器世成壞相者， ^1-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-229]]

謂火水風三種成壞， ^1-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-230]]

由火水風災令大地等數成壞故，能燒浸燥，如其次第。 ^1-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-231]]

又有三災頂， ^1-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-232]]

謂第二第三第四靜慮， ^1-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-234]]

{由火水風災令大地等數成壞故，能燒浸燥，如其次第。}由火水風能壞世界安立處所，乃至第一第二第三靜慮邊際。 ^1-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-235]]

次上所餘，名三災頂，如其次第第二第三第四靜慮處所差別。 ^1-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-236]]

第四靜慮外宮殿等，雖無外災成壞，然彼諸天與宮殿等俱生俱滅，說有成壞。 ^1-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-237]]

由彼有情初生時與宮殿等俱生、終沒時與彼俱滅， ^1-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-238]]

即說此為成壞故。 ^1-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-239]]

又有三種中劫， ^1-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-240]]

所謂飢饉、 ^1-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-241]]

疫病、 ^1-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-242]]

刀兵， ^1-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-243]]

此小三災劫究竟滿方乃出現。 ^1-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-244]]

謂世界成已 ^1-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-245]]

一中劫初唯減， ^1-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-246]]

一中劫後唯增， 十八中劫亦增亦減。 ^1-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-249]]

一中劫初唯減者，謂劫成時第二十一劫。 ^1-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-250]]

一中劫後唯增者，謂最後劫。 ^1-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-251]]

十八中劫亦增亦減者，謂於中間十八。 ^1-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-252]]

二十中劫世界正壞， ^1-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-253]]

二十中劫世界壞已住， ^1-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-254]]

二十中劫世界正成， ^1-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-255]]

二十中劫世界成已住， ^1-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-256]]

合此八十中劫為一大劫。 ^1-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-257]]

由此劫數顯色無色界諸天壽量， ^1-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-258]]

如說以壽盡故、福盡故、業盡故，彼彼有情從彼彼處沒。 ^1-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-259]]

云何壽盡？ ^1-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-260]]

謂時死， ^1-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-261]]

此約時沒說，由所引壽時分究竟應時死故。 ^1-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-262]]

云何福盡？ ^1-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-263]]

謂非時死， ^1-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-264]]

即非福死，此約非時沒說。以彼有情貪著定味，福力減盡因此命終， ^1-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-265]]

由愛定味損害所修引壽業力非時死故。 ^1-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-266]]

云何業盡？ ^1-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-267]]

謂順生受業、順後受業俱盡故死，此約相續沒說， ^1-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-268]]

由於此處順生後受業受用斯盡，以無業故不復生此。 ^1-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-269]]

苦相者， ^1-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-270]]

或三或八或六，廣說如前。 ^1-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-271]]

何故經說若無常者即是苦耶？ ^1-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-272]]

由三分無常為緣，苦相可了知故。 ^1-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-273]]

謂生分無常、 ^1-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-274]]

滅分無常、 ^1-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-275]]

俱分無常。 ^1-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-276]]

生分無常為緣故，苦苦性可了知。 ^1-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-277]]

生分無常者， ^1-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-278]]

謂本無今有， ^1-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-279]]

苦品諸行體是逼迫， ^1-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-280]]

由此無常為緣，苦苦性可了知。 ^1-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-281]]

滅分無常為緣， ^1-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-282]]

壞苦性可了知。 ^1-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-283]]

滅分無常者，謂已有還無， ^1-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-284]]

樂品諸行不可愛樂， ^1-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-285]]

由此無常為緣，壞苦性可了知。 ^1-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-286]]

俱分無常為緣， ^1-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-287]]

行苦性可了知。 ^1-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-288]]

俱分無常者， ^1-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-289]]

謂麁重諸行相續流轉，若生若滅俱不可樂。 ^1-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-290]]

由此俱分無常為緣，行苦性可了知。 ^1-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-291]]

即依此義，薄伽梵說：諸行無常，諸行變壞。又依此義， ^1-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-292]]

言：諸所有受我說皆苦。 ^1-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-294]]

當知此中於不苦不樂受及樂受，密意故說苦苦。 ^1-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-295]]

受苦性，世間共了故， ^1-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-296]]

不復密意說。 ^1-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-297]]

又於生滅二法所隨諸行中有生等八苦，性可了知故， ^1-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-298]]

佛說言：若無常者即是苦。 ^1-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-299]]

又於無常諸行中有生等苦可了者， ^1-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-300]]

如來依此密意說言：由無常故苦，非一切行。 ^1-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-301]]

若不爾，聖道無常故亦應是苦。 ^1-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-302]]

空相者，謂若於是處此非有，由此理正觀為空； ^1-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-303]]

若於是處餘是有，由此理如實知有。 ^1-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-304]]

是名善入空性。 ^1-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-305]]

如實知者，不顛倒義。 ^1-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-306]]

問：於何處誰非有？ ^1-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-307]]

答於蘊界處常恒凝住不變壞法我我所等非有， ^1-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-308]]

由此理彼皆是空。 ^1-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-309]]

問：於是處誰餘有？ ^1-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-310]]

答：即此處無我性。此我無性、無我有性，是謂空性。 ^1-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-312]]

由彼諸行常等相我此中無故。 ^1-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-313]]

諸行恒時離我性相，無我真性此中有故，非一向無，此俱名空性。 ^1-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-314]]

故薄伽梵密意說言：有如實知有，無如實知無。 ^1-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-315]]

又有三種空性， ^1-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-316]]

謂自性空性、 ^1-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-317]]

如性空性、 ^1-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-318]]

真性空性。 ^1-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-319]]

初依遍計所執自性觀，由此自相定非有故。 ^1-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-320]]

第二依依他起自性觀，由此如所計度皆非有故。 ^1-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-321]]

第三依圓成實自性觀，由此即空真性故。 ^1-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-322]]

無我相者， ^1-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-323]]

謂如我論者所立我相蘊界處非此相，由蘊界處我相無故，名無我相。 ^1-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-324]]

我論外道計度諸行為我，彼諸行非此相故名無我。 ^1-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-325]]

故薄伽梵密意說言：一切法皆無我。 ^1-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-326]]

如世尊說：此一切非我所， ^1-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-327]]

此非我處、 ^1-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-328]]

此非我我。 ^1-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-329]]

於如是義，應以正慧如實觀察。 此言何義？ ^1-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-330]]

謂於外事密意說此一切非我所，於內事密意說此非我處、此非我我所。所以者何？ ^1-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-331]]

以於外事唯計我所相，是故但遣我所。於內事通計我我所相，是故雙遣我我所。 ^1-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-332]]

問：前說無常皆剎那相。此云何知？ ^1-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-333]]

答：如心心法是剎那相， ^1-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-334]]

當知色等亦剎那相， ^1-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-335]]

由心執受故、 ^1-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-336]]

等心安危故、 ^1-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-337]]

隨心轉變故、 ^1-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-338]]

是心所依故、 ^1-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-339]]

心增上生故、 ^1-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-340]]

心自在轉故。 ^1-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-341]]

又於最後位變壞可得故、 ^1-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-342]]

生已不待緣自然壞滅故， ^1-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-343]]

當觀色等亦念念滅。 ^1-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-344]]

諸無常行壞滅等相、心心法剎那相，世間共了，更不重辯。諸色等法剎那滅相，世不共了，故今成立。 ^1-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-345]]

由心執受者，謂色等身，由剎那心念念執受故剎那滅。 ^1-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-347]]

等心安危者，謂色等身恒與識俱，識若捨離即便爛壞， ^1-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-348]]

由身與心安危等故， ^1-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-349]]

決定如心念念生滅。 ^1-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-350]]

隨心轉變者， ^1-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-351]]

謂世間現見，心在苦樂貪瞋等位身隨轉變， ^1-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-352]]

隨剎那心而轉變故身念念滅。 ^1-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-353]]

是心所依者，謂世間共知，心依止有根身。 ^1-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-354]]

若法依此生， ^1-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-355]]

非此自無壞。 ^1-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-356]]

能依有可見， ^1-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-357]]

如火芽等依薪種等。 ^1-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-358]]

是故此身是剎那心所依止故，亦剎那滅。 ^1-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-359]]

心增上生者， ^1-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-360]]

謂一切內外色皆心增上所生， ^1-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-361]]

能生因剎那滅故，所生果亦剎那滅。 ^1-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-362]]

如世尊說：諸因諸緣能生於色，彼亦無常，無常因緣力所生故，色云何常？隨此經句義，身定剎那滅。 ^1-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-363]]

心自在轉者， ^1-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-364]]

謂若證得勝威德心， ^1-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-365]]

於一切色如其所欲自在轉變，由隨剎那能變勝解轉變生故，色等剎那道理成就。 ^1-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-366]]

於最後位變壞可得者， ^1-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-367]]

謂諸色等初離自性念念變壞，於最後位欻爾變壞不應道理； ^1-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-368]]

然此可得，故知色等從初已來念念變壞，自類相續漸增為因，能引最後麁相變壞，是故色等念念生滅。 ^1-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-369]]

生已不待緣自然壞滅者， ^1-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-370]]

謂一切法從緣生已，不待壞緣自然壞滅， ^1-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-371]]

以不待餘緣自然壞滅故， ^1-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-372]]

最初生已決定壞滅。 ^1-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-373]]

若言生已初無滅壞、後方有者，不然，無差別故。 ^1-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-374]]

故知一切可滅壞法，初纔生已即便壞滅，是故諸法剎那義成。 ^1-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-375]]

問：如世尊說，諸所有色，彼一切若四大種若四大種所造。此依何意說？ ^1-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-376]]

答：依容有意，說同在一處。依此而有，是造義。 ^1-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-377]]

由所造色離大種處別自建立無功能故， ^1-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-378]]

若於此聚此大種可得，當知此聚唯有此大種非餘。 ^1-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-379]]

或有聚唯一大種， ^1-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-380]]

如乾泥團等。 ^1-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-381]]

或有二大種， ^1-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-382]]

謂即此濕。 ^1-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-383]]

或有三大種， ^1-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-384]]

謂即此煖。 ^1-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-385]]

或有一切大種， ^1-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-386]]

謂即此濕煖泥團等。 ^1-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-387]]

於移轉位所造色亦爾。若於此聚此所造色可得，當知此聚唯此非餘。 ^1-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-388]]

或有聚唯一所造色， ^1-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-389]]

如光等。 ^1-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-390]]

或有二所造色， ^1-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-391]]

如有聲香風等。 ^1-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-392]]

或有三所造色， ^1-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-393]]

如香煙等。 ^1-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-394]]

由此香煙有色香觸差別所顯故。 ^1-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-395]]

觸差別者，謂此中輕性。 ^1-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-397]]

或有四所造色， ^1-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-398]]

如沙糖團等。 ^1-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-399]]

或有五所造色， ^1-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-400]]

謂即此有聲時。 ^1-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-401]]

又若於此聚大種造色隨分可得，當知此中有此非餘。 ^1-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-402]]

此依麁物說非種子，一一聚中有一切種子故。 ^1-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-403]]

又說麁聚色極微集所成者，當知此中極微無體無實無性，唯假建立， ^1-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-404]]

展轉分析無限量故。 ^1-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-405]]

但由覺慧漸漸分析， ^1-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-406]]

細分損減乃至可析邊際，即約此際建立極微。 ^1-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-407]]

問：若諸極微無實體性，何故建立？ ^1-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-408]]

答：為遣一合想故。 ^1-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-409]]

若以覺慧分分分析所有諸色， ^1-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-410]]

爾時妄執一切諸色為一合想即便捨離， ^1-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-411]]

由此順入數取趣無我性故。 ^1-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-412]]

又為悟入諸所有色非真實故。 ^1-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-413]]

若以覺慧如是分析所有諸色至無所有， ^1-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-414]]

爾時便能悟入諸色皆非真實， ^1-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-415]]

因此悟入唯識道理，由此順入諸法無我性故。 ^1-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-417]]

復次苦法略有八種差別，謂有廣大不寂靜苦、 ^1-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-418]]

有寂靜苦、 ^1-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-419]]

有寂靜不寂靜苦、 ^1-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-420]]

有中不寂靜苦、 ^1-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-421]]

有微薄不寂靜苦、 ^1-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-422]]

有微薄寂靜苦、 ^1-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-423]]

有極微薄寂靜苦、 ^1-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-424]]

有非苦似苦住大寂靜。 ^1-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-425]]

云何廣大不寂靜苦？ ^1-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-426]]

謂生欲界未曾積集諸善根者， ^1-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-427]]

由欲界一切生趣苦具足所顯故、未集善根不能遮止往諸趣故， ^1-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-428]]

如其次第名廣大不寂靜苦。 ^1-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-429]]

云何寂靜苦？ ^1-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-430]]

謂即此已生順解脫分善根者，決定趣向般涅槃故。 ^1-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-431]]

云何寂靜不寂靜苦？ ^1-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-432]]

謂即此為世間道離欲已種善根者， ^1-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-433]]

由即此欲界苦為世間道離欲已種善根者決定超越苦苦等故， ^1-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-434]]

然非畢竟故，如其次第。 ^1-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-435]]

如是中不寂靜苦等隨所應當釋。 ^1-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-436]]

云何中不寂靜苦？ ^1-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-437]]

謂生色界遠離順解脫分者。 ^1-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-438]]

云何微薄不寂靜苦？ ^1-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-439]]

謂生無色界遠離順解脫分者。 ^1-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-440]]

云何微薄寂靜苦？ ^1-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-441]]

謂諸有學。 ^1-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-442]]

云何極微薄寂靜苦？ ^1-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-443]]

謂諸無學命根住緣六處。 ^1-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-444]]

云何非苦似苦住大寂靜？ ^1-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-445]]

謂已得究竟菩薩摩訶薩等， ^1-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-446]]

乘大悲願力故 ^1-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-447]]

生諸有中，由能除滅無量眾生相續大苦故，名住大寂靜。 ^1-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-448]]

復次前說死苦。 ^1-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-449]]

死有三種， ^1-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-450]]

謂或善心死、 ^1-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-451]]

或不善心死、 ^1-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-452]]

或無記心死。 ^1-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-453]]

善心死者，謂於明利心現行位，或由自善根力所持故、或由他所引攝故，發起善心趣命終位。 ^1-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-454]]

不善心死者，謂亦於明利心現行位，或由自不善根力所持故、或由他所引攝故，起不善心趣命終位。 ^1-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-455]]

無記心死者，謂若於明利心現行位、若於不明利心現行位， ^1-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-456]]

或由闕二緣故、 ^1-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-457]]

或由加行無功能故， ^1-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-458]]

起無記心趣命終位。 ^1-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-459]]

此中所言善等心死者， ^1-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-460]]

當知依我愛相應將命終心位前說。 ^1-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-461]]

修淨行者臨命終位，於身下分先起冷觸。 ^1-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-462]]

不淨行者臨命終位，於身上分先起冷觸。 ^1-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-463]]

又不淨行者中有生時， ^1-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-464]]

其相顯現如黑羊羔光，或如陰闇夜分。 ^1-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-465]]

修淨行者中有生時， ^1-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-466]]

其相顯現如白練光，或如晴明夜分。 ^1-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-467]]

又此中有，在欲色界正受生位， ^1-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-468]]

亦從無色界命終後位， ^1-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-469]]

亦名意生健達縛等， ^1-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-470]]

極住七日，或有中夭，或時移轉。 ^1-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-471]]

言意生者，謂受化生身，唯心為因故。 ^1-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-472]]

香所引故， ^1-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-473]]

名健達縛，是隨逐於香往受生處義。 ^1-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-474]]

極住七日 ^1-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-475]]

或有中夭者， ^1-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-476]]

此約速得生緣者說。 ^1-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-477]]

若過七日不得生緣， ^1-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-478]]

必定命終還生中有。 ^1-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-479]]

如是展轉乃至七返，更不得過。 ^1-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-480]]

或時移轉者， ^1-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-481]]

謂於此位往餘生處，強緣現前， ^1-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-482]]

如得第四靜慮起阿羅漢增上慢比丘， ^1-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-483]]

彼地中有生時， ^1-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-484]]

由謗解脫邪見故， ^1-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-485]]

轉生地獄中有。 ^1-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-486]]

又住中有中亦能集諸業， ^1-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-487]]

先串習力所引善等思現行故。 ^1-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-488]]

又能覩見同類有情， ^1-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-489]]

謂先所共行善不善者，如於夢中見己與彼現同遊戲。 ^1-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-490]]

又中有形， ^1-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-491]]

似當生處如當生處前時有形而起故。 ^1-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-492]]

又此中有所趣無礙， ^1-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-493]]

如具神通往來迅速，仍於生處有所拘礙。 ^1-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-494]]

又此中有於所生處，如稱兩頭低昂道理，終沒結生時分亦爾。 ^1-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-495]]

又住中有中，於所生處發起貪愛， ^1-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-496]]

亦用餘煩惱為緣助。 ^1-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-497]]

此中有身與貪俱滅、羯邏藍身與識俱生， ^1-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-498]]

此唯是異熟。 ^1-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-499]]

自此已後根漸生長，如緣起中說於四生類，或受卵生、或受胎生、 ^1-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-500]]

或受濕生、或受化生。 ^1-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-501]]

如緣起中說者， ^1-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-502]]

謂名色等前後次第， ^1-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-503]]

如說：最初羯邏藍， ^1-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-504]]

次生頞部曇， ^1-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-505]]

從此生閉尸， ^1-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-506]]

閉尸生鍵南， ^1-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-507]]

次鉢羅奢佉， ^1-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-508]]

後髮毛爪等， ^1-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-509]]

及色根形相，漸漸而生長。 ^1-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-511]]

云何集諦？ ^1-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-512]]

謂諸煩惱及煩惱增上所生諸業， ^1-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-513]]

俱說名集諦，由此集起生死苦故。 ^1-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-515]]

煩惱增上所生業者， ^1-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-516]]

謂有漏業。 ^1-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-517]]

若爾，何故世尊唯說愛為集諦？ ^1-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-518]]

由最勝故。 ^1-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-519]]

謂薄伽梵隨勝而說，若愛、若後有愛、 ^1-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-520]]

若貪喜俱行愛、若彼彼希樂愛，是名集諦。 ^1-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-521]]

言最勝者，是遍行義，由愛具有六遍行義，是故最勝。 ^1-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-522]]

何等為六？ 一、事遍行，謂於一切已得未得自身境界事遍行故。 ^1-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-523]]

於已得自身起愛， ^1-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-524]]

於未得自身起後有愛， ^1-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-525]]

於已得境界起貪喜俱行愛， ^1-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-526]]

於未得境界起彼彼希樂愛。 ^1-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-527]]

二、位遍行，謂於苦苦性等三位諸行中遍隨行故。 ^1-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-528]]

於已得苦苦性位起別離愛， ^1-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-529]]

於未得苦苦性位起不和合愛， ^1-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-530]]

於壞苦性位起不別離愛及和合愛， ^1-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-531]]

已得未得差別故。 ^1-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-532]]

於行苦性位起愚癡愛， ^1-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-533]]

由煩惱麁重所顯故， ^1-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-534]]

及不苦不樂受所顯故。 ^1-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-535]]

唯阿賴耶識是最勝行苦位， ^1-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-536]]

依止此位因我癡門貪愛轉故。 ^1-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-537]]

三、世遍行，謂於三世中遍隨行故。 ^1-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-538]]

於過去世起追憶行遍隨行愛， ^1-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-539]]

於未來世起希樂行遍隨行愛， ^1-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-540]]

於現在世起耽著行遍隨行愛。 ^1-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-541]]

四、界遍行，謂欲色無色三愛次第遍三界故。 ^1-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-542]]

五、求遍行，謂由貪愛遍求欲有邪梵行故。 ^1-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-543]]

由欲求力，不脫欲界，招欲界苦。 ^1-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-544]]

由有求力，不脫二界，招色無色界苦。 ^1-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-545]]

由邪梵行求力，不脫生死，彼彼流轉故。 ^1-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-546]]

六種遍行，謂有無有愛遍行，斷常一切種故。 ^1-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-547]]

煩惱者， ^1-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-548]]

謂由數故、相故、 ^1-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-549]]

緣起故、 ^1-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-550]]

境界故、相應故、 ^1-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-551]]

差別故、邪行故、 ^1-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-552]]

界故、 ^1-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-553]]

眾故、 ^1-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-554]]

斷故，觀諸煩惱。 ^1-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-555]]

數者，謂或六或十。 ^1-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-556]]

六謂貪、瞋、慢、無明、疑、見。 ^1-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-557]]

十謂前五 ^1-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-558]]

見又分五， ^1-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-559]]

謂薩迦耶見、 ^1-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-560]]

邊執見、 ^1-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-561]]

邪見、 ^1-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-562]]

見取、 ^1-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-563]]

戒禁取。 ^1-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-564]]

相者， ^1-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-565]]

若法生時 ^1-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-566]]

相不寂靜， ^1-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-567]]

由此生故身心相續不寂靜轉，是煩惱相。 ^1-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-568]]

不寂靜性 ^1-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-569]]

是諸煩惱共相， ^1-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-570]]

此復有六， ^1-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-571]]

謂散亂不寂靜性、 ^1-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-572]]

顛倒不寂靜性、 ^1-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-573]]

掉舉不寂靜性、 ^1-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-574]]

惛沈不寂靜性、 ^1-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-575]]

放逸不寂靜性、 ^1-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-576]]

無恥不寂靜性。 ^1-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-577]]

緣起者，謂煩惱隨眠未永斷故、 ^1-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-578]]

順煩惱法現在前故、 ^1-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-579]]

不正思惟現前起故， ^1-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-580]]

如是煩惱方乃得生。 ^1-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-581]]

煩惱隨眠未永斷者， ^1-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-582]]

彼品麁重未永拔故。 ^1-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-583]]

順煩惱法現在前者， ^1-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-584]]

現前會遇可愛等境故。 ^1-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-585]]

不正思惟現前起者， ^1-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-586]]

於彼境界取淨等相， ^1-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-587]]

能隨順生貪瞋等故。 ^1-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-588]]

境界者， ^1-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-589]]

謂一切煩惱還用一切煩惱為所緣境，及緣諸煩惱事。 ^1-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-590]]

又欲界煩惱， ^1-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-591]]

除無明、見、疑，餘不能緣上地為境。 ^1-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-592]]

此無明等雖亦有能緣上地者，然彼不能親緣上地如緣自地。 ^1-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-593]]

由依彼門起分別故，立彼為所緣。 ^1-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-594]]

所言無明緣上地者，謂與見等相應見者， ^1-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-595]]

除薩迦耶見， ^1-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-596]]

不見世間，緣他地諸行執為我故。 ^1-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-597]]

上地諸煩惱不緣下地為境，已離彼地欲故。 ^1-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-598]]

又緣滅道諦諸煩惱，不能親緣滅道為境， ^1-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-599]]

由滅道諦出世間智及後得智內所證故。 ^1-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-600]]

唯由依彼妄起分別說為所緣，分別所計境不離分別故。 ^1-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-601]]

又煩惱有二種，謂緣無事及緣有事。 ^1-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-602]]

緣無事者， ^1-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-603]]

謂見及見相應法， ^1-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-604]]

見謂薩迦耶見及邊執見。 ^1-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-605]]

所餘煩惱，名緣有事。 ^1-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-606]]

相應者，謂貪不與瞋相應， ^1-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-607]]

如瞋疑亦爾， ^1-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-608]]

餘皆得相應。 ^1-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-609]]

何故貪不與瞋相應？ ^1-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-610]]

一向相違法，必不俱轉故。 ^1-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-611]]

又貪不與疑相應者， ^1-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-612]]

由慧於境不決定必無染著故。 ^1-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-613]]

餘得相應者，與餘慢等不相違故。 ^1-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-615]]

如貪瞋亦爾，謂瞋不與貪慢見相應。 ^1-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-616]]

若於此事起憎恚，即不於此生於高舉及能推求。 ^1-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-617]]

與餘相應， 如理應知， ^1-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-618]]

慢不與瞋疑相應。 ^1-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-619]]

無明有二：一一切煩惱相應無明、二不共無明。 ^1-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-620]]

不共無明者，謂於諦無智。 ^1-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-621]]

見不與瞋疑相應。 ^1-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-622]]

疑不與貪慢見相應。 ^1-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-623]]

忿等隨煩惱更互不相應， ^1-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-624]]

展轉相違法必不相應故。 ^1-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-625]]

如貪分與瞋分若不相違， ^1-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-626]]

猶如煩惱展轉相應。 ^1-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-627]]

無慚無愧於一切不善品中恒共相應， ^1-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-628]]

若離不顧自他不善現行無是處故。 ^1-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-629]]

惛沈、 ^1-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-630]]

掉舉、 ^1-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-631]]

不信、 ^1-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-632]]

懈怠、 ^1-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-633]]

放逸，於一切染污品中恒共相應， ^1-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-634]]

若離無堪任性等染污性成無是處故。 ^1-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-635]]

差別者， ^1-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-636]]

謂諸煩惱依種種義立種種門差別。 ^1-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-637]]

所謂結、 ^1-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-638]]

縛、 ^1-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-639]]

隨眠、 ^1-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-640]]

隨煩惱、 ^1-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-641]]

纏、 ^1-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-642]]

瀑流、 ^1-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-643]]

軛、 ^1-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-644]]

取、 ^1-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-645]]

繫、 ^1-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-646]]

蓋、 ^1-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-647]]

株杌、 ^1-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-648]]

垢、 ^1-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-649]]

燒害、 ^1-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-650]]

箭、 ^1-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-651]]

所有、 ^1-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-652]]

惡行、 ^1-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-653]]

漏、 ^1-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-654]]

匱、 ^1-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-655]]

熱、 ^1-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-656]]

惱、 ^1-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-657]]

諍、 ^1-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-658]]

熾然、 ^1-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-659]]

稠林、 ^1-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-660]]

拘礙等。 ^1-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-661]]

問：結有幾種？ ^1-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-662]]

云何結？何處結耶？ ^1-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-663]]

答：結有九種， ^1-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-664]]

謂愛結、 ^1-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-665]]

恚結、 ^1-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-666]]

慢結、 ^1-666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-667]]

無明結、 ^1-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-668]]

見結、 ^1-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-669]]

取結、 ^1-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-670]]

疑結、 ^1-670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-671]]

嫉結、 ^1-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-672]]

慳結。 ^1-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-673]]

愛結者， ^1-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-674]]

謂三界貪愛， ^1-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-675]]

結所繫故不厭三界， ^1-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-676]]

由不厭故廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-677]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-678]]

當知此中宣說諸結若相若用若位，辯結差別。 ^1-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-679]]

且如愛結，何等是結？ ^1-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-680]]

謂三界貪是結自性。 ^1-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-681]]

云何為結？ ^1-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-682]]

謂有此者不厭三界，由此展轉不善現行、善不現行。 ^1-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-683]]

於何位結？ ^1-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-684]]

謂於後世苦果生位。 ^1-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-685]]

如是恚結等並如理應知。 ^1-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-686]]

恚結者， ^1-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-687]]

謂於有情苦及順苦法心有損害， ^1-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-688]]

恚結所繫故。 於恚境相心不棄捨， ^1-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-689]]

不棄捨故廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-690]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-691]]

慢結者， ^1-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-692]]

即七慢， ^1-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-693]]

謂慢、 ^1-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-694]]

過慢、 ^1-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-695]]

慢過慢、 ^1-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-696]]

我慢、 ^1-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-697]]

增上慢、 ^1-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-698]]

下劣慢、 ^1-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-699]]

邪慢。 ^1-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-700]]

慢者， ^1-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-701]]

謂於下劣計己為勝、或於相似計己相似，心舉為性。 ^1-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-702]]

過慢者， ^1-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-703]]

謂於相似計己為勝、或復於勝計己相似，心舉為性。 ^1-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-704]]

慢過慢者， ^1-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-705]]

謂於勝己計己為勝，心舉為性。 ^1-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-706]]

我慢者， ^1-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-707]]

於五取蘊觀我我所，心舉為性。 ^1-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-708]]

增上慢者，謂於未得上勝證法計己已得上勝證法，心舉為性。 ^1-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-709]]

下劣慢者， ^1-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-710]]

謂於多分勝計己少分劣，心舉為性。 ^1-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-711]]

邪慢者， ^1-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-712]]

謂實無德計己有德，心舉為性。 ^1-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-713]]

慢結所繫故，於我我所不能了知， ^1-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-714]]

不了知故執我我所， ^1-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-715]]

廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-716]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-717]]

無明結者， ^1-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-718]]

謂三界無智。 ^1-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-719]]

無明結所繫故，於苦法集法不能解了， ^1-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-720]]

不解了故廣行不善、不行諸善，由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-721]]

於苦集法不解了者，謂於果性因性有漏諸行所有過患不了知故。 ^1-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-722]]

{不解了故廣行不善、不行諸善，} ^1-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-723]]

{由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。} ^1-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-724]]

見結者， ^1-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-725]]

即三見， ^1-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-726]]

謂薩迦耶見、 ^1-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-727]]

邊執見、 ^1-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-728]]

邪見。 ^1-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-729]]

見結所繫故，於邪出離妄計追求，謂我當解脫 ^1-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-730]]

我所解脫， ^1-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-731]]

既解脫已我當常住、或當斷滅， ^1-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-732]]

又謂佛法中定無解脫。 ^1-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-733]]

如是執著邪出離已，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-734]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-735]]

取結者， ^1-735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-736]]

謂見取、 ^1-736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-737]]

戒禁取。 ^1-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-738]]

取結所繫故，於邪出離方便妄計執著， ^1-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-739]]

棄捨八聖支道， ^1-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-740]]

妄執薩迦耶見等，及彼為先若戒若禁為清淨道。 ^1-740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-741]]

以妄執著邪出離方便故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-742]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-743]]

疑結者， ^1-743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-744]]

謂於諦猶豫。 ^1-744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-745]]

疑結所繫故， ^1-745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-746]]

於佛法僧寶妄生疑惑， ^1-746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-747]]

以疑惑故於三寶所不修正行。 ^1-747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-748]]

以於三寶所不修正行故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-749]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-750]]

嫉結者， ^1-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-751]]

謂耽著利養，不耐他榮，發起心妬。 ^1-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-752]]

嫉結所繫故，愛重利養、不尊敬法。 ^1-752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-753]]

重利養故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-754]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-755]]

慳結者，耽著利養，於資生具其心悋惜。 ^1-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-756]]

慳結所繫故， ^1-756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-757]]

愛重畜積不尊遠離。 ^1-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-758]]

重畜積故，廣行不善、不行諸善， ^1-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-759]]

由此能招未來世苦與苦相應。 ^1-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-760]]

縛有三種， ^1-760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-761]]

謂貪縛、 ^1-761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-762]]

瞋縛、 ^1-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-763]]

癡縛。 ^1-763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-764]]

由貪縛故，縛諸眾生令處壞苦。 ^1-764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-765]]

由瞋縛故，縛諸眾生令處苦苦。 ^1-765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-766]]

由癡縛故，縛諸眾生令處行苦。 ^1-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-767]]

由貪等縛縛處壞苦等者，以貪瞋癡於樂等受常隨眠故。 ^1-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-768]]

又依貪瞋癡故， ^1-768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-769]]

於善方便不得自在， ^1-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-770]]

故名為縛。 ^1-770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-771]]

猶如外縛縛諸眾生，令於二事不得自在： ^1-771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-772]]

一者不得隨意遊行、 ^1-772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-773]]

二者於所住處不得隨意所作。 ^1-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-774]]

當知內法貪瞋癡縛亦復如是。 ^1-774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-775]]

隨眠有七種， ^1-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-776]]

謂欲愛隨眠、 ^1-776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-777]]

瞋恚隨眠、 ^1-777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-778]]

有愛隨眠、 ^1-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-779]]

慢隨眠、 ^1-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-780]]

無明隨眠、 ^1-780
