![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-781]]

見隨眠、 ^1-781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-782]]

疑隨眠。 ^1-782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-783]]

欲愛隨眠者，謂欲貪品麁重。 ^1-783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-784]]

瞋恚隨眠者，謂瞋恚品麁重。 ^1-784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-785]]

有愛隨眠者，謂色無色貪品麁重。 ^1-785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-786]]

慢隨眠者，謂慢品麁重。 ^1-786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-787]]

無明隨眠者，謂無明品麁重。 ^1-787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-788]]

見隨眠者，謂見品麁重。 ^1-788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-789]]

疑隨眠者，謂疑品麁重。 ^1-789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-790]]

若未離欲求者，由欲愛瞋恚隨眠之所隨眠， ^1-790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-791]]

由依彼門此二增長故。 ^1-791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-792]]

未離有求者，由有愛隨眠之所隨眠。 ^1-792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-793]]

未離邪梵行求者，由慢無明見疑隨眠之所隨眠。 ^1-793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-794]]

由彼眾生得少對治，便生憍慢、 ^1-794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-795]]

愚於聖諦、 ^1-795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-796]]

虛妄計度外邪解脫解脫方便，隨其次第。 ^1-796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-797]]

三見二取，如結中說，於佛聖教正法毘柰耶中猶豫疑惑。 ^1-797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-798]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第六大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第七安慧菩薩糅大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中諦品第一之二 ^1-798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-799]]

隨煩惱者，謂 ^1-799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-800]]

所有諸煩惱皆是隨煩惱。 ^1-800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-801]]

有隨煩惱非煩惱， ^1-801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-802]]

謂除煩惱，所餘染污行蘊所攝一切心法。 ^1-802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-803]]

此復云何？ ^1-803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-804]]

謂除貪等六煩惱，所餘染污行蘊所攝忿等諸心法。 ^1-804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-805]]

又貪瞋癡名隨煩惱心法， ^1-805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-806]]

由此隨煩惱隨惱於心，令不離染、 ^1-806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-807]]

令不解脫、 ^1-807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-808]]

令不斷障故， ^1-808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-809]]

名隨煩惱。 ^1-809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-810]]

如世尊說： ^1-810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-811]]

汝等長夜為貪瞋癡隨所惱亂心恒染污。 ^1-811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-812]]

纏有八種， ^1-812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-813]]

謂惛沈、 ^1-813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-814]]

睡眠、 ^1-814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-815]]

掉舉、 ^1-815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-816]]

惡作、 ^1-816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-817]]

嫉、 ^1-817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-818]]

慳、 ^1-818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-819]]

無慚、 ^1-819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-820]]

無愧， ^1-820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-822]]

數數增盛纏繞於心，故名纏。 ^1-822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-823]]

由此諸纏數數增盛纏繞一切觀行者心，於修善品為障礙故。 ^1-823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-824]]

修善品者，謂隨修習止舉捨相 ^1-824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-825]]

及彼所依梵行等所攝淨尸羅時。 ^1-825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-826]]

此復云何？謂修止時惛沈、睡眠為障， ^1-826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-827]]

於內引沈沒故。 ^1-827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-828]]

修舉時掉舉、惡作為障， ^1-828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-829]]

於外引散亂故。 ^1-829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-830]]

修捨時嫉慳為障， ^1-830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-831]]

由成就此於自他利悋妬門中數數搖動心故。 ^1-831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-832]]

修淨尸羅時無慚、無愧為障， ^1-832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-833]]

由具此二犯諸學處無羞恥故。 ^1-833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-834]]

暴流有四， ^1-834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-835]]

謂欲暴流、 ^1-835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-836]]

有暴流、 ^1-836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-837]]

見暴流、 ^1-837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-838]]

無明暴流。 ^1-838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-839]]

隨流漂鼓是暴流義， ^1-839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-840]]

隨順雜染故。 ^1-840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-842]]

初是習欲求者， ^1-842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-843]]

第二是習有求者， ^1-843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-844]]

後二是習邪梵行求者， ^1-844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-845]]

能依所依相應道理故。 ^1-845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-846]]

見暴流是能依， ^1-846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-847]]

無明暴流為所依， ^1-847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-848]]

由有愚癡顛倒推求解脫及方便故。 ^1-848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-849]]

軛有四種， ^1-849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-850]]

謂欲軛、 ^1-850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-851]]

有軛、 ^1-851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-852]]

見軛、 ^1-852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-853]]

無明軛。 ^1-853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-854]]

障礙離繫是軛義，違背清淨故。 ^1-854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-855]]

此亦隨其次第習三求者 ^1-855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-856]]

相應現行。 ^1-856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-857]]

取有四種， ^1-857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-858]]

謂欲取、 ^1-858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-859]]

見取、 ^1-859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-860]]

戒禁取、 ^1-860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-861]]

我語取。 ^1-861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-862]]

執取諍根、 ^1-862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-863]]

執取後有，是取義。 ^1-863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-864]]

所以者何？ ^1-864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-865]]

由貪著欲繫縛耽染為因， ^1-865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-866]]

諸在家者更相鬪諍， ^1-866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-867]]

此諍根本是第一取。 ^1-867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-868]]

由貪著見繫縛耽染為因， ^1-868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-869]]

諸出家者更相鬪諍， ^1-869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-870]]

此諍根本是後三取。 ^1-870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-871]]

六十二見趣是見取。 ^1-871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-872]]

各別禁戒多分苦行是戒禁取。 ^1-872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-873]]

彼所依止薩迦耶見是我語取。 ^1-873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-874]]

由見取、戒禁取，諸外道輩更相諍論， ^1-874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-875]]

以於是處見不一故。 ^1-875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-876]]

由我語取，諸外道輩互無諍論， ^1-876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-877]]

於我有性皆同見故。 ^1-877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-878]]

然由此取，諸外道等與正法者互有諍論， ^1-878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-879]]

由彼不信有無我故。 ^1-879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-880]]

如是執著諍論根本，復能引取後有苦異熟， ^1-880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-881]]

故名為取。 ^1-881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-882]]

繫有四種， ^1-882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-883]]

謂貪欲身繫、瞋恚身繫、 ^1-883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-884]]

戒禁取身繫、 ^1-884

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-885]]

此實執取身繫。 ^1-885

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-886]]

以能障礙定意性身，故名為繫。 ^1-886

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-887]]

所以者何？ ^1-887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-888]]

由此能障定心自性之身故名繫，非障色身。 ^1-888

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-889]]

何以故？能為四種心亂因故。 ^1-889

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-890]]

謂由貪愛財物等因令心散亂， ^1-890

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-891]]

於鬪諍事不正行為因令心散亂， ^1-891

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-892]]

於難行戒禁苦惱為因令心散亂，不如正理推求境界為因令心散亂。 ^1-892

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-893]]

由彼依止各別見故， ^1-893

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-894]]

於所知境不如正理種種推度妄生執著，謂唯此真餘並愚妄，由此為因令心散動。 ^1-894

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-895]]

於何散動？ ^1-895

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-896]]

謂於定心如實智見。 ^1-896

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-897]]

蓋有五種， ^1-897

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-898]]

謂貪欲蓋、 ^1-898

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-899]]

瞋恚蓋、 ^1-899

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-900]]

惛沈睡眠蓋、 ^1-900

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-901]]

掉舉惡作蓋、 ^1-901

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-902]]

疑蓋。 ^1-902

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-903]]

能令善品不得顯了是蓋義， ^1-903

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-904]]

覆蔽其心障諸善品令不轉故。 ^1-904

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-905]]

問：於何等位障諸善法？ ^1-905

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-906]]

答：於樂出家位、 ^1-906

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-907]]

覺正行位、 ^1-907

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-908]]

止舉捨位。 ^1-908

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-910]]

於樂出家時貪欲蓋為障， ^1-910

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-911]]

希求受用外境界門， ^1-911

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-912]]

於彼不欣樂故。 ^1-912

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-913]]

於覺正行時瞋恚蓋為障， ^1-913

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-914]]

於所犯學處同梵行者正發覺時， ^1-914

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-915]]

由心瞋恚不正學故。 ^1-915

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-916]]

於止舉兩位，惛沈睡眠掉舉惡作蓋為障， ^1-916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-917]]

如前所說能引沈沒及散亂故。 ^1-917

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-918]]

於捨位，疑蓋為障， ^1-918

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-919]]

遠離決定不能捨故。 ^1-919

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-920]]

株杌有三， ^1-920

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-921]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-921

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-922]]

由依止貪瞋癡先所串習為方便故， ^1-922

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-923]]

成貪等行， ^1-923

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-924]]

心不調順、 ^1-924

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-925]]

無所堪能、難可解脫。令諸眾生難斷此行，故名株杌。 ^1-925

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-926]]

所以者何？ ^1-926

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-927]]

對治道犁難可破壞， ^1-927

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-928]]

約此義故立為株杌。 ^1-928

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-929]]

於無量生串習貪等以成其行，堅固難拔猶株杌故。 ^1-929

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-930]]

垢有三種， ^1-930

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-931]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-931

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-932]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，毀犯如是尸羅學處。 ^1-932

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-933]]

由此有智同梵行者，或於聚落或閑靜處見已， ^1-933

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-934]]

作如是言： ^1-934

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-935]]

此長老作如是事、行如是行，為聚落刺。 ^1-935

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-936]]

點染不淨，說名為垢， ^1-936

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-937]]

以貪瞋癡能現犯戒不淨相故。 ^1-937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-938]]

燒害有三， ^1-938

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-939]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-939

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-940]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-941]]

長時數受生死燒惱，故名燒害。 ^1-941

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-942]]

由於無始生死流轉，因貪瞋癡數被生死苦燒害故。 ^1-942

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-943]]

箭有三種，謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-943

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-944]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，於有有具深起追求相續不絕， ^1-944

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-945]]

於佛法僧苦集滅道常生疑惑，故名為箭。 ^1-945

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-946]]

於諸有財三寶四諦，隨愛疑門能射傷故。 ^1-946

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-947]]

所有有三， ^1-947

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-948]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-948

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-949]]

由依止貪瞋癡故，積畜財物有怖 ^1-949

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-950]]

有怨， ^1-950

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-951]]

多住散亂，故名所有。 ^1-951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-952]]

由多積集所有資具，恒與怖等共相應故。 ^1-952

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-953]]

惡行有三， ^1-953

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-954]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-954

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-955]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-955

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-956]]

恒行身語意惡行，故名惡行， ^1-956

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-957]]

由貪瞋癡能引殺生等諸不善行故。 ^1-957

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-958]]

又即依此貪瞋癡門，廣生無量惡不善行故，建立三不善根。 ^1-958

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-959]]

所以者何？以諸有情，愛味世間，所有為因，行諸惡行，貪求財利行惡行故。 ^1-959

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-960]]

分別世間，怨相為因，行諸惡行。由懷瞋恚不忍他過，多行惡故。 ^1-960

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-961]]

執著世間，邪法為因，行諸惡行。由懷愚癡起顛倒見，因祠祀等行諸惡故。 ^1-961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-962]]

是故此貪瞋癡亦名惡行，亦名不善根。 ^1-962

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-963]]

漏有三種， ^1-963

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-964]]

謂欲漏、 ^1-964

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-965]]

有漏、 ^1-965

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-966]]

無明漏。 ^1-966

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-967]]

令心連注流散不絕， ^1-967

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-968]]

故名為漏。此復云何？ ^1-968

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-969]]

依外門流注故立欲漏， ^1-969

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-970]]

依內門流注故立有漏， ^1-970

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-971]]

依彼二所依門流注故立無明漏。 ^1-971

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-972]]

令心連注流散不絕，是漏義。 ^1-972

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-973]]

匱有三種， ^1-973

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-974]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-974

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-975]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-976]]

於有及資生具恒起追求無有厭足，常為貧乏眾苦所惱故，故名匱。 ^1-976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-977]]

以貪瞋癡能令身心恒乏短故。 ^1-977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-978]]

熱有三種， ^1-978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-979]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-980]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-981]]

不如正理執著諸相、執著隨好， ^1-981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-982]]

由執著相及隨好故燒惱身心，故名為熱。 ^1-982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-983]]

不如正理妄執相好，燒身心故。 ^1-983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-984]]

惱有三種， ^1-984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-985]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-986]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-987]]

隨彼彼處愛樂耽著，彼若變壞 ^1-987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-988]]

便增愁歎，種種憂苦熱惱所觸，故名為惱。 ^1-988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-989]]

由於色等諸可樂事深愛著已， ^1-989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-990]]

彼若變壞，是諸有情便為種種愁歎等苦所惱亂故。 ^1-990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-991]]

諍有三種， ^1-991

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-992]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-993]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-994]]

執持刀杖興諸戰諍種種鬪訟，是故貪等說名為諍。 ^1-994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-995]]

熾然有三， ^1-995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-996]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-997]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-998]]

為非法貪大火所燒。 ^1-998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-999]]

非法貪者， ^1-999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1000]]

謂隨貪著不善業道。 ^1-1000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1001]]

又為不平等貪大火所燒。 ^1-1001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1002]]

不平等貪者，謂非法非理貪求境界。 ^1-1002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1003]]

又為邪法大火所燒。 邪法者，謂諸外道惡說法律。 ^1-1003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1004]]

以非法貪等能發身心熾然大火，如火熾然故名熾然。 ^1-1004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1005]]

稠林有三， ^1-1005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1006]]

謂貪、瞋、癡。 ^1-1006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1007]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-1007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1008]]

於諸生死根本行中廣興染著， ^1-1008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1009]]

令諸有情感種種身流轉五趣， ^1-1009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1010]]

令諸有情處生死本，行大樹稠林難可出離，是故貪等說名稠林。 ^1-1010

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1011]]

拘礙有三， ^1-1011

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1012]]

謂貪，瞋，癡。 ^1-1012

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1013]]

由依止貪瞋癡故， ^1-1013

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1014]]

顧戀身財無所覺了 ^1-1014

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1015]]

樂處憒閙， ^1-1015

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1016]]

得少善法便生厭足，由此不能修諸善法，故名拘礙。 ^1-1016

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1017]]

依貪瞋癡顧身財等，拘礙有情不得自在修諸善故。 ^1-1017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1018]]

又此顧戀身等，即依五種心拘礙說。 ^1-1018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1019]]

何等為五？謂顧戀身、顧戀諸欲樂相雜住、闕於隨順教誡教授、於諸善品得少為足，拘礙心故名心拘礙。諸如是等煩惱義門差別無量。 ^1-1019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1020]]

邪行者， ^1-1020

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1021]]

謂貪瞋二煩惱，迷境界及見起邪行。 ^1-1021

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1022]]

修道所斷、 ^1-1022

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1023]]

見道所斷， ^1-1023

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1024]]

隨其次第。 ^1-1024

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1025]]

貪瞋二種緣少淨相及相違相為境界故，名迷境界。 雖亦緣有情起於貪瞋，然依境界門起故，亦名迷境界。 ^1-1025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1026]]

慢，迷有情及見起邪行， ^1-1026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1027]]

以於下劣等起計己勝等，行於有情門邪解轉故。 ^1-1027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1028]]

薩迦耶見、邊執見、邪見，迷所知境起邪行， ^1-1028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1029]]

依增益損減門，如其所應。 ^1-1029

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1030]]

見取、戒禁取、迷諸見起邪行、 ^1-1030

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1031]]

由於諸見過失取為第一及戒禁清淨故。 ^1-1031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1032]]

疑，迷對治起邪行， ^1-1032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1033]]

於諸諦中成二解故。 ^1-1033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1034]]

無明，迷一切起邪行。 ^1-1034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1035]]

又十煩惱皆迷苦集起諸邪行，是彼因緣所依處故。 ^1-1035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1036]]

所以者何？ ^1-1036

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1037]]

苦集二諦皆是十種煩惱因緣，又為依處， ^1-1037

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1038]]

是故一切迷此因緣依處起諸邪行。 ^1-1038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1039]]

又十煩惱皆迷滅道起諸邪行，由此能生彼怖畏故。 ^1-1039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1040]]

所以者何？ ^1-1040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1041]]

由煩惱力樂著生死， ^1-1041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1042]]

於清淨法起懸崖想生大怖畏。 ^1-1042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1043]]

又諸外道於滅道諦，妄起種種顛倒分別。 ^1-1043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1044]]

是故十惑皆迷滅道起諸邪行。 ^1-1044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1045]]

界云何？ ^1-1045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1046]]

謂除瞋，餘一切通三界繫。 ^1-1046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1047]]

瞋唯欲界繫， ^1-1047

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1048]]

緣違損境生故。 ^1-1048

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1049]]

又貪，於欲界與樂喜捨相應。 ^1-1049

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1050]]

如於欲界， ^1-1050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1051]]

於初二靜慮亦爾。 ^1-1051

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1052]]

於第三靜慮與樂捨相應， ^1-1052

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1053]]

已上唯與捨相應。 ^1-1053

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1054]]

貪於欲界與樂相應者，謂在五識身。 ^1-1054

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1055]]

與喜相應者，在意識身。 ^1-1055

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1056]]

與捨相應者，在一切處。 ^1-1056

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1057]]

於相續末位所以不與憂苦相應者，由此欣行轉故。 ^1-1057

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1058]]

瞋與苦憂捨相應。 ^1-1058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1059]]

苦相應者，在五識身。 ^1-1059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1060]]

憂相應者，在第六識。 ^1-1060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1061]]

{與捨相應者，於一切處。 如前說，} ^1-1061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1062]]

所以不與喜樂相應者， ^1-1062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1063]]

由此慼行轉故。 ^1-1063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1064]]

瞋能逼惱，自相續故名慼行。 ^1-1064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1065]]

與捨相應者，於一切處。 如前說， ^1-1065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1066]]

慢於欲界與喜捨相應， ^1-1066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1067]]

於初二靜慮與樂喜捨相應， ^1-1067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1068]]

於第三靜慮與樂捨相應， ^1-1068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1069]]

已上唯捨相應。 ^1-1069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1070]]

慢於欲界樂不相應者， ^1-1070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1071]]

以五識無故。 ^1-1071

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1072]]

若爾，於初二靜慮，云何與樂相應？ ^1-1072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1073]]

與意地樂相應，故無過。 ^1-1073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1074]]

云何於彼有意地樂？ ^1-1074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1075]]

由說彼地有喜樂故。 ^1-1075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1076]]

如經言：云何為喜？ ^1-1076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1077]]

謂已轉依者依於轉識，心悅心踊心適心調心安適受受所攝。 ^1-1077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1078]]

依於轉識者，即依意識，於三摩呬多位餘識無故。 ^1-1078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1079]]

云何為樂？ ^1-1079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1080]]

謂已轉依者依阿賴耶識攝受所依，所依怡悅安適受受所攝。 ^1-1080

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1081]]

{依於轉識者，即依意識，於三摩呬多位餘識無故。} ^1-1081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1082]]

此經意說樂受依初二靜慮生時， ^1-1082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1083]]

與如是心心法聚相應，由欣踊行還令此聚皆得踊悅， ^1-1083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1084]]

又令所依阿賴耶識自體安樂怡適。 ^1-1084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1085]]

由此樂受作二事故，體雖是一，建立二種若喜若樂，是故說此相應。 ^1-1085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1086]]

慢與樂喜相應。 ^1-1086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1087]]

如慢，薩迦耶見、邊執見、見取、戒禁取亦爾。 ^1-1087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1088]]

邪見於欲界與憂喜捨相應， ^1-1088

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1089]]

於色無色界隨所有受皆與相應。 ^1-1089

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1090]]

云何邪見於欲界與憂喜相應？ ^1-1090

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1091]]

謂先造妙行惡行者，見此無果生欣慼故。 ^1-1091

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1092]]

所以不與苦樂相應者， ^1-1092

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1093]]

由一切見皆在意地故。 ^1-1093

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1094]]

疑於欲界與憂捨相應， ^1-1094

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1095]]

於色無色界隨所有受皆與相應。 ^1-1095

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1096]]

所以欲界非喜相應者， ^1-1096

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1097]]

不決定心若未息滅，喜不生故。 ^1-1097

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1098]]

色界中疑疑上靜慮者，由喜樂定力所引持故亦得隨轉， ^1-1098

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1099]]

是故於彼亦與喜樂相應。 ^1-1099

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1100]]

無明有二種，謂相應、不共。相應無明，一切煩惱相應故，若於是處隨所有受皆得相應。 ^1-1100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1101]]

不共無明，於欲界與憂捨相應， ^1-1101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1102]]

於上界隨所有受皆得相應。 ^1-1102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1103]]

喜樂相應不相應， ^1-1103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1104]]

理應如疑說。 ^1-1104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1105]]

何故諸煩惱皆與捨相應？ ^1-1105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1106]]

以一切煩惱墮中庸位方息沒故。 ^1-1106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1107]]

所以者何？ ^1-1107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1108]]

煩惱生起展轉相續漸漸微薄， ^1-1108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1109]]

勢力將盡墮處中位，於此位中必與捨受相應。 ^1-1109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1110]]

又貪於欲界在六識身。 ^1-1110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1111]]

如貪，瞋、無明亦爾。 ^1-1111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1112]]

貪於色界在四識身， ^1-1112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1113]]

彼無鼻舌識故。 ^1-1113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1114]]

於無色界唯在意識身。 ^1-1114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1115]]

如貪，無明亦爾。 ^1-1115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1116]]

慢、見疑於一切處唯在意識身，由彼於稱量等門轉故。 ^1-1116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1117]]

又貪瞋慢於欲界緣一分事轉。 ^1-1117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1118]]

如於欲界，於色無色界亦爾。 ^1-1118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1119]]

慢緣一分事轉者， ^1-1119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1120]]

隨於一分高舉生故。 ^1-1120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1121]]

所餘煩惱於一切處遍緣一切事轉。 ^1-1121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1122]]

眾云何？ ^1-1122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1123]]

謂二眾煩惱， ^1-1123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1124]]

見道所斷眾、 ^1-1124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1125]]

修道所斷眾。 ^1-1125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1126]]

欲界見苦所斷具十煩惱。 ^1-1126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1127]]

如見苦所斷，見集滅道所斷亦爾。 ^1-1127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1129]]

若迷此起邪行，即見此所斷。 ^1-1129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1130]]

問：若緣此為境，即迷此起邪行耶？ ^1-1130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1131]]

答：不必爾，緣無漏為境，煩惱唯於有漏事隨增故。 ^1-1131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1132]]

若是處是彼因緣及所依處，彼迷此起邪行，是見苦所斷。如見苦所斷，見集滅道所斷亦爾，隨其所應。色界見四種所斷，各有九煩惱，除瞋。 ^1-1132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1133]]

如色界，無色界亦爾。 ^1-1133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1134]]

如是見道所斷煩惱眾，總有一百一十二。 ^1-1134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1135]]

欲界修所斷有六煩惱， ^1-1135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1136]]

謂俱生薩迦耶見、 ^1-1136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1137]]

邊執見， ^1-1137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1138]]

及貪、瞋、慢、無明。 ^1-1138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1139]]

何等名為修所斷俱生薩迦耶見？ ^1-1139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1140]]

謂聖弟子雖見道已生，而依止此故我慢現行。 ^1-1140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1141]]

如經言：長老駄索迦當知，我於五取蘊不見我我所， ^1-1141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1142]]

然於五取蘊有我慢我欲我隨眠，未永斷、 ^1-1142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1143]]

未遍知、 ^1-1143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1144]]

未滅、 ^1-1144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1145]]

未吐。 ^1-1145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1146]]

猶如乳母有垢膩衣，雖以鹵土等水浣濯極令離垢， ^1-1146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1147]]

若未香熏，臭氣隨轉。 ^1-1147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1148]]

復以種種香物熏坌，臭氣方盡。 ^1-1148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1149]]

如是佛聖弟子，雖以見道永斷分別身見之垢，若未以修道熏習相續，無始串習虛妄執著習氣所引不分別事我見隨轉。 ^1-1149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1150]]

復以隨道熏習相續，彼方永滅。 ^1-1150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1151]]

俱生邊執見者，謂斷見所攝。 ^1-1151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1152]]

由此見故於涅槃界其心退轉生大怖畏，謂我我今者何所在耶？ ^1-1152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1153]]

貪等煩惱修道所斷者，謂除見品所攝。 ^1-1153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1154]]

色界修道所斷有五，除瞋。 ^1-1154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1155]]

如色界，無色界亦爾。 ^1-1155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1156]]

如是修道所斷煩惱總有十六。 ^1-1156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1157]]

斷云何？謂如此差別斷、由此作意斷、 ^1-1157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1158]]

從此而得斷。 ^1-1158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1159]]

如此差別斷者，謂遍知故、 ^1-1159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1160]]

遠離故、 ^1-1160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1161]]

得對治故。 ^1-1161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1162]]

遍知者， ^1-1162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1163]]

謂彼因緣事遍智、 ^1-1163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1164]]

自體遍智、 ^1-1164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1165]]

過患遍智。 ^1-1165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1166]]

彼因緣事遍智者，謂知煩惱隨眠未永斷故，如是等如前說。 ^1-1166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1167]]

自體遍智者，謂知此煩惱生已極惱亂心性。 ^1-1167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1168]]

過患遍智者，謂知此煩惱能引自害、 ^1-1168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1169]]

能引害他、 ^1-1169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1170]]

能引俱害， ^1-1170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1171]]

能生現法過、 ^1-1171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1172]]

能生後法過、 ^1-1172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1173]]

能生現法後法過， ^1-1173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1174]]

能令有情受此所生身心憂苦。 ^1-1174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1175]]

遠離者，雖彼暫生而不堅執。 ^1-1175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1176]]

由彼因緣事遍智等三種遍智， ^1-1176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1177]]

於彼已生一切煩惱，心不堅執方便遠離。 ^1-1177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1178]]

得對治者，謂未生者令不生故、已生者令斷故。 ^1-1178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1179]]

得對治道，為令未生已生煩惱不生永斷，修治道故。 ^1-1179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1180]]

問：何等作意能斷耶？ ^1-1180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1181]]

答：總緣作意， ^1-1181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1182]]

觀一切法皆無我性，能斷煩惱。 ^1-1182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1183]]

總緣作意者， ^1-1183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1184]]

謂合緣一切法共相行作意。 ^1-1184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1185]]

問：若唯總緣諸法無我智能斷煩惱者， ^1-1185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1186]]

何故顯示無常等行？ ^1-1186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1187]]

答：非為斷煩惱故修習彼行，但為修治無我行故。 ^1-1187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1189]]

由依無常行引得苦行， ^1-1189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1190]]

依止苦行引無我行。 ^1-1190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1191]]

如經言：無常故苦， ^1-1191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1192]]

苦故無我。 ^1-1192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1193]]

是故建立此無我行為無上。 ^1-1193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1194]]

無上有三種， ^1-1194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1195]]

謂智無上、 ^1-1195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1196]]

行無上、 ^1-1196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1197]]

解脫無上。 ^1-1197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1198]]

智無上者，謂無我智， ^1-1198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1199]]

得此智已更無所求故。 ^1-1199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1200]]

行無上者，謂樂速通行， ^1-1200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1201]]

一切行中最第一故。 ^1-1201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1202]]

解脫無上者，謂無學不動解脫， ^1-1202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1203]]

於一切解脫最為勝故。 ^1-1203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1204]]

此三無上，如其次第依止見修無學道說。 ^1-1204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1205]]

問：從何而得斷耶？ ^1-1205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1206]]

答：不從過去已滅故、 ^1-1206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1207]]

不從未來未生故、 ^1-1207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1208]]

不從現在道不俱故， ^1-1208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1209]]

然從諸煩惱麁重而得斷。 ^1-1209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1210]]

為斷如是如是品麁重，生如是如是品對治。 ^1-1210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1211]]

若此品對治生，即此品麁重滅。平等平等，猶如世間明生暗滅。 ^1-1211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1212]]

由此品離繫故，令未來煩惱住不生法中，是名為斷。 ^1-1212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1213]]

復次煩惱增上所生業云何？謂若思業、若思已業，總名業相。 ^1-1213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1214]]

又業有五種， ^1-1214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1215]]

謂取受業、作用業、 ^1-1215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1216]]

加行業、 ^1-1216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1217]]

轉變業、 ^1-1217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1218]]

證得業。 ^1-1218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1219]]

取受業者，謂眼等能見色等。 ^1-1219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1220]]

作用業者，謂地等能任持等。 ^1-1220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1221]]

或復諸法自相所作， ^1-1221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1222]]

謂所有色質礙變壞如是等。 ^1-1222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1223]]

加行業者，謂意解為先起身業等。 ^1-1223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1224]]

轉變業者，謂金師等造莊嚴具等。 ^1-1224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1225]]

證得業者，謂聖道等證涅槃等。 ^1-1225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1226]]

今此義中意多分別加行業，頗亦兼有證得、作用二業。 ^1-1226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1227]]

思業云何？ ^1-1227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1228]]

謂福業、 ^1-1228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1229]]

非福業、 ^1-1229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1230]]

不動業。 ^1-1230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1231]]

思已業云何？ ^1-1231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1232]]

謂身業、 ^1-1232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1233]]

語業、 ^1-1233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1234]]

意業。 ^1-1234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1235]]

又此身語意三業或善、或不善。 ^1-1235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1236]]

不善者即十不善業道， ^1-1236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1237]]

謂殺生、 ^1-1237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1238]]

不與取、 ^1-1238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1239]]

欲邪行、 ^1-1239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1240]]

妄語、 ^1-1240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1241]]

離間語、 ^1-1241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1242]]

麁惡語、 ^1-1242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1243]]

綺語、 ^1-1243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1244]]

貪欲、 ^1-1244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1245]]

瞋恚、 ^1-1245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1246]]

邪見。 ^1-1246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1247]]

善者即十善業道，謂 ^1-1247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1248]]

離殺生乃至離雜穢語、 ^1-1248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1249]]

無貪、 ^1-1249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1250]]

無瞋、 ^1-1250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1251]]

正見。 ^1-1251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1252]]

今於此中唯以業道顯身等業者， ^1-1252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1253]]

為隨順經就勝而說， ^1-1253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1254]]

彼方便等亦身等業所攝故。 ^1-1254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1255]]

前三中四後三業道，隨其次第是身語意業相。 ^1-1255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1256]]

又殺生等應以五門分別其相， ^1-1256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1257]]

謂事故、 ^1-1257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1258]]

意樂故、 ^1-1258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1259]]

方便故、 ^1-1259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1260]]

煩惱故、 ^1-1260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1261]]

究竟故殺生等。 ^1-1261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1262]]

事者，謂有情數、 ^1-1262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1263]]

非有情數， ^1-1263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1264]]

如其所應依此處所起殺生等。 ^1-1264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1265]]

意樂者，謂於此事起此想意樂，及起當作此業道意樂。 ^1-1265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1266]]

方便者，謂此作用，或自或他發身語意。 ^1-1266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1267]]

煩惱者，謂貪瞋癡，如其所應或總或別。 ^1-1267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1268]]

究竟者，由彼彼方便，如是如是業道圓滿，或於爾時或於後時。 ^1-1268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1269]]

於此義中殺生事者，謂有情數。 ^1-1269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1270]]

意樂者，謂此想及必害意。 ^1-1270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1271]]

方便者，謂為害故，如刀杖等。 ^1-1271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1272]]

煩惱者，謂貪等。 ^1-1272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1273]]

究竟者，謂彼眾生由方便故，或無間死或後時死。 ^1-1273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1274]]

不與取等，事及究竟，當廣分別，餘如理應思。 ^1-1274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1275]]

不與取事者，謂他所攝，若有情數、 ^1-1275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1276]]

非有情數。 ^1-1276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1277]]

究竟者，謂取為己有。 ^1-1277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1278]]

欲邪行事者，謂非所行女； ^1-1278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1279]]

或雖所行，非分、 ^1-1279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1280]]

非處、 ^1-1280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1281]]

非時、 ^1-1281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1282]]

非量。 ^1-1282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1283]]

及不應理一切男及不男。 ^1-1283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1284]]

究竟者，謂兩兩交會。 ^1-1284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1285]]

虛誑語事者， ^1-1285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1286]]

謂見聞覺知、 ^1-1286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1287]]

不見不聞不覺不知。 ^1-1287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1288]]

意樂者，謂別異想，欲別異說。 ^1-1288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1289]]

究竟者，謂時眾，及對言者領解。 ^1-1289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1290]]

離間語事者，謂諸有情若和合、若不和合。 ^1-1290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1291]]

意樂者，謂即於彼起乖離及不和合意。 ^1-1291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1292]]

究竟者，謂所破領解。 ^1-1292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1293]]

麁惡語事者，謂諸有情能為損害。 ^1-1293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1294]]

究竟者，謂發麁惡言。 ^1-1294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1295]]

綺語事者，謂能引攝不饒益義。 ^1-1295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1296]]

究竟者，謂正發此言。 ^1-1296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1297]]

貪欲事者，謂他所攝資財。 ^1-1297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1298]]

意樂者，謂起此想及愛樂。 ^1-1298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1299]]

方便者，謂思量籌度欲為己有。 ^1-1299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1300]]

究竟者，決定執為己有。 ^1-1300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1301]]

瞋恚事者，謂諸有情能為損害。 ^1-1301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1302]]

究竟者，謂決定加害等。 ^1-1302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1303]]

邪見事者，謂實有義。 ^1-1303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1304]]

意樂者，謂於實有起非有想及彼欲樂。 ^1-1304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1305]]

究竟者，謂決定誹謗。 ^1-1305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1306]]

如經言：故思造業。云何名為故思造業？ ^1-1306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1307]]

略有五種， ^1-1307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1308]]

謂他所教勅故思造業、 ^1-1308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1309]]

他所勸請故思造業、 ^1-1309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1310]]

無所了知故思造業、 ^1-1310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1311]]

根本執著故思造業、 ^1-1311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1312]]

顛倒分別故思造業。 ^1-1312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1313]]

他所教勅故思造業者， ^1-1313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1314]]

猶如有一雖不欲樂，因他強力之所教勅，發起故思行不善業。 ^1-1314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1315]]

他所勸請故思造業者， ^1-1315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1316]]

猶如有一雖不欲樂，因他勸請、因他引導，執為利益，發起故思行不善業。 ^1-1316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1317]]

無所了知故思造業者， ^1-1317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1318]]

猶如有一不了德失，無所執著隨欲所作，發起故思行不善業。 ^1-1318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1319]]

根本執著故思造業者， ^1-1319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1320]]

猶如有一為貪瞋等諸不善根纏蔽其心，猛利執著，發起故思行不善業。 ^1-1320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1321]]

顛倒分別故思造業者， ^1-1321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1322]]

猶如有一依不平等因見愛樂邪法，為求當來可愛異熟，發起故思行不善業。 ^1-1322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1323]]

於此五中，根本執著故思造業、顛倒分別故思造業，此二種若作若增長非不受異熟。 ^1-1323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1324]]

所以者何？ ^1-1324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1325]]

前三故思作業，雖作不增長， ^1-1325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1326]]

輕故不必受異熟。 ^1-1326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1327]]

後二故思作業，若作若增長， ^1-1327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1328]]

重故必定受異熟。 ^1-1328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1329]]

作者，謂起造諸業令其現行。 ^1-1329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1330]]

增長者，謂令習氣增益， ^1-1330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1331]]

於阿賴耶識中長養異熟種子故。 ^1-1331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1332]]

如經言決定受業者， ^1-1332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1333]]

云何決定？謂作業決定、 ^1-1333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1334]]

受異熟決定、 ^1-1334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1335]]

分位決定。 ^1-1335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1336]]

作業決定者，由宿業力感得決定異熟相續， ^1-1336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1337]]

於此生中必造此業。何以故應造此業？ ^1-1337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1338]]

期限決定故，終不越限必造此業。 ^1-1338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1339]]

乃至諸佛世尊大神通力，亦不能為障令其不造，隨因決定力果相續轉變故。 ^1-1339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1340]]

受異熟決定者，如先所說故思造業。 ^1-1340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1341]]

分位決定者，謂現法受等分位決定業。 ^1-1341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1342]]

如由此業於現法中必定受異熟， ^1-1342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1343]]

由此業必受生異熟、 ^1-1343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1344]]

由此業必受後異熟。 ^1-1344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1345]]

又十不善業道異熟果者， ^1-1345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1346]]

於三惡趣中隨下中上品， ^1-1346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1347]]

受傍生、餓鬼、那落迦異熟。 ^1-1347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1348]]

等流果者，各隨其相於人趣中感得自身眾具衰損。 ^1-1348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1349]]

謂從惡趣沒後生人中，由殺盜等，各隨其相感得自身眾具衰損， ^1-1349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1350]]

所謂壽命短促、 ^1-1350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1351]]

常貧窮等，如其所應。 ^1-1351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1352]]

增上果者，各隨其相感得所有外事衰損。 ^1-1352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1353]]

謂殺生等各隨其相，感稼穡等外事衰損。 ^1-1353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1354]]

所謂外具乏少光澤，是殺生增上果，如是等。 ^1-1354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1355]]

如經言：於一切十不善業道修習多修習故，生於那落迦、傍生、餓鬼，是彼異熟果。 ^1-1355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1356]]

若得來此人同分中， ^1-1356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1357]]

由殺生故今得短壽， ^1-1357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1358]]

不與取故乏少財物， ^1-1358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1359]]

欲邪行故妻不貞良， ^1-1359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1360]]

由虛誑語故多被誹謗， ^1-1360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1361]]

離間語故親友乖離， ^1-1361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1362]]

麁惡語故恒聞不如意聲， ^1-1362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1363]]

由綺語故言不威肅， ^1-1363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1364]]

由貪欲故貪轉猛盛， ^1-1364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1365]]

由瞋恚故瞋轉猛盛， ^1-1365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1366]]

由邪見故癡轉猛盛， ^1-1366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1367]]

諸邪見者癡增上故， ^1-1367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1368]]

是彼等流果。 ^1-1368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1369]]

由極修習殺生業故，一切外事乏少光澤， ^1-1369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1370]]

不與取故多遭霜雹， ^1-1370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1371]]

欲邪行故多諸塵坌， ^1-1371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1372]]

妄語故多諸臭穢， ^1-1372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1373]]

離間語故高下險阻， ^1-1373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1374]]

麁惡語故其地醎鹵磽确穢惡， ^1-1374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1375]]

綺語故時候乖變， ^1-1375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1376]]

貪欲故果實尠少， ^1-1376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1377]]

瞋恚故果味辛苦， ^1-1377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1378]]

邪見故果味辛苦或全無果， ^1-1378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1379]]

是彼增上果。 ^1-1379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1380]]

又十善業道異熟果者，謂於人天趣中受人天異熟。 ^1-1380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1381]]

等流果者，謂即於彼處各隨其相感得自身眾具興盛。 ^1-1381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1382]]

增上果者，謂即於彼處各隨其相感得外事興盛。 ^1-1382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1383]]

如不善業道建立異熟果等三種差別， ^1-1383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1384]]

如是有漏善業道於人天中三果差別，如其所應亦當建立。 ^1-1384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1385]]

又善不善業於善趣惡趣中感生異熟時，有二種差別，謂招引業、圓滿業。 ^1-1385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1386]]

招引業者，謂由此業能牽異熟果。 ^1-1386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1387]]

圓滿業者，謂由此業生已領受愛不愛果。 ^1-1387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1388]]

復次或有業由一業力牽得一身， ^1-1388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1389]]

謂由一業力長養一生異熟種子故。 ^1-1389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1390]]

或有業由一業力牽得多身， ^1-1390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1391]]

謂由一業力長養多生異熟種子故。 ^1-1391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1392]]

或有業由多業力牽得一身， ^1-1392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1393]]

謂由多業剎那數數長養一生異熟種子故。 ^1-1393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1394]]

或有業由多業力牽得多身， ^1-1394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1395]]

謂多剎那業更相資待展轉長養多生異熟種子故。 ^1-1395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1396]]

問：若一有情成就多業，云何次第受異熟果耶？ ^1-1396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1397]]

答：於彼身中重者先熟， ^1-1397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1398]]

或將死時現在前者、 ^1-1398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1399]]

或先所數習者、 ^1-1399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1400]]

或最初所行者，彼異熟先熟。 ^1-1400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1401]]

如經言：有三種業，謂福業、非福業、不動業。 福業者， ^1-1401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1402]]

謂欲界繫善業。 ^1-1402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1403]]

非福業者，謂不善業。 ^1-1403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1404]]

不動業者，謂色無色界繫善業。 ^1-1404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1405]]

問：何故色無色繫善業名不動耶？ ^1-1405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1406]]

答：如欲界中餘趣圓滿善不善業，遇緣轉得餘趣異熟。 ^1-1406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1407]]

非色無色繫業有如是事， ^1-1407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1408]]

所受異熟界地決定故。 ^1-1408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1409]]

是故約與異熟不可移轉，名為不動。 ^1-1409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1410]]

又定地攝故說為不動。 ^1-1410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1411]]

問：如經中說無明緣行。若福非福及與不動：云何福及不動行緣無明生耶？ ^1-1411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1412]]

答：有二種愚，一異熟愚、二真實義愚。 ^1-1412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1413]]

由異熟愚故發非福行， ^1-1413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1414]]

由真實義愚故發福及不動行。 ^1-1414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1415]]

由異熟愚發非福行者， ^1-1415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1416]]

由彼一向是染污性，無明合時必不容受信解，異熟行相正見故。 ^1-1416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1417]]

由真實義愚發福不動行者， ^1-1417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1418]]

真實義即四聖諦， ^1-1418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1419]]

於彼愚癡名真實義愚。未見諦者雖起善心，由彼隨眠所隨縛故亦名愚癡。 ^1-1419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1420]]

由彼勢力，於三界苦不如實知，便能發起後有因性福、不動行。 ^1-1420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1421]]

非已見諦者能發此業， ^1-1421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1422]]

無真實義愚故。 ^1-1422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1423]]

是故彼業說因此生。 ^1-1423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1424]]

復次殺生業道，貪瞋癡為方便， ^1-1424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1425]]

由瞋究竟。 ^1-1425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1426]]

如殺生，麁惡語、瞋恚業道亦爾。 ^1-1426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1427]]

殺生貪為方便者，為皮肉等故。 ^1-1427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1428]]

瞋為方便者，為除怨等故。 ^1-1428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1429]]

癡為方便者，為祠祀等故。 ^1-1429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1430]]

由瞋究竟者，離無慈悲必不殺害他有情故。 ^1-1430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1431]]

麁惡語等如理應知。 ^1-1431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1432]]

不與取業道， ^1-1432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1433]]

貪瞋癡為方便， ^1-1433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1434]]

由貪究竟。 ^1-1434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1435]]

如不與取，欲邪行、貪欲亦爾。 ^1-1435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1436]]

問：貪欲等云何用貪等為方便？ ^1-1436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1437]]

答：前說貪欲業道於他資財決定執為己有為性，若即於此資財先起餘貪，加行追求欲為己有， ^1-1437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1438]]

即立此為貪方便。 ^1-1438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1439]]

若先起瞋，餘勿有此，是瞋方便。 ^1-1439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1440]]

若先起癡，謂於他物取為己有無有過失，是癡方便。 ^1-1440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1441]]

如是所餘，如理應知。 ^1-1441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1442]]

虛誑語業道，貪瞋癡為方便， ^1-1442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1443]]

於三種中隨由一究竟。 ^1-1443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1444]]

如妄語，離間語、綺語亦爾。 ^1-1444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1446]]

邪見業道，貪瞋癡為方便， ^1-1446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1447]]

由癡究竟。 ^1-1447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1448]]

復次如經言：有共業、 ^1-1448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1449]]

有不共業， ^1-1449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1450]]

有強力業、 ^1-1450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1451]]

有劣力業。云何共業？ ^1-1451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1452]]

若業能令諸器世間種種差別。 ^1-1452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1453]]

云何不共業，若業能令有情世間種種差別。 ^1-1453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1454]]

或復有業令諸有情展轉增上，由此業力說諸有情更互相望為增上緣， ^1-1454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1455]]

以彼互有增上力故，亦名共業。 ^1-1455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1456]]

由此勢力，諸有情類展轉互為諸心心法變異生因。 ^1-1456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1457]]

是故經言：如是有情與餘有情互相見等，而不受用、不易可得。 ^1-1457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1458]]

云何強力業？ ^1-1458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1459]]

謂對治力強補特伽羅，故思所造諸不善業，由對治力所攝伏故， ^1-1459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1460]]

令當受那落迦業轉成現法受， ^1-1460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1461]]

應現法受業轉令不受。 ^1-1461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1462]]

所以此業名強力者，由能對治業力強故。 ^1-1462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1463]]

此能治業望所治業其力強勝，令彼所感諸苦異熟轉變滅故。 ^1-1463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1464]]

又故思所造一切善業望不善業，對治力強皆名強力。 ^1-1464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1465]]

依此業故，薄伽梵說：我聖弟子能以無量廣大之業善熏其心，諸所造作有量之業，此業不能牽引、 ^1-1465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1466]]

不能留住， ^1-1466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1467]]

亦不能令墮在彼數。 ^1-1467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1468]]

又對治力劣補特伽羅，故思所造諸不善業，望諸善業皆名強力。又故思造業異熟決定，不斷不知，名強力業。 ^1-1468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1469]]

此中意說，一切善不善業無有差別，但異熟決定，諸聖道力所不斷者，皆名強力業。 ^1-1469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1470]]

又欲界繫諸不善業性皆是強，眾多煩惱隨煩惱等為助伴故。 ^1-1470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1471]]

又先所串習名強力業，以數熏習於相續故。 ^1-1471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1472]]

又依強位名強力業，由處盛年所造諸業猛利執著淨信所發故。 ^1-1472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1473]]

又不可治者所造諸業名強力業，由無涅槃法者所有諸業，非對治力所能伏故。 ^1-1473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1474]]

又由田故發強力業，謂害母等業。 ^1-1474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1475]]

又由心加行故發強力業，謂於無上菩提發強大願等。 ^1-1475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1476]]

此所生業其力猛盛，名強力業。 ^1-1476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1477]]

復次由九種因發強力業， ^1-1477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1478]]

謂田故、事故、 ^1-1478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1479]]

自體故、 ^1-1479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1480]]

所依故、 ^1-1480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1481]]

作意故、 ^1-1481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1482]]

意樂故、 ^1-1482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1483]]

助伴故、 ^1-1483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1484]]

多修習故、 ^1-1484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1485]]

與多眾生共所行故，由此九因發強力業。 ^1-1485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1486]]

{與此相違，是劣力業。} ^1-1486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1488]]

田者，謂具大功德，堪為福田。 ^1-1488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1489]]

事者，謂所施物多而精妙。 ^1-1489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1490]]

自體者，謂戒勝於施、修勝於戒，如是等。 ^1-1490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1491]]

所依者，謂已離欲者作諸福業。 ^1-1491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1492]]

作意者，謂猛利淨信俱行作意。 ^1-1492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1493]]

意樂者，謂希求涅槃所有意樂。 ^1-1493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1494]]

助伴者，謂更廣修習餘福業事共相攝受。 ^1-1494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1495]]

多修習者，謂數數修習或數數尋思。 ^1-1495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1496]]

與多眾生共所行者，謂自作教他見作隨喜。 ^1-1496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1497]]

與此相違，是劣力業。 ^1-1497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1498]]

如世尊說：若有說言彼彼丈夫補特伽羅，隨如是如是業，若作若增長，還受如是如是異熟。若有是事，便不應修清淨梵行，亦不可知正盡諸苦作苦邊際。 若有說言彼彼丈夫補特伽羅，隨如是如是順所受業，若作若增長，還受如是如是順所受異熟。 若有是事，便應修習清淨梵行，又亦可知正盡諸苦作苦邊際。如是經言有何密意？ ^1-1498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1499]]

此中佛意為欲遮止如是邪說，謂樂俱行業還能感得樂俱行異熟、 ^1-1499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1500]]

苦俱行業還能感得苦俱行異熟、 ^1-1500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1501]]

不苦不樂俱行業還能感得不苦不樂俱行異熟，故作是說。 ^1-1501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1502]]

若有說言彼彼丈夫補特伽羅，隨如是如是業若作若增長，還受如是如是異熟。 ^1-1502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1503]]

若有是事，便不應修清淨梵行，亦不可知正盡諸苦作苦邊際。何故便不應修清淨梵行？ ^1-1503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1504]]

以諸煩惱極猛利者，要由智慧自勵自策，與憂苦俱護持禁戒。 ^1-1504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1505]]

若於當來受彼異熟時，還與憂苦俱者護持禁戒，應無義利。 ^1-1505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1506]]

又於他妻等與喜樂俱毀犯禁戒，若於當來受彼異熟時，還與喜樂俱者精勤遠離，即為唐捐。 ^1-1506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1507]]

是故說言：若有是事，便不應修清淨梵行。 ^1-1507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1508]]

何故亦不可知正盡諸苦作苦邊際？ ^1-1508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1509]]

即由如是修習梵行招苦異熟故。 ^1-1509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1510]]

是故世尊為遮如是外道邪說，樂俱行業還受樂異熟、苦俱行業還受苦異熟、不苦不樂俱行業還受不苦不樂異熟，異熟因異熟果決定相似，故說是經。 ^1-1510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1511]]

又為開許如是正說，謂樂俱行業順樂受者還受樂異熟順、 ^1-1511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1512]]

苦受者還受苦異熟、 ^1-1512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1513]]

順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 ^1-1513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1514]]

{樂俱行業有二種，謂善、不善。隨其所應順當來世苦受、樂受、不苦不樂受，還能感得樂等異熟。} ^1-1514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1515]]

苦俱行業順樂受者還受樂異熟、順苦受者還受苦異熟、順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 ^1-1515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1516]]

不苦不樂受俱行業順樂受者還受樂異熟、順苦受者還受苦異熟、順不苦不樂受者還受不苦不樂異熟。 ^1-1516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1517]]

樂俱行業有二種，謂善、不善。隨其所應順當來世苦受、樂受、不苦不樂受，還能感得樂等異熟。 ^1-1517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1518]]

如是苦俱行業、不苦不樂俱行業，各有二種隨順樂等感樂等異熟，隨其所應。 如是名為此經密意。 ^1-1518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1519]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第七大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第八安慧菩薩糅大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中諦品第一之三 ^1-1519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1520]]

復次業差別有三種，謂律儀業、 ^1-1520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1521]]

不律儀業、 ^1-1521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1522]]

非律儀非不律儀業。 ^1-1522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1523]]

律儀業復有三種，謂別解脫律儀所攝、 ^1-1523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1524]]

靜慮律儀所攝、 ^1-1524
