![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1525]]

無漏律儀所攝。 ^1-1525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1526]]

別解脫律儀所攝業者，即是七眾所受律儀，謂 ^1-1526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1527]]

比丘律儀、 ^1-1527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1528]]

比丘尼律儀、 ^1-1528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1529]]

式叉摩那律儀、 ^1-1529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1530]]

沙彌律儀、 ^1-1530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1531]]

沙彌尼律儀、 ^1-1531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1532]]

鄔波索迦律儀、 ^1-1532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1533]]

鄔波斯迦律儀及 ^1-1533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1534]]

近住律儀。 ^1-1534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1535]]

依止何等補特伽羅建立出家律儀？ ^1-1535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1536]]

依能修行遠離惡行、遠離欲行。 ^1-1536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1537]]

由比丘等出家五眾， ^1-1537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1538]]

乃能盡壽遠離殺生等惡行，及能遠離非梵行故。 ^1-1538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1539]]

依止何等補特伽羅建立鄔波索迦律儀、鄔波斯迦律儀？ ^1-1539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1540]]

依能盡壽遠離惡行、不遠離欲行。 ^1-1540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1541]]

由彼二眾建立盡壽離欲邪行，非離非梵行故。 ^1-1541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1542]]

依止何等補特伽羅建立近住律儀？ ^1-1542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1543]]

依止不能遠離惡行 ^1-1543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1544]]

及不遠離欲行。 ^1-1544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1545]]

是故為彼但制日夜近住律儀， ^1-1545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1546]]

為令漸漸俱修學故。 ^1-1546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1547]]

問：若唯修學鄔波索迦一分學處，為說成就鄔波索迦律儀？ ^1-1547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1548]]

為說不成就耶？ ^1-1548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1549]]

答：應說成就而名犯戒。 ^1-1549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1550]]

問：扇𢮎、半擇迦等，為遮彼受鄔波索迦律儀不耶？ ^1-1550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1551]]

答：不遮彼受鄔波索迦律儀， ^1-1551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1552]]

然遮彼鄔波索迦性， ^1-1552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1553]]

不堪親近承事比丘比丘尼等二出家眾故。 ^1-1553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1554]]

如扇𢮎、半擇迦不堪親近承事比丘比丘尼等二出家眾故， ^1-1554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1555]]

遮彼鄔波索迦性。二形亦爾，男女煩惱恒俱現行，不堪親近承事二眾，故不別說。 ^1-1555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1556]]

又半擇迦有五種，謂 ^1-1556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1557]]

生便半擇迦、 ^1-1557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1558]]

嫉妬半擇迦、 ^1-1558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1559]]

半月半擇迦、 ^1-1559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1560]]

灌灑半擇迦、 ^1-1560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1561]]

除去半擇迦。 ^1-1561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1562]]

靜慮律儀所攝業者，謂 ^1-1562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1563]]

能損伏發起犯戒煩惱種子。離欲界欲者所有遠離、 ^1-1563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1564]]

離初二三靜慮欲者所有遠離， ^1-1564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1565]]

是名靜慮律儀所攝身語業性。 ^1-1565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1566]]

發起犯戒煩惱者，謂貪瞋等欲界所繫煩惱隨煩惱。能損伏彼種子者，謂由伏對治力損彼種子。 ^1-1566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1567]]

離欲界欲者，謂由伏對治力，或少分離欲、或全分離欲。所有遠離者，謂從彼犯戒所得遠離性。 ^1-1567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1568]]

離初二三靜慮欲者，謂由遠分對治力，令彼發起犯戒煩惱所有種子轉更衰損。 ^1-1568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1569]]

所以不說離第四靜慮欲者，由無色界麁色無故，略不建立色戒律儀。 ^1-1569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1570]]

無漏律儀所攝業者，謂已見諦者由無漏作意力所得無漏遠離戒性。 ^1-1570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1571]]

不律儀業者，謂諸不律儀者，或由生彼種姓中故、或由受持彼事業故，所期現行彼業決定。 ^1-1571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1572]]

何等名為不律儀者？ ^1-1572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1573]]

所謂屠羊、 ^1-1573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1574]]

養鷄 ^1-1574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1575]]

養猪、 ^1-1575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1576]]

捕鳥捕魚、獵鹿罝兔、 ^1-1576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1577]]

劫盜魁膾、害牛縛象、立壇呪龍、 ^1-1577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1578]]

守獄讒搆好為損等。 ^1-1578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1579]]

{不律儀業者，謂諸不律儀者，或由生彼種姓中故、或由受持彼事業故，所期現行彼業決定。} ^1-1579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1580]]

屠羊者，為欲活命屠養買賣。 ^1-1580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1581]]

如是養鷄猪等，隨其所應。 ^1-1581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1582]]

縛象者，恒處山林調執野象。 ^1-1582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1583]]

立壇呪龍者，習呪龍蛇戲樂自活。 ^1-1583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1584]]

讒搆者，以離間語毀壞他親持用活命。 ^1-1584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1585]]

或由生彼種姓中或由受持彼事業者，謂即生彼家若生餘家，如其次第。 ^1-1585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1586]]

所期現行彼業決定者，謂身語方便為先決定，要期現行彼業，是名不律儀業。 ^1-1586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1587]]

非律儀非不律儀業者，謂 ^1-1587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1588]]

住非律儀非不律儀者所有善不善業， ^1-1588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1589]]

若布施愛語等業、若毆擊等業，律儀不律儀所不攝故，名非律儀非不律儀。 ^1-1589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1590]]

又業差別有三種，謂順樂受業、順苦受業、順不苦不樂受業。 ^1-1590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1591]]

順樂受業者，謂從欲界乃至第三靜慮所有善業。 ^1-1591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1592]]

順苦受業者，謂不善業。 ^1-1592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1593]]

順不苦不樂受業者，謂第四靜慮已上所有善業等。 ^1-1593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1594]]

復次業差別有三種，謂順現法受業、順生受業、順後受業。 ^1-1594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1595]]

順現法受業者，若業於現法中異熟成熟。 ^1-1595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1596]]

謂從慈定起已，於彼造作若損若益，必得現異熟。 ^1-1596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1597]]

如從慈定起， ^1-1597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1598]]

從無諍定、 ^1-1598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1599]]

滅盡定、 ^1-1599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1600]]

預流果、阿羅漢果起亦爾。 ^1-1600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1601]]

又於佛為上首僧中造善惡業，必得現異熟。 ^1-1601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1602]]

又有餘猛利意樂方便所行善不善業，亦得現異熟。 ^1-1602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1603]]

所以名為現法受業，若業於此生作即於此生熟故。 ^1-1603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1604]]

順生受業者，若業於無間生中異熟成熟。 ^1-1604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1605]]

{復有所餘善不善業，於無間生異熟熟者，一切皆名順生受業。} ^1-1605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1606]]

無間生者，即次此生。 謂五無間業等能得生異熟。 ^1-1606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1607]]

問：若造一無間者，於無間生中可受其異熟； ^1-1607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1608]]

若造多無間業者，於無間生中云何得受其異熟？ ^1-1608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1609]]

答：於一生中頓受一切所得異熟，無有過失。所以者何？ ^1-1609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1610]]

若造眾多無間業者，所感身形最極柔軟，所感苦具眾多猛利，由此頓受種種大苦。 ^1-1610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1611]]

復有所餘善不善業，於無間生異熟熟者，一切皆名順生受業。 ^1-1611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1612]]

順後受業者，若業於無間生後異熟成熟。 ^1-1612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1613]]

於此業中從初熟位建立順現法受等名，不唯受此一位異熟。 ^1-1613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1614]]

若業於此生造，即從此生已去異熟成熟，說名順現法受業。 ^1-1614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1615]]

若業於此生造，從無間生已去異熟成熟，說名順生受業。 ^1-1615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1616]]

若業於此生造，度無間生已去異熟成熟，說名順後受業。 若作是說，即為善順《訶怨心經》。 如彼經言：由無間業，於那落迦中數數死生，受大苦異熟。 ^1-1616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1617]]

{順後受業者，若業於無間生後異熟成熟。} ^1-1617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1618]]

復次有四種諸業差別，謂黑黑異熟業、白白異熟業、黑白黑白異熟業、非黑白無異熟業，能盡諸業。 ^1-1618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1619]]

黑黑異熟業者，謂不善業，由染污故、不可愛異熟故。 ^1-1619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1620]]

白白異熟業者，謂三界善業，不染污故、可愛異熟故。 ^1-1620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1621]]

黑白黑白異熟業者，謂欲界繫雜業，善不善雜故。 ^1-1621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1622]]

云何一業亦善不善？ ^1-1622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1623]]

此中不約生剎那相說一種業亦善不善，然約意樂及方便總說一業，是此經意。 ^1-1623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1624]]

約此二種，若黑若白互不相似，建立一種黑白業故。 ^1-1624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1625]]

或有業意樂故黑、方便故白。或有業方便故黑、意樂故白。 ^1-1625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1626]]

意樂故黑、方便故白者，猶如有一為欲誑他，先現其相，令信己故行於惠施乃至出家。 ^1-1626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1627]]

方便故黑、意樂故白者，猶如有一欲令子及門徒遠危處安，由憐愍心現發種種身語麁惡，遂於此時發生雜染非黑白無異熟業。 ^1-1627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1628]]

能盡諸業者，謂於方便無間道中諸無漏業， ^1-1628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1629]]

以方便無間道是彼諸業斷對治故。 ^1-1629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1630]]

非黑者，離煩惱垢故。 ^1-1630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1631]]

白者，一向清淨故。 ^1-1631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1632]]

無異熟者，生死相違故。 ^1-1632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1633]]

能盡諸業者，由無漏業力永拔黑等三有漏業與異熟習氣故。 ^1-1633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1634]]

復次總約一切無漏業無有差別所有障礙隨順體性，如其次第建立曲、穢、濁等諸染污業，淨牟尼等諸清淨業。 ^1-1634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1635]]

曲業者，若身語意業能障正直八聖道支令不生長。 ^1-1635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1636]]

穢業者，若身等業能污相續，依此發生如是障業。 ^1-1636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1637]]

濁業者，若身等業依止外道顛倒見生，一切如來清淨聖教之所對治不信，混濁之所攝故。 ^1-1637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1638]]

復有差別，墮在斷常邊，違處中行義，名曲。 ^1-1638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1639]]

損減見所攝，增惡清淨所立法義，名穢。 ^1-1639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1640]]

薩迦耶見所攝，障真無我見義，名濁。 ^1-1640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1641]]

淨業者，與如是等諸雜染業相違類解。三淨業者，謂善淨尸羅。 正直見所攝，身語意業遠離毀犯戒見垢故。 ^1-1641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1642]]

三牟尼業者，謂學無學所有無漏身語意業，唯諸牟尼有此業故。 ^1-1642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1643]]

又有施等諸清淨業。 ^1-1643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1644]]

施業云何？ ^1-1644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1645]]

謂因緣故、等起故、處所故、自體故，分別施業。 ^1-1645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1646]]

因緣者，謂無貪、無瞋、無癡善根。 ^1-1646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1647]]

等起者，謂彼俱行思。 ^1-1647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1648]]

處所者，謂所施物。 ^1-1648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1649]]

自體者，謂正行施時身語意業。 ^1-1649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1650]]

云何施圓滿？ ^1-1650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1651]]

謂數數施故、無偏黨施故、隨其所欲圓滿施故， ^1-1651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1652]]

施得圓滿。依此義故， ^1-1652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1653]]

經作是說。為大施主者，此顯數數施義，由彼串習成性數數能施故。 ^1-1653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1654]]

一切沙門婆羅門等者，此顯無偏黨施義，無有差別一切施故。 ^1-1654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1655]]

若食若飲等者，此顯隨其所欲圓滿施義，如所意願一切資財皆施與故。 ^1-1655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1656]]

復次無所依施故、 ^1-1656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1657]]

廣清淨施故、 ^1-1657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1658]]

極歡喜施故、 ^1-1658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1659]]

數數施故、 ^1-1659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1660]]

田器施故、 ^1-1660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1661]]

善分布新舊施故， ^1-1661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1662]]

施得圓滿。 ^1-1662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1663]]

依此義故，經作是說：解脫捨舒手施，樂遠離常祠祀捨具足，於正施時樂等分布。如是諸句隨其次第，顯示無所依施等。 ^1-1663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1664]]

無所依施者，謂不迴向有及資財而行惠施。 ^1-1664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1665]]

田器者，謂貧苦田、功德田。舒手者，廣行惠施，手不潛縮故。常祠祀者，串習祠祀以成性故。捨具足者，慧為先故。於正施時樂等分布者，於來求者以所施物等分布故。 ^1-1665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1666]]

云何應知施物圓滿？ ^1-1666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1667]]

謂所施財物非誑詐得故、 ^1-1667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1668]]

所施財物非侵他得故、 ^1-1668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1669]]

所施財物非穢離垢故、 ^1-1669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1670]]

所施財物清淨故、 ^1-1670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1671]]

所施財物如法所引故， ^1-1671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1672]]

是名施物圓滿。依此義故， ^1-1672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1673]]

經作是說：發起正勤所得財物者，此顯施物非誑詐得， ^1-1673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1674]]

誑詐得者謂不起正勤而得財物，於自住處他所寄物謀詐得故。 ^1-1674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1675]]

運手臂力所得財物者，此顯施物非侵他得。 ^1-1675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1676]]

侵他得者，非自運動手力而得。 ^1-1676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1677]]

他所勤苦種種方便獲得財物，侵陵取故。 ^1-1677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1678]]

離污垢物者，此顯施物非穢離垢，由所施物遠離污垢染污故。 ^1-1678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1679]]

如法財者，此顯施物清淨，遠離刀毒酒等非淨施物故。 ^1-1679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1680]]

如法所得者，此顯施物如法所引，遠離偽斗稱等邪命財故。 ^1-1680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1681]]

復次如經中說：成就尸羅，善能防護別解脫律儀，軌則所行皆悉圓滿，見微細罪生大怖畏，於諸學處善能受學。 ^1-1681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1682]]

云何成就尸羅？ ^1-1682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1683]]

能受能護淨尸羅故。 ^1-1683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1684]]

謂受持淨戒相應無缺，故名成就尸羅。 ^1-1684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1685]]

云何善能防護別解脫律儀？ ^1-1685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1686]]

能善護持出離尸羅故。 ^1-1686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1687]]

謂為求解脫，別別防護所有律儀，故名別解脫律儀，由此律儀能速出離生死苦故。 ^1-1687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1688]]

云何軌則所行皆悉圓滿？ ^1-1688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1689]]

具淨尸羅，難為毀責故。 ^1-1689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1690]]

軌則圓滿者，諸威儀等非聰慧人所呵責故。 ^1-1690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1691]]

所行圓滿者，遠離五種諸比丘眾所不行處故。 ^1-1691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1692]]

何等為五？ 謂唱令家、 ^1-1692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1693]]

婬女家、 ^1-1693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1694]]

酤酒家、 ^1-1694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1695]]

王家、 ^1-1695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1696]]

旃荼羅羯恥那家。 ^1-1696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1697]]

云何見微細罪生大怖畏？ ^1-1697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1698]]

勇猛恭敬所學尸羅故。 ^1-1698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1699]]

於遮罪中勇猛恭敬修學護持猶如性罪，是故名為見微細罪生大怖畏。 ^1-1699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1700]]

云何於諸學處善能受學？ ^1-1700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1701]]

圓滿受學所學尸羅故。 ^1-1701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1702]]

謂具足圓滿受學學處，是故名為於諸學處善能受學。 ^1-1702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1703]]

從是已後依止尸羅， ^1-1703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1704]]

釋佛經中護身等義。 ^1-1704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1705]]

云何名為防護身語？ ^1-1705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1706]]

由彼正解所攝持故。 ^1-1706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1707]]

謂如佛所聽往來等事，必先覺察方正行故。 ^1-1707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1708]]

云何身語具足圓滿？ ^1-1708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1709]]

終不毀犯所毀犯故。 ^1-1709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1710]]

謂不違損清淨尸羅。 ^1-1710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1711]]

云何身語清淨現行？ ^1-1711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1712]]

由無悔等漸次修行，久至得定為依止故。 ^1-1712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1713]]

謂依定力令犯戒垢極遠離故。 ^1-1713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1714]]

云何身語極善現行？ ^1-1714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1715]]

染污尋思所不雜故。 ^1-1715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1716]]

謂染污尋思所不能雜，一向淨故。 ^1-1716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1717]]

云何身語無罪現行？ ^1-1717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1718]]

遠離邪願修梵行故。 ^1-1718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1719]]

謂不迴向有及資財修行梵行，為諸聖賢所稱讚故。 ^1-1719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1720]]

云何身語無害現行？ ^1-1720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1721]]

不輕陵他易共住故。 ^1-1721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1722]]

謂不由自高陵蔑於人、難共住等，為損害故。 ^1-1722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1723]]

云何身語隨順現行？ ^1-1723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1724]]

由能隨順涅槃得故。 ^1-1724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1725]]

謂能隨順得涅槃得， ^1-1725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1726]]

能引聖道故。 ^1-1726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1727]]

云何身語隨隱顯現行？ ^1-1727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1728]]

隱善顯惡故。 ^1-1728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1729]]

謂隱自功德、顯自過失。 ^1-1729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1730]]

云何身語親善現行？ ^1-1730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1731]]

同梵行者攝受尸羅故。 ^1-1731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1732]]

謂同梵行攝受尸羅，應歸趣故。 ^1-1732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1733]]

云何身語應儀現行？ ^1-1733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1734]]

於尊尊位離憍慢故。 ^1-1734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1735]]

謂於尊長及等尊長所，摧伏憍慢如應供事故。 ^1-1735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1736]]

云何身語敬順現行？ ^1-1736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1737]]

於尊教誨敬順受故。 ^1-1737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1738]]

謂於尊語言敬順而受，離自見取故。 ^1-1738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1739]]

云何身語無熱現行？ ^1-1739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1740]]

遠離苦行熱惱下劣欲解故。 ^1-1740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1741]]

謂離外道下劣欲解行諸苦行，不自燒然故。 ^1-1741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1742]]

云何身語不惱現行？ ^1-1742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1743]]

棄捨財業無悔惱故。 ^1-1743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1744]]

謂由棄捨財業無有追悔，彼於後時無熱惱故。 ^1-1744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1745]]

云何身語無悔現行？ ^1-1745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1746]]

雖得少分不以為喜，無悔恨故。 ^1-1746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1747]]

謂修善品雖獲少分不生喜足，離諸悔恨，盡其所能而修習故。 ^1-1747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1748]]

如世尊說：如是有情皆由自業， ^1-1748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1749]]

業所乖諍， ^1-1749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1750]]

從業所生， ^1-1750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1751]]

依業出離， ^1-1751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1752]]

業能分判一切有情高下勝劣。 ^1-1752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1753]]

云何有情皆由自業？ ^1-1753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1754]]

由自造業而受異熟故。 ^1-1754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1755]]

謂諸有情由其自業，故名自業。 ^1-1755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1756]]

自者，不與他共，受自業異熟，故名自業。 ^1-1756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1757]]

云何業所乖諍？ ^1-1757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1758]]

於受自業所得異熟時，善不善業互違諍故。謂諸有情由業乖諍，故名業所乖諍。 ^1-1758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1759]]

隨善惡業力自所受異熟，愛不愛別故。 ^1-1759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1760]]

問：是諸有情受自作業愛不愛異熟時，初從何生？為無因耶？為因、世、性、自在等耶？ ^1-1760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1761]]

答：從業所生。 ^1-1761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1762]]

云何從業所生？ ^1-1762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1763]]

是諸有情遠離無因惡因，唯從業所生故。 ^1-1763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1764]]

謂諸有情遠離無因惡因，唯由業等因緣所生。 ^1-1764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1765]]

如是已說依業流轉，為明歸滅亦依諸業， ^1-1765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1766]]

是故經言依業出離。云何依業出離？依對治業解業縛故。 ^1-1766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1767]]

謂依無漏業能斷有漏業故，唯依業而得出離。 ^1-1767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1768]]

云何有情高下？ ^1-1768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1769]]

謂由業故，於善惡趣得自體差別。 ^1-1769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1770]]

云何勝劣？ ^1-1770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1771]]

謂諸有情成就功德過失差別。 ^1-1771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1772]]

如世尊說：有情業異熟不可思議。如是經意，非一切種皆不可思議。 ^1-1772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1773]]

云何業異熟不可思議？云何可思議？ ^1-1773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1774]]

謂諸善業，於人天趣得可愛異熟，是可思議。 ^1-1774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1775]]

諸不善業，墮三惡趣，得不愛異熟，是可思議。 ^1-1775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1776]]

由善惡業往善惡趣，感得可愛不可愛異熟，世間智者能思議故、由此能引發正見等功德故。 ^1-1776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1777]]

即由此業，感諸有情自身異熟等種種差別不可思議。 ^1-1777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1778]]

{由善惡業往善惡趣，感得可愛不可愛異熟，世間智者能思議故、由此能引發正見等功德故。} ^1-1778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1779]]

謂內身等異熟，有形色等無量差別難可思議， ^1-1779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1780]]

除一切智不能思議，強思議者發狂等過故。 ^1-1780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1781]]

復次即善不善業處差別、事差別、因差別、異熟差別、品類差別等皆不可思議。 ^1-1781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1782]]

由即此業處差別等無量無邊，難可思議故。 ^1-1782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1783]]

處者，謂住如是處，造如是業。或於城邑、或於村落，如是等。 ^1-1783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1784]]

事者，謂所依事，或有情數、或非有情數。 ^1-1784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1785]]

因者，謂善不善根，如其所應。 ^1-1785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1786]]

異熟者，謂異熟內身。 ^1-1786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1787]]

品類者，謂種種差別無量品類。 ^1-1787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1788]]

又有種種外事差別，能感業用不可思議。 ^1-1788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1789]]

由何等業感棘刺等鋒鋩銛利，如是等類，墮在世間不思議攝。 ^1-1789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1790]]

思議世間，佛所制故。 ^1-1790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1791]]

又末尼珠、藥草、呪術相應業用不可思議。 ^1-1791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1792]]

末尼相應業者，謂月愛珠等能出水等業用難思。 ^1-1792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1793]]

{呪相應業者，謂誦此呪便不燒等。} ^1-1793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1794]]

藥相應業者，謂執持此藥藏隱形等。 ^1-1794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1795]]

呪相應業者，謂誦此呪便不燒等。 ^1-1795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1796]]

術相應業者，謂由彼彼術故治熱病等。 ^1-1796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1797]]

又諸觀行者威德業用不可思議。 ^1-1797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1798]]

云何？ ^1-1798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1799]]

彼心威德力故，能動大地昇虛空等。 ^1-1799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1800]]

又諸菩薩自在業用不可思議， ^1-1800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1801]]

所謂命自在故、 ^1-1801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1802]]

心自在故、 ^1-1802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1803]]

財自在故、 ^1-1803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1804]]

業自在故、 ^1-1804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1805]]

生自在故、 ^1-1805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1806]]

勝解自在故、 ^1-1806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1807]]

願自在故、 ^1-1807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1808]]

神通自在故、 ^1-1808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1809]]

智自在故、 ^1-1809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1810]]

法自在故。 ^1-1810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1811]]

諸大菩薩由如是等自在力故，所作業用不可思議。 ^1-1811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1812]]

謂諸菩薩由命自在力，持諸壽行隨所欲樂爾所時住。 ^1-1812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1813]]

由心自在力，隨其所樂於三摩地入出自在。 ^1-1813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1817]]

由勝解自在力，轉大地等為水火等，勝解自在。 ^1-1817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1818]]

由願自在力，隨其所樂能引無數自利利他圓滿大願。 ^1-1818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1819]]

由神通自在力，為欲攝化無量有情，顯示種種神通變現。 ^1-1819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1820]]

由智自在力，於諸法義訓釋言詞，無滯辯說圓滿究竟。 ^1-1820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1821]]

由法自在力，以無量種名句文身，建立素怛纜等無上教法，隨其所應，乃至一切有情於一時間能令彼心皆大歡喜。 ^1-1821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1822]]

又一切佛所作諸佛應所作事，業用不可思議。 ^1-1822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1823]]

云何？ ^1-1823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1824]]

如來到於究竟無功用處，證得清淨一味法界。 ^1-1824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1825]]

諸佛世尊之所應作利益安樂諸有情事，隨時如應皆能成立。 ^1-1825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1826]]

如是諸佛及佛境界不可思議。 ^1-1826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1827]]

復次如是集諦總有四種行相， ^1-1827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1828]]

所謂因相、 ^1-1828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1829]]

集相、 ^1-1829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1830]]

生相、 ^1-1830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1831]]

緣相。 ^1-1831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1832]]

因相云何？ 謂能引發後有習氣因，是名因相，由業煩惱是能引發後有習氣因故。 ^1-1832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1833]]

集相云何？謂彼彼有情所集習氣，於彼彼有情類為等起因，是名集相。 ^1-1833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1834]]

由諸有情所集習氣，於人天等有情類中能為相似形貌種類平等起因故。 ^1-1834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1835]]

生相云何？謂各別內身無量品類差別生因，是名生相。 ^1-1835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1836]]

是諸有情各別內身、相續決定趣生地等，所有一切品類差別，乃至有頂生因故。 ^1-1836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1837]]

緣相云何？謂諸有情別別得捨因，是名緣相。 ^1-1837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1838]]

能令有情得未曾得自體，捨已曾得自體故。 ^1-1838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1839]]

如是名為集諦體相。 ^1-1839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1840]]

云何滅諦？ ^1-1840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1841]]

謂由相故、 ^1-1841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1842]]

甚深故、 ^1-1842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1843]]

世俗故、 ^1-1843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1844]]

勝義故、 ^1-1844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1845]]

不圓滿故、 ^1-1845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1846]]

圓滿故、 ^1-1846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1847]]

無莊嚴故、 ^1-1847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1848]]

有莊嚴故、 ^1-1848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1849]]

有餘故、 ^1-1849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1850]]

無餘故、 ^1-1850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1851]]

最勝故、 ^1-1851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1852]]

差別故，分別滅諦。 ^1-1852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1853]]

相者，謂真如聖道煩惱不生， ^1-1853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1854]]

若滅依、若能滅、若滅性，是滅諦相。 ^1-1854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1855]]

如世尊說：眼耳及與鼻舌身及與意，於此處名色究竟滅無餘。 ^1-1855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1856]]

又說：是故汝今當觀是處，所謂此處眼究竟滅遠離色想，乃至意究竟滅遠離法想。 ^1-1856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1857]]

由此道理顯示所緣真如境上有漏法滅，是滅諦相。 ^1-1857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1858]]

甚深者，謂彼諸行究竟寂滅。如是寂滅望彼諸行，不可說異、 ^1-1858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1859]]

不異、 ^1-1859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1860]]

亦異亦不異、 ^1-1860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1861]]

非異非不異。 ^1-1861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1862]]

所以者何？ ^1-1862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1863]]

若彼諸行究竟寂滅，如是寂滅與彼諸行可說異者，應與諸行不相繫屬條然異體。 ^1-1863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1864]]

若不異者，應是染相。 ^1-1864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1865]]

由此道理，非俱非不俱。 ^1-1865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1866]]

何以故？ ^1-1866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1867]]

無戲論故。 ^1-1867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1868]]

於此義中若生戲論，非正思議、 ^1-1868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1869]]

非道、 ^1-1869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1870]]

非如、 ^1-1870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1871]]

亦非善巧方便思故。 ^1-1871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1872]]

如世尊說：此六觸處，盡、離、欲滅、寂靜、沒等。 ^1-1872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1874]]

若謂有異、無異、亦有異亦無異、非有異非無異者，於 ^1-1874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1875]]

無戲論便生戲論。 ^1-1875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1876]]

乃至有六處，可有諸戲論； ^1-1876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1877]]

六處既滅絕諸戲論，即是涅槃。 ^1-1877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1878]]

若於如是絕諸戲論寂滅涅槃不正思議，是名戲論，於應異思議乃異思議故。 ^1-1878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1879]]

云何應異思議？ ^1-1879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1880]]

謂正妙離等種種思議。 ^1-1880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1881]]

世俗者，謂以世間道摧伏種子所得滅， ^1-1881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1882]]

是故世尊別名說為彼分涅槃。 ^1-1882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1883]]

勝義者，謂以聖慧永拔種子所得滅。 ^1-1883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1884]]

不圓滿者，謂諸有學或預流果攝、或一來果攝、或不還果攝等所有滅。 ^1-1884

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1885]]

圓滿者，謂諸無學阿羅漢果攝等所有滅。 ^1-1885

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1886]]

無莊嚴者，謂慧解脫阿羅漢所有滅，無三明等最勝功德所莊嚴故。 ^1-1886

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1887]]

有莊嚴者，謂俱分解脫三明六通阿羅漢等所有滅，有無量種種量勝功德所莊嚴故。 ^1-1887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1888]]

有餘者，謂有餘事滅。 ^1-1888

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1889]]

無餘者，謂無餘事滅。 ^1-1889

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1890]]

最勝者，謂佛菩薩無住涅槃攝所有滅，以常安住一切有情利樂事故。 ^1-1890

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1891]]

差別者，謂無餘永斷、 ^1-1891

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1892]]

永出、 ^1-1892

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1893]]

永吐、 ^1-1893

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1894]]

盡、 ^1-1894

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1895]]

離欲、 ^1-1895

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1896]]

滅、 ^1-1896

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1897]]

寂靜、 ^1-1897

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1898]]

沒等。 ^1-1898

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1899]]

何故名無餘永斷？由餘句故。 ^1-1899

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1900]]

謂無餘永斷是標句， ^1-1900

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1901]]

餘是釋句， ^1-1901

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1902]]

是故說言由餘句故， ^1-1902

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1903]]

由後別句釋此總故。 ^1-1903

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1904]]

纏及隨眠皆悉永斷故，名無餘永斷。 ^1-1904

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1905]]

何故名永出？永出諸纏故。 ^1-1905

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1906]]

此依斷諸纏說，謂已生者皆遠離故。 ^1-1906

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1907]]

何故名永吐？永吐隨眠故。 ^1-1907

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1908]]

此依斷隨眠說，謂除根本永不生故。 ^1-1908

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1909]]

如是諸滅由見修道對治別故，建立二種，盡及離欲故。 ^1-1909

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1910]]

次問言何故名盡？見道對治得離繫故。 ^1-1910

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1911]]

煩惱聚中餘少分故，亦名為盡。 ^1-1911

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1912]]

何故名離欲？ ^1-1912

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1913]]

修道對治得離繫故。 ^1-1913

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1914]]

由彼修道離諸地欲漸次所顯，故名離欲。 ^1-1914

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1915]]

由有如是俱離繫故，當來苦滅故。 ^1-1915

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1916]]

次問言何故名滅？當來彼果苦不生故，能成未來苦不生法，故名為滅。 ^1-1916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1917]]

又於現法憂惱寂靜故。 ^1-1917

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1918]]

次問言何故名寂靜？ ^1-1918

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1919]]

於現法中彼果心苦永不行故。 ^1-1919

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1920]]

何故名沒？餘所有事永滅沒故。 ^1-1920

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1921]]

宿業煩惱所感諸蘊自然滅盡，故名為沒。 ^1-1921

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1922]]

由如是等別句，釋前無餘永滅總句。 ^1-1922

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1923]]

何故此滅復名無為？離三相故。 ^1-1923

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1924]]

生滅住異三有為相究竟相違，故名無為。 ^1-1924

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1925]]

何故此滅復名難見？超過肉眼、天眼境故。 ^1-1925

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1926]]

唯聖慧眼所行境界，故名難見。 ^1-1926

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1927]]

何故此滅復名不轉？永離諸趣差別轉故。 ^1-1927

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1928]]

離地獄等往來流轉恒常安住，故名不轉。 ^1-1928

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1929]]

何故此滅名不卑屈？ ^1-1929

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1930]]

離三愛故。 ^1-1930

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1931]]

永離欲色無色三愛，於諸有中無所卑屈，故名不卑屈。 ^1-1931

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1932]]

何故此滅復名甘露？ ^1-1932

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1933]]

離蘊魔故。 ^1-1933

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1934]]

永離一切死所依蘊，故名甘露。 ^1-1934

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1935]]

何故此滅復名無漏？永離一切煩惱魔故。 ^1-1935

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1936]]

何故此滅復名舍宅？無罪喜樂所依事故。 ^1-1936

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1937]]

解脫喜樂所依止故名舍宅。 ^1-1937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1938]]

何故此滅復名洲渚？三界隔絕故。 ^1-1938

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1939]]

於生死大海挺出高原，故譬洲渚。 ^1-1939

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1940]]

何故此滅復名弘濟？能遮一切大苦災橫故。 ^1-1940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1941]]

證得此滅，生老病等諸苦災橫永遠離故。 ^1-1941

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1942]]

何故此滅復名歸依？ ^1-1942

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1943]]

無有虛妄意樂方便所依處故。 ^1-1943

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1944]]

由於彼滅所發意樂及正方便，無虛妄性所依處故。 所依止義是歸依義。 ^1-1944

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1945]]

何故此滅名勝歸趣？能為歸趣一切最勝聖性所依處故。 ^1-1945

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1946]]

由此寂滅能為歸趣最勝聖性所依止處，是阿羅漢證得方便所緣境故。 ^1-1946

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1947]]

何故此滅復名不死？永離生故。 ^1-1947

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1948]]

諸無生者必不死故。 ^1-1948

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1949]]

何故此滅名無熱惱？ ^1-1949

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1950]]

永離一切煩惱熱故。 ^1-1950

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1951]]

永離一切求不得苦大熱惱故。 ^1-1951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1952]]

何故此滅名無熾然？ ^1-1952

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1953]]

永離一切愁嘆憂苦諸惱亂故。 ^1-1953

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1954]]

一切愁等熾然永息極清涼故，名無熾然。 ^1-1954

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1955]]

何故此滅復名安隱？ ^1-1955

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1956]]

離怖畏住所依處故。 ^1-1956

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1957]]

無老病死等一切怖畏，聖住所依故名安隱。 ^1-1957

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1958]]

何故此滅復名清涼？ ^1-1958

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1959]]

諸利益事所依處故。 ^1-1959

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1960]]

一切清涼善法所依，故名清涼。 ^1-1960

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1961]]

何故此滅復名樂事？第一義樂事故。 ^1-1961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1962]]

出世間樂所依事，故名樂事。 ^1-1962

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1963]]

何故此滅名趣吉祥？ ^1-1963

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1964]]

為證得彼易修方便所依處故， ^1-1964

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1965]]

為證涅槃易修方便所緣境故。 ^1-1965

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1966]]

何故此滅復名無病？ ^1-1966

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1967]]

永離一切障礙病故。 ^1-1967

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1968]]

離煩惱等諸障礙病，故名無病。 ^1-1968

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1969]]

何故此滅復名不動？ ^1-1969

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1970]]

永離一切散動故。 ^1-1970

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1971]]

離諸境界戲論散動，故名不動。 ^1-1971

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1972]]

何故此滅復名涅槃？ ^1-1972

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1973]]

無相寂滅大安樂住所依處故。 ^1-1973

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1974]]

永離一切色等諸想，究竟寂滅大安樂住所緣境故，名為涅槃。 ^1-1974

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1975]]

復次依滅諦辯無生等名義差別， ^1-1975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1976]]

與苦諦相義相違故。 ^1-1976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1977]]

苦諦相者，於彼彼處有情類中相續而生。 ^1-1977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1978]]

為翻彼相，是故問言：何故此滅復名無生？離相續故。 ^1-1978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1979]]

苦諦相者續生已後，自身眾分漸次圓滿。 ^1-1979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1980]]

為翻彼相，是故問言：何故此滅復名無起？ ^1-1980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1981]]

永離此後漸生起故。 ^1-1981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1982]]

苦諦相者，宿業煩惱勢力所造。 ^1-1982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1983]]

為翻彼相，是故問言：何故此滅復名無造？ ^1-1983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1984]]

永離前際諸業煩惱勢力所引故。 ^1-1984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1985]]

又現在苦是能造作，餘有異熟諸業煩惱所依止處。 ^1-1985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1986]]

為翻彼相，是故問言：何故此滅復名無作？ ^1-1986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1987]]

不作現在諸業煩惱所依處故。 ^1-1987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1988]]

又苦相者，後有異熟相續生起無有間斷。 ^1-1988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1989]]

為翻彼相，是故問言：何故此滅復名不生？ ^1-1989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1990]]

永離未來相續生故。 ^1-1990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1992]]

復次滅諦有四種相，謂 ^1-1992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1993]]

滅相、 ^1-1993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1994]]

靜相、 ^1-1994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1995]]

妙相、 ^1-1995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1996]]

離相。 ^1-1996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1997]]

何故名滅相？煩惱離繫故。 ^1-1997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1998]]

謂流轉因煩惱離繫，故名滅。 ^1-1998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-1999]]

何故名靜相？ ^1-1999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2000]]

苦離繫故。 ^1-2000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2001]]

行苦所攝不寂靜相取蘊離繫，故名靜。 ^1-2001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2002]]

何故名妙相？ ^1-2002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2003]]

樂淨事故。 ^1-2003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2004]]

諸煩惱苦究竟離繫，自然樂淨以為自體，故名妙。 ^1-2004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2005]]

何故名離相？ ^1-2005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2006]]

常利益事故。 ^1-2006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2007]]

不復退還、最極安隱，如其次第名常利益。 ^1-2007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2008]]

安隱利益最勝善性，是滅諦相。 ^1-2008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2009]]

云何道諦？ ^1-2009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2010]]

謂由此道故，知苦、 ^1-2010

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2011]]

斷集、 ^1-2011

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2012]]

證滅、 ^1-2012

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2013]]

修道， ^1-2013

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2014]]

是略說道諦相。 ^1-2014

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2015]]

今於此中依四聖諦，以其作用顯道體相。 ^1-2015

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2016]]

又道有五種，謂 ^1-2016

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2017]]

資糧道、 ^1-2017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2018]]

方便道、 ^1-2018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2019]]

見道、 ^1-2019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2020]]

修道、 ^1-2020

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2021]]

究竟道。 ^1-2021

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2022]]

如是五種，依道自性及眷屬，以顯道諦差別。 ^1-2022

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2023]]

資糧道者，謂諸異生所有尸羅， ^1-2023

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2024]]

守護根門、 ^1-2024

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2025]]

飲食知量、 ^1-2025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2026]]

初夜後夜常不睡眠、勤修止觀、 ^1-2026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2027]]

正知而住。 ^1-2027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2028]]

復有所餘進習諸善， ^1-2028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2029]]

聞思修所生慧修習此故，得成現觀解脫所依器性。 ^1-2029

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2030]]

謂諸異生所有尸羅乃至正知而住等，是資糧道體， ^1-2030

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2031]]

由彼修習淨尸羅等極圓滿故。復有所餘進習諸善者，謂無悔等，由聞思等所生諸慧得成煖等，次第見諦，永斷諸障相續堪任性。 ^1-2031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2032]]

方便道者，謂所有資糧皆是方便。 ^1-2032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2033]]

或有方便非資糧，謂已積集資糧道者所有順決擇分善根， ^1-2033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2034]]

謂煖法、頂法、順諦忍法、世第一法。 ^1-2034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2035]]

煖法者，謂各別內證於諸諦中明得三摩地鉢羅若及彼相應等法， ^1-2035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2036]]

由淨定心依諦增上契經等法，於意言門諸義顯現。緣彼所生奢摩他、毘鉢舍那等，是名煖法。 ^1-2036

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2037]]

頂法者，謂各別內證於諸諦中明增三摩地鉢羅若及如相應等法， ^1-2037

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2038]]

由彼頂法展轉增進居上位故。 ^1-2038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2039]]

順諦忍法者，謂各別內證於諸諦中一分已入隨順三摩地、鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^1-2039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2040]]

一分已入者，於無所取一向忍解故。 ^1-2040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2041]]

一分隨順者，於無能取隨順通達所依處故。 ^1-2041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2042]]

世第一法者，謂各別內證於諸諦中無間心三摩地鉢羅若及彼相應等法， ^1-2042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2043]]

從此無間必起最初出世道故。 ^1-2043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2044]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第八大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第九安慧菩薩糅大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中諦品第一之四 ^1-2044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2045]]

云何見道？ ^1-2045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2046]]

若總說，謂世第一法無間無所得三摩地鉢羅若及彼相應等法。 ^1-2046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2047]]

由無分別奢摩他、毘鉢舍那等為體相故。 ^1-2047

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2048]]

又所緣能緣平等平等智為其相， ^1-2048

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2049]]

由此通達所取能取無性真如故。 ^1-2049

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2050]]

又遣各別有情假、 ^1-2050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2051]]

法假，遍遣二假所緣法智為相。 ^1-2051

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2052]]

云何遣各別有情假所緣法智為相？ ^1-2052

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2053]]

由此智於自相續中不分別我相故。 ^1-2053

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2054]]

不分別者，是除遣義。 ^1-2054

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2055]]

云何遣各別法假所緣法智為相？ ^1-2055

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2056]]

由此智於自相續中不分別色等法相故。 ^1-2056

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2057]]

云何遍遣二假所緣法智為相？ ^1-2057

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2058]]

由此智於一切處無有差別，不分別我及法相故。 ^1-2058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2059]]

復次若別說見道差別， ^1-2059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2060]]

謂世第一法無間苦法智忍、 ^1-2060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2061]]

苦法智、 ^1-2061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2062]]

苦類智忍、 ^1-2062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2063]]

苦類智， ^1-2063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2064]]

集法智忍、 ^1-2064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2065]]

集法智、 ^1-2065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2066]]

集類智忍、 ^1-2066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2067]]

集類智， ^1-2067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2068]]

滅法智忍、 ^1-2068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2069]]

滅法智、 ^1-2069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2070]]

滅類智忍、 ^1-2070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2071]]

滅類智， ^1-2071

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2072]]

道法智忍、 ^1-2072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2073]]

道法智、 ^1-2073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2074]]

道類智忍、 ^1-2074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2075]]

道類智， ^1-2075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2076]]

如是十六種。 ^1-2076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2077]]

於諸聖諦中法類智忍及法類智是見道差別相。 ^1-2077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2078]]

苦者，謂苦諦。 ^1-2078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2079]]

苦法者，謂苦諦增上所起教法。 ^1-2079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2080]]

法智者，謂於方便道中觀察諦增上法智。 ^1-2080

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2081]]

智忍者，謂先觀察增上力故，於各別苦諦中起現證無漏慧， ^1-2081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2082]]

由此慧故永捨見苦所斷一切煩惱。今於此中所說義者， ^1-2082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2083]]

謂於方便道中觀察依止苦諦所起契經等法，如理作意所攝智增上力故， ^1-2083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2084]]

於自相續苦諦中現證彼真如，出世間慧正見體生， ^1-2084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2085]]

由此慧故永捨一切見苦所斷三界所繫二十八隨眠， ^1-2085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2086]]

是故名為苦法智忍。 ^1-2086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2087]]

苦法智者，謂忍無間由此智故於前所斷煩惱解脫而得作證。 ^1-2087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2088]]

所以者何？ ^1-2088

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2089]]

先由忍故永斷一切見苦所斷煩惱， ^1-2089

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2090]]

令所依轉從此無間由如是智生證得轉依，是名苦法智。 ^1-2090

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2091]]

苦類智忍者，謂苦法智無間無漏慧生於苦法智忍及苦法智。各別內證，言後諸聖法皆是此種類。 ^1-2091

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2092]]

所以者何？ ^1-2092

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2093]]

由初二種若忍若智，是後一切學與無學聖法種類，從此彼得生故。 ^1-2093

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2094]]

是故無漏慧生各別內證。緣此為境，言後諸聖法皆是此種類，是故名為苦類智忍。 ^1-2094

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2095]]

苦類智者，謂此無間無漏智生，審定印可苦類智忍。 ^1-2095

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2096]]

所以者何？ ^1-2096

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2097]]

由苦類智忍無間無漏智生，於苦類智忍內證印可，故名苦類智。 ^1-2097

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2098]]

如是於餘諦中隨其所應諸忍諸智，盡當知於此位中由法忍法智覺悟所取，由類忍類智覺悟能取。 ^1-2098

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2099]]

所以者何？ ^1-2099

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2100]]

出世間道有二境界， ^1-2100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2101]]

謂真如及正智， ^1-2101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2102]]

法智品道真如為境、 ^1-2102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2103]]

類智品道正智為境， ^1-2103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2104]]

由此諸忍智如實了知故。 ^1-2104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2105]]

又於此一切忍智位中說名安住無相觀者， ^1-2105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2106]]

如薄伽梵說第六無相住補特伽羅者，即現住此忍智位中者是也， ^1-2106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2107]]

於此位中一切相皆不可得故。 ^1-2107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2108]]

又無相住有六種，謂空、無相、無願、滅定、有頂、見道。 ^1-2108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2109]]

如是十六心剎那說名見道。 ^1-2109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2110]]

所以者何？ ^1-2110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2111]]

由如是忍智所攝十六心剎那，於曾所未見四聖諦境各以四剎那見故，名為見道。 ^1-2111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2112]]

又心剎那者，謂於所知境智生究竟名一剎那， ^1-2112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2113]]

非唯於本無今有生時名心剎那。何以故？乃至於所知境能知智生所作究竟名一剎那故。 ^1-2113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2114]]

如說苦應遍知是一心剎那， ^1-2114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2115]]

如是集應永斷等亦爾。 ^1-2115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2116]]

又如上說見道差別， ^1-2116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2117]]

皆假建立非真實爾。 ^1-2117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2118]]

何以故？ ^1-2118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2119]]

出世位中各別內證絕戲論故。 ^1-2119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2120]]

復次一切道諦由四種相應隨覺了，謂 ^1-2120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2121]]

安立故、 ^1-2121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2122]]

思惟故、 ^1-2122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2123]]

證受故、 ^1-2123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2124]]

圓滿故。 ^1-2124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2125]]

安立者，謂聲聞等隨自所證已得究竟， ^1-2125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2126]]

為欲令他亦了知故， ^1-2126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2127]]

由後得智以無量種名句文身安立道諦， ^1-2127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2128]]

謂於諸諦中有如是如是忍、如是如是智，如是等。 ^1-2128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2129]]

思惟者，謂正修習現觀方便，以世間智如所安立思惟數習。 ^1-2129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2130]]

證受者，謂如是數習已， ^1-2130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2131]]

自內證受最初見道，正出世間無戲論位。 ^1-2131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2132]]

圓滿者，謂此位後圓滿轉依乃至證得究竟。 ^1-2132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2133]]

彼既證得究竟位已，復由後得智以名句文身安立道諦。 ^1-2133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2134]]

如是四相，真實道輪數數展轉，相依而轉無有斷絕。 ^1-2134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2135]]

如經言，遠塵離垢於諸法中正法眼生者，此依見道說。 ^1-2135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2136]]

諸法忍能遠塵、 ^1-2136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2137]]

諸法智能離垢， ^1-2137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2138]]

由彼最初於諸諦中妙聖慧眼為自性故。 ^1-2138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2139]]

法忍能遠塵者，由諸法忍永斷一切煩惱塵故。 ^1-2139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2140]]

法智能離垢者，由諸法智已斷障垢依得生故。 ^1-2140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2141]]

又於此忍智兩位如其次第，遍智故、永斷故，道得清淨，依此而說遠塵離垢。 ^1-2141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2142]]

又經言，見法、得法、極通達法、究竟堅法，越度一切希望疑惑，不假他緣，於大師教餘不能引，於諸法中得無所畏者，此亦依見道說。 ^1-2142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2143]]

於所化有情，聖諦現觀無間說故。 ^1-2143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2144]]

見法者，謂諸法忍， ^1-2144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2145]]

由彼通達真實法故。 ^1-2145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2146]]

得法者，謂諸法智， ^1-2146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2147]]

彼於轉依能作證故。 ^1-2147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2148]]

極通達法者，謂諸類忍。 ^1-2148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2149]]

通達諸聖法，是此二類故。 ^1-2149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2150]]

究竟堅法者，謂諸類智。 ^1-2150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2151]]

於諸所知已究竟故， ^1-2151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2152]]

越度一切希望者， ^1-2152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2153]]

由諸忍智出世間道， ^1-2153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2154]]

證得長夜所希聖果，於自所證無希慮故。 ^1-2154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2155]]

越度一切疑惑者， ^1-2155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2156]]

於此位中於他所證無有猶豫，謂餘亦能證此勝位故。 ^1-2156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2157]]

不假他緣者， ^1-2157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2158]]

於所修道中雖無他導引自然善巧故。 ^1-2158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2159]]

於大師教餘不能引者，於佛聖教已得證淨，雖轉餘生不為邪道所化引故。 ^1-2159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2160]]

於諸法中得無所畏者，於依所證問記法中， ^1-2160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2161]]

如惡欲增上慢者怯劣心永無有故。 ^1-2161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2162]]

云何修道？ ^1-2162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2163]]

謂見道上所有世間道出世間道、 ^1-2163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2164]]

軟道中道上道、 ^1-2164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2165]]

方便道、 ^1-2165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2166]]

無間道、 ^1-2166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2167]]

解脫道、 ^1-2167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2168]]

勝進道等，皆名修道。 ^1-2168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2169]]

所以者何？ ^1-2169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2170]]

諸佛聖弟子已得諦現觀， ^1-2170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2171]]

從此已上為斷餘結，方便數習世間道等，是名修道。 ^1-2171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2172]]

世間道者，謂 ^1-2172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2173]]

世間初靜慮、第二靜慮、第三靜慮、 ^1-2173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2174]]

第四靜慮， ^1-2174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2175]]

空無邊處、 ^1-2175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2176]]

識無邊處、 ^1-2176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2177]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2178]]

非想非非想處。 ^1-2178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2179]]

如是靜慮無色，由四種相應廣分別，謂雜染故、 ^1-2179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2180]]

清白故、 ^1-2180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2181]]

建立故、 ^1-2181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2182]]

清淨故。 ^1-2182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2183]]

雜染者，謂四無記根：一愛、二見、三慢、四無明。 ^1-2183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2184]]

由此四惑染污其心，於諸染污靜慮定門令色無色界一切有覆無記，煩惱隨煩惱生長不絕。 ^1-2184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2185]]

所以者何？ ^1-2185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2186]]

由有愛故，味上靜慮雜染所染， ^1-2186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2187]]

貪味淨定輕安樂故。 ^1-2187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2188]]

由有見故，見上靜慮雜染所染， ^1-2188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2189]]

依止靜慮發起計度先際等見故。 ^1-2189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2190]]

由有慢故，慢上靜慮雜染所染， ^1-2190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2191]]

依證勝定起高慢故。 ^1-2191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2192]]

由無明故，疑上靜慮雜染所染， ^1-2192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2193]]

求解脫者由未通達真實道理，於勝品所證常生疑惑，為解脫耶不解脫耶。 ^1-2193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2194]]

如是煩惱恒染其心，令色無色大小二惑相續流轉。 ^1-2194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2195]]

清白者，謂淨靜慮無色，由性善故說名清白。 ^1-2195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2196]]

雖是世間，離纏垢故亦名為淨。 ^1-2196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2197]]

建立者，有四種建立， ^1-2197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2198]]

謂支分建立、 ^1-2198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2199]]

等至建立、 ^1-2199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2200]]

品類建立、 ^1-2200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2201]]

名想建立。 ^1-2201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2202]]

於諸靜慮具四建立； ^1-2202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2203]]

諸無色中唯有三種，除支分。 ^1-2203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2204]]

支分建立者，謂初靜慮有五支。 ^1-2204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2205]]

何等為五？ ^1-2205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2206]]

一尋、 ^1-2206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2207]]

二伺、 ^1-2207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2208]]

三喜、 ^1-2208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2209]]

四樂、 ^1-2209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2210]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2211]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2212]]

一內等淨、 ^1-2212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2213]]

二喜、 ^1-2213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2214]]

三樂、 ^1-2214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2215]]

四心一境性。 ^1-2215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2216]]

第三靜慮有五支。 ^1-2216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2217]]

何等為五？一捨、 ^1-2217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2218]]

二念、 ^1-2218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2219]]

三正智、 ^1-2219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2220]]

四樂、 ^1-2220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2221]]

五心一境性。 ^1-2221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2222]]

第四靜慮有四支。 ^1-2222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2223]]

何等為四？ ^1-2223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2224]]

一捨清淨、 ^1-2224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2225]]

二念清淨、 ^1-2225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2226]]

三不苦不樂受、 ^1-2226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2227]]

四心一境性。 ^1-2227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2228]]

問：法有無量，何故唯立尋等為支？ ^1-2228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2229]]

答：對治支故、利益支故、彼二所依自性支故。 ^1-2229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2230]]

由此三種支分滿足，不待餘故。 ^1-2230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2231]]

初靜慮中尋伺二種是對治支， ^1-2231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2232]]

能斷欲界欲恚害等尋伺故。 ^1-2232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2233]]

喜樂二種是利益支， ^1-2233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2234]]

由尋伺支治所治已得離生喜樂故。 ^1-2234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2235]]

心一境性是彼二所依自性支， ^1-2235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2236]]

依止定力尋等轉故。 ^1-2236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2237]]

第二靜慮中內等淨是對治支， ^1-2237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2238]]

由此能治尋伺故。 ^1-2238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2239]]

喜樂是利益支。心一境性是彼二所依自性支，義如前說。 ^1-2239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2240]]

第三靜慮中捨念正知是對治支， ^1-2240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2241]]

由此三能治喜故。 ^1-2241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2242]]

樂是利益支。心一境性是彼二所依自性支，義如前說。 ^1-2242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2243]]

第四靜慮中捨清淨念清淨是對治支， ^1-2243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2244]]

由此二能治樂故。 ^1-2244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2245]]

不苦不樂受是利益支。 ^1-2245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2246]]

心一境性是彼二所依自性支。 ^1-2246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2247]]

諸無色中不立支分，以奢摩他一味性故。 ^1-2247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2248]]

云何等至建立者？ ^1-2248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2249]]

謂由七種作意證入初靜慮，如是乃至非想非非想處。 ^1-2249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2250]]

何等名為七種作意？ ^1-2250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2251]]

謂了相作意、 ^1-2251
