![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2252]]

勝解作意、 ^1-2252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2253]]

遠離作意、 ^1-2253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2254]]

攝樂作意、 ^1-2254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2255]]

觀察作意、 ^1-2255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2256]]

方便究竟作意、方便究竟果作意。 ^1-2256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2257]]

此廣分別，如〈聲聞地〉後瑜伽處。 ^1-2257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2258]]

云何證入初靜慮時由七作意？ ^1-2258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2259]]

謂由定地作意，見欲界中過患等故，了達麁相。 ^1-2259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2260]]

初靜慮中此相無故，名為靜相。是名了相作意。 ^1-2260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2261]]

如是作意猶為聞思之所間雜，從此已上超越聞思一向修相，緣麁靜相以為境界，修奢摩他、毘鉢舍那，數數思惟如所尋思麁靜性相，是名勝解作意。 ^1-2261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2262]]

由修習此故，最初斷道生彼俱行作意，名遠離作意， ^1-2262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2263]]

由此能斷上品煩惱故，及能遠離彼品麁重故。 ^1-2263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2264]]

此觀行者復欣樂上斷見上斷功德， ^1-2264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2265]]

已觸少分遠離喜樂， ^1-2265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2266]]

為欲除去惛沈睡眠，時時修習淨妙作意以悅其心，是名攝樂作意。 ^1-2266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2267]]

如是正修行者方便善品所資持故， ^1-2267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2268]]

令欲界繫煩惱纏垢不復現行，因此為欲審察煩惱斷與未斷，復更作意觀察彼生隨順淨相，是名觀察作意。 ^1-2268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2269]]

如是行者數數觀察進修對治，為令欲界一切煩惱於暫時間得離繫故， ^1-2269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2270]]

此對治道相應作意，是初靜慮最後方便故，名方便究竟作意。 ^1-2270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2271]]

從此無間證得根本最初靜慮俱行作意，是名方便究竟果作意。 ^1-2271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2272]]

又由了相作意發希願心，為正了知所應斷所應得，為斷故、為得故， ^1-2272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2273]]

由勝解作意為所求義發正方便， ^1-2273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2274]]

由遠離作意捨上品惑， ^1-2274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2275]]

由攝樂作意捨中品惑， ^1-2275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2276]]

由觀察作意安心於所證、遠離增上慢， ^1-2276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2277]]

由方便究竟作意捨下品惑， ^1-2277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2278]]

由方便究竟果作意領彼所修作意修果。 ^1-2278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2279]]

如為證入初靜慮定修七作意，如是乃至為證入非想非非想處定，如應當知。 ^1-2279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2280]]

又麁相者，謂於一切下地，從欲界乃至無所有處， ^1-2280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2281]]

如是麁相略有二種： ^1-2281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2282]]

一住於重苦，不寂靜住故； ^1-2282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2283]]

二命行微少，壽命短促故。 ^1-2283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2284]]

靜相者，謂於一切上地，從初靜慮乃至非想非非想處，與麁相相違故。 ^1-2284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2285]]

品類建立者，於初靜慮定具三品熏修，謂軟、中、上。 ^1-2285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2286]]

如初靜慮，餘靜慮及無色，三品熏修亦爾。 ^1-2286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2287]]

由軟中上品熏修初靜慮故，於初靜慮中還生三異熟。 ^1-2287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2288]]

如初靜慮，於餘靜慮中若熏修若生果各三品亦爾。 ^1-2288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2289]]

諸靜慮中三品熏修生三果者， ^1-2289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2290]]

謂梵眾天、梵輔天、大梵天如是等，廣說如前。 ^1-2290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2291]]

於無色界中無別處所故，不立生果差別。 ^1-2291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2292]]

所以者何？ ^1-2292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2293]]

於無色界無有安堵宮殿等處故，不建立生果差別。 ^1-2293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2294]]

然由三品熏修無色定故，彼異熟生時有高有下、有劣有勝。 ^1-2294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2295]]

彼異熟生時有高有下者，由壽命等有差別故。 ^1-2295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2296]]

有劣勝者，染污不染污、多分少分有差別故。 ^1-2296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2297]]

名想建立者， ^1-2297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2299]]

謂於四靜慮中三摩地差別，無量名字、不可算數、不可思議。 ^1-2299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2300]]

何以故？ ^1-2300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2301]]

於初靜慮所攝定中，諸佛世尊及得究竟大威德菩薩摩訶薩所入三摩地，彼三摩地一切聲聞及獨覺等尚不了其名，豈能知數，況復證入。 ^1-2301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2302]]

如《般若波羅蜜多經》中說三摩地其數過百。如是於餘大乘經中說三摩地其數無量。 ^1-2302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2303]]

如於初靜慮所攝定，於餘靜慮無色所攝定亦爾。 ^1-2303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2304]]

如是所說皆依靜慮波羅蜜多。 ^1-2304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2305]]

清淨者，謂初靜慮中邊際定，乃至非想非非想處邊際定，是名清淨靜慮。 ^1-2305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2306]]

無色邊際定者，為欲引發勝品功德得自在等，修堪任定到究竟處故。 ^1-2306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2307]]

出世間道者，謂於修道中法智類智品所攝苦智集智滅智道智。 ^1-2307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2308]]

如是八智相於見道中已廣說。 ^1-2308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2309]]

及彼相應三摩地等，或未至定所攝，或初靜慮乃至無所有處所攝。 ^1-2309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2310]]

非想非非想處唯是世間，不明了想恒現行故。 ^1-2310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2311]]

由不明了想恒現在前，非極明了現行聖道之所依止，是故一向世間所攝。 ^1-2311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2312]]

由此道理，彼想羸劣不能猛利取所緣相，故名無相。 ^1-2312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2313]]

復云何知非想非非想處無有聖道？ ^1-2313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2314]]

由世尊說：乃至有想三摩鉢底，方能如實照了通達。 ^1-2314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2315]]

滅盡三摩鉢底是出世間， ^1-2315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2316]]

由聖道後所證得故，要於人趣方能引發。 ^1-2316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2317]]

言引發者，是初起義。 ^1-2317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2318]]

或於人趣或於色界能現在前， ^1-2318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2319]]

先已生起後重現前故。或於人趣者，謂即於此生。或於色界者，謂後生彼。 ^1-2319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2321]]

云何聖弟子已得無色定已離色界，欲復生色界耶？ ^1-2321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2322]]

不必永離色界欲方入無色定， ^1-2322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2323]]

是故此中應作四句。 ^1-2323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2324]]

若已離色界欲者，一切皆能入無色界寂靜解脫定耶？ ^1-2324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2325]]

設能入無色界寂靜解脫定者，一切已離色界欲耶？ 答：此初句者，謂依未至定已離色界欲，而不能得入無色界寂靜解脫定。 ^1-2325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2326]]

第二句者，謂諸聖者已得第四靜慮，不求生無色界而起厭背，第四靜慮行恒現在前。 捨斷結道依勝進道，漸次能入無色界寂靜解脫定。 ^1-2326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2327]]

第三句者，謂即此行者勤求離欲，依斷結道漸次能入無色界寂靜解脫定。 ^1-2327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2328]]

第四句者，謂除上爾所相。 ^1-2328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2329]]

問：無色界中何故滅定不起現前？ ^1-2329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2330]]

答：生無色界此滅盡定多分不起現前，由住寂靜解脫異熟者於此滅定多不發起勤方便故。所以者何？ ^1-2330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2331]]

諸聖弟子為欲安住寂靜住故，於人趣等引此滅定令現在前。 ^1-2331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2332]]

若已生無色界者，不由功用自然安住第一寂靜解脫異熟住， ^1-2332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2333]]

不復發起方便功用求此滅定令現在前。 ^1-2333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2334]]

軟道者，謂軟軟、軟中、軟上品道。 ^1-2334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2335]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地地中上上、上中、上下三品煩惱。 ^1-2335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2336]]

中道者，謂中軟、中中、中上品道。 ^1-2336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2337]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地地中中上、中中、中軟三品煩惱。 ^1-2337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2338]]

上道者，謂上軟、上中、上上三品道。 ^1-2338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2339]]

由此道故，能捨三界所繫地地中軟上、軟中、軟軟三品煩惱。 ^1-2339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2340]]

如是軟中上品道，復各別分為軟等三，建立九品，為顯修道所斷煩惱漸次斷故。 ^1-2340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2341]]

復何因緣軟軟品道能斷上上品煩惱？ ^1-2341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2342]]

由此煩惱於極猛利毀滅慚愧無羞恥者身中麁重現行，易可覺了易可分別， ^1-2342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2343]]

是故此上品煩惱猶如麁垢，微少對治即能除遣。 ^1-2343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2344]]

若下下品煩惱在與上相違者身中微隱現行，難可覺了難可分別，如微隱垢大力對治方能除遣。 ^1-2344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2345]]

由此道理，當知所餘能治所治相翻建立亦爾。 ^1-2345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2346]]

方便道者，謂由此道能捨煩惱。 ^1-2346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2347]]

所以者何？ ^1-2347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2348]]

由正修如是道時，能漸捨離各別上品等煩惱所生品類麁重一分，漸得轉依，是名修道中方便道。 ^1-2348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2349]]

無間道者，謂由此道無間永斷煩惱，令無所餘。 ^1-2349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2350]]

所以者何？ ^1-2350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2351]]

由此道無間能永除遣此品煩惱所生品類麁重，令無有餘。 ^1-2351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2352]]

又轉麁重依得無麁重，是名修道中無間道。 ^1-2352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2353]]

解脫道者，謂由此道證斷煩惱所得解脫。 ^1-2353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2354]]

所以者何？ 由此道能證煩惱永斷所得轉依故。 ^1-2354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2356]]

勝進道者，謂為斷餘品煩惱，所有方便無間解脫道，是名勝進道。 ^1-2356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2357]]

所以者何？ ^1-2357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2358]]

為斷此品後餘煩惱所有方便無間解脫道，望此品是勝進，故名勝進道。 ^1-2358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2359]]

又復棄捨斷煩惱方便，或勤方便思惟諸法、或勤方便安住諸法、或進修餘三摩鉢底諸所有道，名勝進道。 ^1-2359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2360]]

又復者，為顯餘義。捨斷煩惱諸方便道， ^1-2360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2361]]

但正思惟契經等法，或復於先所思所證法中安住觀察，或復進入餘勝品定，諸如是等名勝進道。 ^1-2361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2362]]

又為引發勝品功德，或復安住諸所有道，名勝進道。 ^1-2362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2363]]

所以者何？ ^1-2363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2364]]

若為引發神通無量等諸勝品功德，或彼生已現前安住，如是等道名勝進道。 ^1-2364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2365]]

如是已廣說修道相差別，今乘義勢更辯諸道修之差別。 修云何？ ^1-2365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2366]]

略說有四種，謂 ^1-2366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2367]]

得修、 ^1-2367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2368]]

習修、 ^1-2368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2369]]

除去修、 ^1-2369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2370]]

對治修。 ^1-2370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2371]]

得修者，謂未生善法修習令生。 ^1-2371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2372]]

習修者，謂已生善法修令堅住不忘倍復增廣。 ^1-2372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2373]]

除去修者，謂已生惡不善法修令永斷。 ^1-2373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2374]]

對治修者，謂未生惡不善法修令不生。 ^1-2374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2375]]

如是四種修差別相，隨其所應依四正斷說。 ^1-2375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2376]]

為得故修名為得修，由此修力得所未得諸善法故。 ^1-2376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2377]]

習即是修名為習修，由此修力數習已得諸善法故。 ^1-2377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2378]]

為除故修名除去修，由此修力除去現行位諸不善法故。 ^1-2378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2379]]

修習對治名對治修，對治未來諸不善法令成不生法故。 ^1-2379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2380]]

又道生時能安立自習氣，是名得修， ^1-2380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2381]]

從此種類展轉增盛相續生故。 ^1-2381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2382]]

又即此道現前修習，是名習修， ^1-2382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2383]]

由即此道現前行故。 ^1-2383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2384]]

又即此道現在前時能捨自障，是名除去修， ^1-2384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2385]]

由此能滅自所對治麁重障故。 ^1-2385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2386]]

又即此道既捨自障，又令彼未來住不生法中，是名對治修， ^1-2386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2387]]

由已得轉依於未來世安置彼障令住不生法故。 ^1-2387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2388]]

又由具四種對治故，名對治修， ^1-2388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2389]]

謂厭壞對治、 ^1-2389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2390]]

斷對治、 ^1-2390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2391]]

持對治、 ^1-2391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2392]]

遠分對治。 ^1-2392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2394]]

厭壞對治者，於有漏諸行見多過患， ^1-2394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2395]]

謂以如病如癰等行厭壞五取蘊故。 ^1-2395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2396]]

斷對治者，謂方便及無間道， ^1-2396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2397]]

由彼能斷諸煩惱故。 ^1-2397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2398]]

持對治者，謂解脫道， ^1-2398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2399]]

由彼任持斷得故。 ^1-2399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2400]]

遠分對治者，謂此後諸道， ^1-2400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2401]]

由彼令先所斷煩惱轉遠離故。 ^1-2401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2402]]

如是四種對治差別，是前對治修差別義。 ^1-2402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2403]]

復次道差別有十一種， ^1-2403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2404]]

謂觀察事道、 ^1-2404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2405]]

勤功用道、 ^1-2405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2406]]

修治定道、 ^1-2406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2407]]

現觀方便道、 ^1-2407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2408]]

親近現觀道、 ^1-2408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2409]]

現觀道、 ^1-2409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2410]]

清淨出離道、 ^1-2410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2411]]

依根差別道、 ^1-2411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2412]]

淨修三學道、 ^1-2412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2413]]

發諸功德道、 ^1-2413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2414]]

遍攝諸道道。 ^1-2414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2415]]

當知此中由覺分等差別故，建立十一種道， ^1-2415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2416]]

如其次第，謂三十七菩提分法、 ^1-2416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2417]]

四種正行、 ^1-2417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2418]]

四種法迹、 ^1-2418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2419]]

奢摩他、 ^1-2419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2420]]

毘鉢舍那、 ^1-2420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2421]]

三無漏根。 ^1-2421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2422]]

觀察事道者，謂四念住， ^1-2422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2423]]

由此最初以不淨等行觀察一切身受心法事故。 ^1-2423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2424]]

勤功用道者，謂四正斷， ^1-2424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2425]]

由遍觀察一切事已，為斷諸障發勤精進故。 ^1-2425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2426]]

修治定道者，謂四神足。 ^1-2426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2427]]

如是淨除一切障已，復由欲勤心觀門修三摩地，令成調順堪任性故。 ^1-2427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2428]]

現觀方便道者，謂信等五根。 ^1-2428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2429]]

如是修治三摩地已，為欲證得無漏聖道，勤修增上緣煖頂方便故。 ^1-2429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2430]]

親近現觀道者，謂信等五力。 ^1-2430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2431]]

如是已得增上緣者，為欲無間通達諦理， ^1-2431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2432]]

修習摧伏不信等障，忍第一法近方便故。 ^1-2432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2433]]

現觀道者，謂七覺支，由此最初各別內證覺真理故。 ^1-2433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2434]]

清淨出離道者，謂聖八支道， ^1-2434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2435]]

由從此後為令修道所斷煩惱永得清淨修出離道故。 ^1-2435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2436]]

由此道理，菩提分法如是次第。 ^1-2436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2437]]

依根差別道者，謂四正行。 ^1-2437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2438]]

由依近分根本等地差別及利鈍根差別故。 ^1-2438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2439]]

苦正行者，依止未至及無色定，如其次第，止觀劣故。樂正行者，依止根本靜慮，雙道轉故。二遲通者，謂鈍根依苦樂。二速通者，謂利根依苦樂。 ^1-2439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2440]]

淨修三學道者，謂四法迹，由此淨修增上戒等三學故。 ^1-2440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2441]]

無貪無恚，能淨修治增上戒學，終不於貪欲瞋恚門毀犯所學處故。正念能淨修治增上心學，由不忘所緣持心令定故。正定能淨修治增上慧學，由心得定能證如實智故。 ^1-2441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2442]]

發諸功德道者，謂奢摩他、毘鉢舍那，由此能成辦一切功德故。 ^1-2442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2443]]

攝諸道道者，謂三無漏根，由此能攝初中究竟一切道故。 ^1-2443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2444]]

未知欲知根，攝方便道及見道。已知根，攝修道。具知根，攝究竟道。 ^1-2444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2445]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第九大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十安慧菩薩糅大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中諦品第一之五 ^1-2445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2446]]

復次一切菩提分法無有差別，皆由五門而得建立，謂 ^1-2446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2447]]

所緣故、 ^1-2447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2448]]

自體故、助伴故、 ^1-2448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2449]]

修習故、 ^1-2449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2450]]

修果故。 ^1-2450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2451]]

如初四念住有五門，所餘菩提分法亦爾。 ^1-2451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2452]]

四念住所緣境者，謂身、受、心、法。 ^1-2452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2453]]

復有四事，謂我所依事、我受用事、我自體事、我染淨事。 ^1-2453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2454]]

何故唯建立此為所緣境？ ^1-2454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2455]]

由顛倒覺愚癡凡夫多分計我，依止有根身， ^1-2455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2456]]

受用苦樂等，取了境為相，由貪等染污，由信等清淨。 ^1-2456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2457]]

是故最初為正觀察真實事相，是故建立此四種事為所緣境。 ^1-2457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2458]]

念住自體者，謂慧及念。 ^1-2458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2459]]

由佛經中有於身等循觀言故，及有念住言故，如其次第。 ^1-2459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2460]]

念住助伴者，謂彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2461]]

彼者，彼念慧二法。 ^1-2461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2462]]

念住修習者，謂於內身等修循身等觀。 ^1-2462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2463]]

如於內，於外、於內外亦爾。 ^1-2463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2464]]

內身者，謂於此身中所有內色處。 ^1-2464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2465]]

由自身中眼耳鼻舌身根內處所攝故、墮有情數故名內。 ^1-2465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2466]]

外身者，謂外所有外色處。 ^1-2466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2467]]

由外色聲香味觸等外處所攝故、非有情數故名外。 ^1-2467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2468]]

內外身者，謂內處相應所有外處根所依止。 ^1-2468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2469]]

由己身中眼等五處相應根所依住所有色等外處，墮有情數故、外處所攝故名內外。 ^1-2469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2470]]

又於他身中所有內色處，約處建立、約身建立，說名內外。 ^1-2470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2471]]

云何於身修循身觀？ ^1-2471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2472]]

謂以分別影像身與本質身平等循觀，於身境循觀身相似性故，名於身循身觀。 ^1-2472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2473]]

由循觀察分別影像身門，審諦觀察本質身故。 ^1-2473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2474]]

內受者，謂因內身所生受。 ^1-2474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2475]]

緣眼等處為境界故，依自身生故名內。 ^1-2475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2476]]

外受者，謂因外身所生受。 ^1-2476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2477]]

緣色等處為境界故，依他身生故名外。 ^1-2477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2478]]

內外受者，謂因內外身所生受。 ^1-2478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2479]]

緣自身中外處為境故，緣他身中內處為境故名內外。 ^1-2479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2480]]

如受，心法亦爾。 ^1-2480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2481]]

如於身修循身觀，如是於受等修循受等觀，如其所應。 ^1-2481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2482]]

又修習者，謂欲、勤、策、勵、勇猛、不息、正念、正知及不放逸，修習差別故。 ^1-2482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2483]]

欲修習者，謂為對治不作意隨煩惱。 ^1-2483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2484]]

勤修習者，謂為對治懈怠隨煩惱。 ^1-2484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2485]]

策修習者，謂為對治沈掉隨煩惱。 ^1-2485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2486]]

勵修習者，謂為對治心下劣性隨煩惱。 ^1-2486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2487]]

心下劣性者，謂於勝品所證功德，由自輕蔑門心生怯弱性。 ^1-2487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2488]]

勇猛修習者，謂為對治踈漏疲倦隨煩惱。 ^1-2488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2489]]

踈漏疲倦者，謂能引蚊虻等處所生逼惱。 ^1-2489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2490]]

不息修習者，謂為對治得少善法生知足喜隨煩惱。 ^1-2490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2491]]

由得少善生知足喜故，止息所餘勝進善品。 ^1-2491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2492]]

正念修習者，謂為對治忘失尊教隨煩惱。 ^1-2492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2493]]

正知修習者，謂為對治毀犯追悔隨煩惱。 ^1-2493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2494]]

毀犯追悔者，謂於往來等事不正知而行，先越學處後生悔惱。 ^1-2494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2495]]

不放逸修習者，謂為對治捨諸善軛隨煩惱。 ^1-2495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2496]]

捨善軛者，由放逸過失故，於所造修勝進善品捨勤方便不能究竟。 ^1-2496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2497]]

念住修果者，謂斷四顛倒、趣入四諦、身等離繫，是名修果。 ^1-2497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2498]]

斷四顛倒者，謂四念住隨其次第能斷淨樂常我四種顛倒，修不淨觀故、 ^1-2498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2499]]

了知諸受皆是苦故、通達諸識依緣差別念念變異故、 ^1-2499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2500]]

觀察染淨唯有諸法無作用者故。 ^1-2500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2501]]

又此四種如其次第趣入四諦，亦名修果。 ^1-2501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2502]]

由身念住趣入苦諦， ^1-2502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2503]]

所有色身皆行苦相，麁重所顯故。 ^1-2503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2504]]

是故修觀行時能治，此輕安於身差別生故。 ^1-2504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2505]]

由受念住趣入集諦，以樂等諸受是和合愛等所依處故。 ^1-2505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2506]]

由心念住趣入滅諦， ^1-2506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2507]]

觀離我識當無所有，懼我斷門生涅槃怖永遠離故。 ^1-2507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2508]]

由法念住趣入道諦， ^1-2508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2509]]

為斷所治法為修能治法故。 ^1-2509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2510]]

又此四種，如其次第能證得身受心法離繫果，由此修習漸能遠離身等麁重故。 ^1-2510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2511]]

四正斷所緣境者，謂已生未生、所治能治法。 ^1-2511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2512]]

初正斷緣已生所治法為境，為斷已生惡不善法，樂欲生故。 ^1-2512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2513]]

第二正斷緣未生所治法為境， ^1-2513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2514]]

第三正斷緣未生能治法為境， ^1-2514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2515]]

第四正斷緣已生能治法為境。 ^1-2515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2516]]

如經所說，應廣配釋。 ^1-2516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2517]]

正斷自體者，謂精進。 ^1-2517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2518]]

正斷助伴者，謂彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2519]]

正斷修習者，如經說生欲策勵，發起正勤策心持心。 ^1-2519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2520]]

此中諸句顯修正勤及所依止。 ^1-2520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2521]]

所依止者，謂欲，樂欲為先，發精進故。 ^1-2521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2522]]

正勤者，謂策勵等。 ^1-2522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2523]]

於止舉捨相作意等中，若由止等相作意，不顧戀所緣境，純修習對治，爾時名策勵。為欲損減沈沒掉舉發起正勤。 ^1-2523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2524]]

所以者何？ ^1-2524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2525]]

若沈沒隨煩惱生時，為損減彼故，以淨妙等作意策練其心。若掉舉隨煩惱生時， ^1-2525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2526]]

即以內證略攝門制持其心。 爾時名為發起正勤。 ^1-2526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2527]]

即為顯此損減沈掉善巧方便，故次說言策心持心。 ^1-2527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2528]]

正斷修果者，謂盡棄捨一切所治，於能對治若得若增，是名修果。 ^1-2528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2529]]

初二正斷盡捨一切所治，如其所應，斷捨一切已生未生惡不善法故。 ^1-2529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2530]]

第三正斷得能對治，能生未生諸善法故。 ^1-2530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2531]]

第四正斷增能對治，已生善法令增廣故。 ^1-2531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2532]]

四神足所緣境者，謂已成滿定所作事。 ^1-2532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2533]]

此復云何？ ^1-2533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2534]]

由已成滿三摩地力，發起種種神變等事，是所緣境。 ^1-2534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2535]]

神足自體者，謂三摩地。 ^1-2535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2536]]

神足助伴者，謂欲勤心觀及彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2537]]

欲三摩地者，謂由殷重方便證心一境性。 ^1-2537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2538]]

殷重方便者，謂由猛利樂欲、猛利恭敬方便得三摩地。 ^1-2538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2539]]

勤三摩地者，謂由無間方便觸心一境性。 ^1-2539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2540]]

勤者，謂常精進無時暫間。 ^1-2540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2541]]

心三摩地者，謂由先修定力觸心一境性。 ^1-2541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2542]]

所以者何？ ^1-2542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2543]]

由於前生數修定力，令彼種子功能增長，由種子力令心任運於三摩地隨順轉變，由此速證心一境性。 ^1-2543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2544]]

觀三摩地者，謂由聞他教法內自簡擇觸心一境性。 ^1-2544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2545]]

又欲三摩地者，謂由生欲觸心一境性。 ^1-2545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2546]]

勤三摩地者，謂由策勵發起正勤觸心一境性。 ^1-2546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2547]]

心三摩地者，謂由持心觸心一境性。 ^1-2547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2548]]

觀三摩地者，謂由策心觸心一境性。 ^1-2548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2549]]

為顯發生神足因性故， ^1-2549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2550]]

引修正斷中生欲策勵等諸句持心策心，是此次第。 ^1-2550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2551]]

心三摩地者，由持心故得定持心，於內寂靜略攝速證定故。 ^1-2551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2552]]

觀三摩地者，由策心故得定，依法觀門策練其心速得定故。 ^1-2552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2553]]

神足修習者，謂數修習八種斷行。 ^1-2553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2554]]

何等為八？謂欲、精進、信、安、正念、正知、思、捨。 ^1-2554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2555]]

如是八種略攝為四，謂加行、攝受、繼屬、對治。 ^1-2555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2556]]

加行者，謂欲、精進、信。 ^1-2556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2557]]

欲為精進依，信為欲因。 ^1-2557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2558]]

所以者何？ ^1-2558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2559]]

由欲求故為得此義發勤精進，如是欲求不離信受，有體等故。 ^1-2559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2560]]

攝受者，謂安，由此輕安攝益身心故。 ^1-2560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2561]]

繼屬者，謂正念、正知，由不忘所緣安心一境故。若有放逸生，如實了知故，隨其次第。 ^1-2561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2562]]

對治者，謂思、捨。 ^1-2562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2563]]

策心持心二加行力已生，沈掉能遠離故，又能引發離隨煩惱止等相故。 ^1-2563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2564]]

復次欲勤心觀修有二種，謂并因緣聚散遠離修不劣不散，彼二所依隨順修，此中顯示欲等能遠離聚散及因緣等二種修義。 ^1-2564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2565]]

聚因緣者，謂遠離毘鉢舍那故，由懈怠門所生沈沒。 ^1-2565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2566]]

散因緣者，謂遠離不淨想故，由掉動門所生高舉。 ^1-2566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2567]]

聚者，謂由惛沈睡眠門，於內踧踖。 ^1-2567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2568]]

散者，謂由隨順淨妙相門，於外馳散。 ^1-2568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2569]]

不劣隨順修者，謂依觀察相觀察諸法。 ^1-2569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2570]]

不散隨順修者，謂依不淨想觀察髮毛等事。 ^1-2570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2571]]

彼二所依隨順修者，謂修光明想。 ^1-2571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2572]]

依如是次第，薄伽梵說：我之欲樂無有下劣亦無高舉，於內不聚於外不散，有前後想及上下想，開發其心遠離纏縛，與光明俱。自修其心，當令我心無諸闇蔽。 ^1-2572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2573]]

神足修果者，謂已善修治三摩地故， ^1-2573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2574]]

隨所欲證所通達法，即能隨心通達變現。 ^1-2574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2575]]

又於別別處所法中，證得堪能自在作用， ^1-2575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2576]]

如所願樂能辦種種神通等事，又能引發勝品功德。 ^1-2576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2577]]

五根所緣境者，謂四聖諦。 ^1-2577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2578]]

由諦現觀方便所攝，作此行故。 ^1-2578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2579]]

五根自體者，謂信、精進、念、定、慧。 ^1-2579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2580]]

五根助伴者，謂彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2581]]

五根修習者，謂信根於諸諦起忍可行修習。 ^1-2581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2582]]

精進根於諸諦生忍可已為覺悟故，起精進行修習。 ^1-2582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2583]]

念根於諸諦發精進已，起不忘失行修習。 ^1-2583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2584]]

定根於諸諦既繫念已，起心一境性行修習。 ^1-2584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2585]]

慧根於諸諦心既得定，起簡擇行修習。 ^1-2585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2586]]

五根修果者，謂能速發諦現觀， ^1-2586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2587]]

由此增上力不久能生見道故。 ^1-2587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2588]]

又能修治煖、頂，引發忍、世第一法， ^1-2588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2589]]

即現此身已入順決擇分位故。 ^1-2589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2590]]

如五根，五力亦爾。 ^1-2590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2591]]

差別者，由此能損減所對治障，不可屈伏，故名為力。 ^1-2591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2592]]

謂五力所緣境等與根相似， ^1-2592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2593]]

然果有差別。 ^1-2593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2594]]

所以者何？如說果者，謂能損減不信等障故，勝過於前。 ^1-2594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2595]]

雖與五根所緣境界自體等相似，然不可屈伏義有差別故，別立力分。 ^1-2595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2596]]

七覺支所緣境者，謂四聖諦如實性。 ^1-2596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2597]]

如實性者，即是勝義清淨所緣故。 ^1-2597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2598]]

覺支自體者，謂念、擇法、精進、喜、安、定、捨。 ^1-2598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2599]]

如是七法是覺支自體。 ^1-2599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2600]]

念者是所依支，由繫念故，令諸善法皆不忘失。 ^1-2600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2601]]

擇法者是自體支， ^1-2601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2602]]

是覺自相故。 ^1-2602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2603]]

精進者是出離支， ^1-2603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2604]]

由此勢力能到所到故。 ^1-2604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2605]]

喜者是利益支， ^1-2605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2606]]

由此勢力身調適故。 ^1-2606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2607]]

安、定、捨者是不染污支， ^1-2607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2608]]

由此不染污故、依此不染污故、體是不染污故。 ^1-2608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2609]]

如其次第，由安故不染污，由此能除麁重過故。 ^1-2609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2610]]

依定故不染污，依止於定得轉依故。 ^1-2610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2611]]

捨是不染污體， ^1-2611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2612]]

永除貪憂，不染污位為自性故。 ^1-2612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2613]]

覺支助伴者，謂彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2614]]

覺支修習者，謂依止遠離、 ^1-2614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2615]]

依止無欲、依止寂滅， ^1-2615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2616]]

迴向棄捨修念覺支。 ^1-2616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2617]]

如念覺支，乃至捨覺支亦爾。 ^1-2617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2618]]

如是四句，隨其次第顯示緣四諦境修習覺支。所以者何？ ^1-2618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2619]]

若緣苦體為惱苦時，於苦境界必求遠離故，名依止遠離。 ^1-2619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2620]]

若緣愛相苦集為苦集時，於此境界必求離欲故，名依止離欲。 ^1-2620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2621]]

若緣苦滅為苦滅時，於此境界必求作證故，名依止寂滅。 ^1-2621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2622]]

棄捨者，謂趣苦滅行，由此勢力棄捨苦故。 ^1-2622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2623]]

是故若緣此境時，於此境界必求修習故，名迴向棄捨。 ^1-2623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2624]]

覺支修果者，謂見道所斷煩惱永斷， ^1-2624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2625]]

由七覺支是見道自體故。 ^1-2625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2626]]

八聖道支所緣境者，謂即此後時四聖諦如實性。 ^1-2626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2627]]

由見道後所緣境界，即先所見諸諦如實性為體故。 ^1-2627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2628]]

道支自體者，謂正見、 ^1-2628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2629]]

正思、 ^1-2629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2630]]

惟正語、 ^1-2630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2631]]

正業、 ^1-2631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2632]]

正命、 ^1-2632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2633]]

正精進、 ^1-2633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2634]]

正念、 ^1-2634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2635]]

正定， ^1-2635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2636]]

如是八法名道支自體。 ^1-2636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2637]]

正見者，是分別支， ^1-2637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2638]]

如先所證真實簡擇故。 ^1-2638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2639]]

正思惟者，是誨示他支， ^1-2639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2640]]

如其所證方便安立發語言故。 ^1-2640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2641]]

正語、正業、正命者，是令他信支， ^1-2641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2642]]

如其次第令他於證理者決定信有見戒正命清淨性故。 ^1-2642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2643]]

所以者何？ ^1-2643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2644]]

由正語故，隨自所證，善能問答論議決擇，由此了知有見清淨。 ^1-2644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2645]]

由正業故，往來進止正行具足，由此了知有戒清淨。 ^1-2645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2646]]

由正命故，如法乞求佛所聽許衣鉢資具，由此了知有命清淨。 ^1-2646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2647]]

正精進者，是淨煩惱障支，由此永斷一切結故。 ^1-2647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2648]]

正念者，是淨隨煩惱障支， ^1-2648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2649]]

由此不忘失正止舉相等，永不容受沈掉等隨煩惱故。 ^1-2649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2650]]

正定者，是能淨最勝功德障支， ^1-2650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2651]]

由此引發神通等無量勝功德故。 ^1-2651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2652]]

道支助伴者，謂彼相應心心法等。 ^1-2652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2653]]

道支修習者，如覺支說，謂依止遠離、依止無欲、依止寂滅，迴向棄捨修習正見，乃至廣說。如是諸句義，如前所說道理應隨順知。 ^1-2653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2654]]

道支修果者，謂分別誨示他，令他信煩惱障淨、隨煩惱障淨、最勝功德障淨故。 ^1-2654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2655]]

四正行者，謂 ^1-2655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2656]]

苦遲通行、 ^1-2656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2657]]

苦速通行、 ^1-2657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2658]]

樂遲通行、 ^1-2658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2659]]

樂速通行。 ^1-2659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2660]]

初謂鈍根未得根本靜慮， ^1-2660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2661]]

第二謂利根未得根本靜慮， ^1-2661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2662]]

第三謂鈍根已得根本靜慮， ^1-2662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2663]]

第四謂利根已得根本靜慮。 ^1-2663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2664]]

苦正行者，謂依未至及無色地， ^1-2664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2665]]

如其次第，奢摩他、毘鉢舍那微劣故。 ^1-2665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2666]]

樂正行者，謂依靜慮雙道轉故。 ^1-2666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2667]]

遲通者，謂鈍根依苦樂二地。 ^1-2667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2668]]

速通者，謂利根依苦樂二地。 ^1-2668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2669]]

四法迹者，謂無貪、無瞋、正念、正定。 ^1-2669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2670]]

無貪無瞋能令增上戒學清淨， ^1-2670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2671]]

不因貪恚門毀犯學處故。 ^1-2671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2672]]

正念能令增上心學清淨， ^1-2672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2673]]

由於所緣無有忘失，持心令定故。 ^1-2673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2674]]

正定能令增上慧學清淨， ^1-2674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2675]]

由定心者能如實遍知故。 ^1-2675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2676]]

奢摩他者，謂 ^1-2676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2677]]

於內攝心令住、 ^1-2677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2678]]

等住、 ^1-2678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2679]]

安住、 ^1-2679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2680]]

近住、 ^1-2680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2681]]

調順、 ^1-2681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2682]]

寂靜、 ^1-2682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2683]]

最極寂靜、 ^1-2683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2684]]

專注一趣、 ^1-2684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2685]]

平等攝持。 ^1-2685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2686]]

如是九行令心安住，是奢摩他。 ^1-2686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2687]]

令住者，攝外攀緣，內離散亂，最初繫心故。 ^1-2687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2688]]

等住者，最初繫縛麁動心已，即於所緣相續繫念，微細漸略故。 ^1-2688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2689]]

安住者，或時失念於外馳散，尋復斂攝故。 ^1-2689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2690]]

近住者，從初已來為令其心於外不散，親近念住故。 ^1-2690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2691]]

調順者，從先已來於散亂因色等法中起過患想，增上力故調伏其心令不流散故。 ^1-2691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2692]]

寂靜者，於擾動心散亂惡覺隨煩惱中深見過患，攝伏其心令不流散故。 ^1-2692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2693]]

最極寂靜者，或時失念散亂，覺等率爾現行，即便制伏令不更起故。 ^1-2693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2694]]

專注一趣者，精勤加行無間無缺，相續安住勝三摩地故。 ^1-2694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2695]]

平等攝持者，善修習故，不由加行遠離功用，定心相續離散亂轉故。 ^1-2695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2696]]

毘鉢舍那者，謂 ^1-2696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2697]]

簡擇諸法、 ^1-2697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2698]]

最極簡擇、 ^1-2698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2699]]

普遍尋思、 ^1-2699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2700]]

周審觀察， ^1-2700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2701]]

為欲對治麁重相結故、為欲制伏諸顛倒故、令無倒心善安住故。 ^1-2701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2702]]

此中諸句依正行所緣境說、或依善巧所緣境說、或依淨煩惱所緣境說。 ^1-2702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2703]]

簡擇諸法者，盡所有故。 ^1-2703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2704]]

最極簡擇者，如所有故。 ^1-2704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2705]]

普遍尋思者，由有分別作意俱行慧建立諸法相故。 ^1-2705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2706]]

周審觀察者，委具推求故。 ^1-2706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2707]]

{為欲對治麁重相結故、為欲制伏諸顛倒故、令無倒心善安住故。} ^1-2707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2708]]

又依奢摩他、毘鉢舍那立四種道。 ^1-2708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2709]]

或有一類已得奢摩他非毘鉢舍那，此類依奢摩他進修毘鉢舍那。 ^1-2709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2710]]

或有一類已得毘鉢舍那非奢摩他，此類依毘鉢舍那進修奢摩他。 ^1-2710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2711]]

或有一類不得奢摩他亦非毘鉢舍那，此類專心制伏沈掉雙修二道。 ^1-2711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2712]]

或有一類已得奢摩他及毘鉢舍那，此類奢摩他、毘鉢舍那二道和合平等雙轉。 ^1-2712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2713]]

此中依於止觀說四種道。 ^1-2713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2714]]

初已得止故宴坐住心乃至平等攝持，未得觀故還復宴坐，依三摩地簡擇諸法，乃至周審觀察。 ^1-2714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2715]]

第二與此相違。 ^1-2715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2716]]

第三二俱未得，雙進修習。 ^1-2716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2717]]

云何修習？ ^1-2717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2718]]

謂聽聞法，由受持門進修正觀， ^1-2718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2719]]

以此為先進修於止。 ^1-2719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2720]]

第四已得二種，相應俱轉。 ^1-2720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2721]]

三根者，謂 ^1-2721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2722]]

未知欲知根、 ^1-2722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2723]]

已知根、 ^1-2723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2724]]

具知根。 ^1-2724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2725]]

未知欲知根者，謂於方便道及於見道十五心剎那中所有諸根。 ^1-2725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2726]]

此中顯示順決擇分所攝方便道，及見道十五剎那所有諸根，是未知欲知根體。 ^1-2726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2727]]

言諸根者，謂意根信等五根，由未至等地所依差別故。如其所應，有樂、喜、憂、捨根隨一。 ^1-2727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2728]]

憂根者，謂方便道時順決擇分後，於上解脫希求欲證，愁慼所攝。 ^1-2728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2729]]

如是十根先未知真，為欲得知，修習轉故，名未知欲知根。 ^1-2729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2730]]

已知根者，從第十六見道心剎那已上，於一切有學道中所有諸根，是已知根體。 ^1-2730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2731]]

所以者何？ ^1-2731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2732]]

即前十根從第十六見道心剎那，乃至金剛喻定，於如是有學道中，未有所應知境曾所不知故，名已知根。 ^1-2732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2733]]

具知根者，謂於無學道所有諸根。 ^1-2733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2734]]

言諸根者，即前所說九根，除憂根，於無學道中說名具知根。 ^1-2734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2735]]

具知者，謂阿羅漢等，此所有根，名具知根。 ^1-2735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2736]]

無學身中無有憂根，所應學無故。 ^1-2736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2737]]

復次依止修道分別修義， ^1-2737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2738]]

謂依初靜慮地現修道時，亦修欲界繫所有善根， ^1-2738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2739]]

於彼得自在故。 ^1-2739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2741]]

如依初靜慮地修欲界善，如是依一切上地現修道時，皆能修習下界下地所有善根， ^1-2741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2742]]

於彼得自在故。 ^1-2742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2743]]

當知此中所說義者，謂依止上地現前修習道時，下界下地所有善根雖不現前，亦皆修習。 ^1-2743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2744]]

何以故？於彼得自在故。 ^1-2744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2745]]

自在者，謂轉增勝現行自在故。 ^1-2745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2746]]

究竟道者，謂依金剛喻定，一切麁重永已息故、一切繫得永已斷故、永證一切離繫得故。 從此次第無間轉依，證得盡智及無生智十無學法。 ^1-2746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2747]]

何等為十？ ^1-2747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2748]]

所謂無學正見 ^1-2748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2749]]

乃至無學正定、 ^1-2749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2750]]

無學正解脫、 ^1-2750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2751]]

無學正智。 ^1-2751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2752]]

如是等法名究竟道。 ^1-2752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2753]]

云何名一切麁重？ ^1-2753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2754]]

略說有二十四種，謂 ^1-2754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2755]]

一切遍行戲論麁重、 ^1-2755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2756]]

領受麁重、 ^1-2756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2757]]

煩惱麁重、 ^1-2757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2758]]

業麁重、 ^1-2758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2759]]

異熟麁重、 ^1-2759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2760]]

煩惱障麁重、 ^1-2760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2761]]

業障麁重、 ^1-2761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2762]]

異熟障麁重、 ^1-2762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2763]]

蓋麁重、 ^1-2763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2764]]

尋思麁重、 ^1-2764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2765]]

飲食麁重、 ^1-2765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2766]]

交會麁重、 ^1-2766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2767]]

夢麁重、 ^1-2767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2768]]

病麁重、 ^1-2768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2769]]

老麁重、 ^1-2769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2770]]

死麁重、 ^1-2770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2771]]

勞倦麁重、 ^1-2771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2772]]

堅固麁重、 ^1-2772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2773]]

麁麁重、 ^1-2773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2774]]

中麁重、 ^1-2774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2775]]

細麁重、 ^1-2775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2776]]

煩惱障麁重、 ^1-2776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2777]]

定障麁重、 ^1-2777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2778]]

所知障麁重。 ^1-2778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2779]]

如是二十四種，略攝一切麁重。 ^1-2779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2780]]

一切遍行戲論麁重者，謂執眼等諸法習氣，無始時來依附阿賴耶識相續不斷，即此名為戲論習氣。 ^1-2780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2781]]

從此習氣，眼等諸法及名言執數數生起。 ^1-2781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2782]]

領受麁重者，謂有漏諸受習氣。 ^1-2782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2783]]

煩惱麁重者，謂煩惱隨眠。 ^1-2783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2784]]

業麁重者，謂有漏業習氣。 ^1-2784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2785]]

異熟麁重者，謂異熟無堪能性。 ^1-2785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2786]]

煩惱障麁重者，謂猛利長時煩惱性。 ^1-2786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2787]]

業障麁重者，謂能障道無間等業障性。 ^1-2787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2788]]

異熟障麁重者，謂與諦現觀相違，地獄等自體得。 ^1-2788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2789]]

蓋麁重者，謂能障礙善品方便盛貪欲等性。 ^1-2789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2790]]

尋思麁重者，謂能障礙欣樂出家欲尋思等性。 ^1-2790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2791]]

飲食麁重者，謂極多少食於方便行無堪任性。 ^1-2791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2792]]

交會麁重者，謂兩兩形交身心疲損性。 ^1-2792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2793]]

夢麁重者，謂睡眠所發身惛劣性。 ^1-2793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2794]]

病麁重者，謂諸界互違所發不安隱性。 ^1-2794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2795]]

老麁重者，謂大種衰變所起不隨轉性。 ^1-2795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2796]]

死麁重者，謂臨命終時諸根亂性。 ^1-2796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2797]]

勞倦麁重者，謂遠行等所作支體頓弊性。 ^1-2797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2798]]

堅固麁重者，謂無涅槃法者，如其所應所有戲論麁重等性。 ^1-2798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2799]]

麁、中、細麁重者，謂欲色無色所有麁重，如其次第。 ^1-2799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2800]]

煩惱障麁重者，謂聲聞獨覺菩提所治。 ^1-2800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2801]]

定障麁重者，謂九次第定所發功德所治。 ^1-2801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2802]]

所知障麁重者，謂一切智性所治。 ^1-2802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2803]]

如是隨其所應，一切麁重永已息故，名究竟道。 ^1-2803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2804]]

如說如是行者，心解脫圓滿、慧解脫圓滿，身麁重永息， ^1-2804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2805]]

由成就念為因故。於最初門善調善護、善防善覆、極善修治，謂於眼所識色，乃至於意所識法亦爾。 ^1-2805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2806]]

繫得者，謂於麁重積集，假立繫得性。 ^1-2806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2807]]

離繫得者，謂於麁重離散，假立離繫得性。 ^1-2807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2808]]

金剛喻定者，謂居修道最後斷結道位所有三摩地。 ^1-2808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2809]]

此復略有二種，謂 ^1-2809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2810]]

方便道攝、 ^1-2810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2811]]

無間道攝。 ^1-2811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2812]]

方便道攝者，謂從此已去，不為一切障所礙，而能破一切障。 ^1-2812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2813]]

無間道攝者，謂從此無間盡智、無生智生。 ^1-2813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2814]]

又此三摩地，無間、堅固、一味、遍滿。 ^1-2814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2815]]

云何此金剛喻定名無間、 ^1-2815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2816]]

謂此相續流非世間行所間缺故。 ^1-2816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2817]]

堅固者，非一切障所壞，能壞一切障，極堅猛故。 ^1-2817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2818]]

一味者，無分別性，純一味故。 ^1-2818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2819]]

遍滿者，緣一切所知法共相真如為境故。 ^1-2819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2820]]

為顯此義，薄伽梵說：如大石山無缺無隙無穴一段，極善圓滿，十方猛風所不動轉。 ^1-2820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2821]]

無間轉依者，謂已證得無學道者所有三種轉依。何等為三？ ^1-2821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2822]]

謂心轉依、 ^1-2822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2823]]

道轉依、 ^1-2823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2824]]

麁重轉依。 ^1-2824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2825]]

心轉依者，謂已得無學道，證得法性心自性清淨，永離一切客塵隨煩惱故，名為轉依，即是真如轉依義。 ^1-2825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2826]]

道轉依者，謂昔世間道於現觀時，轉成出世，說名有學，餘有所作故。 ^1-2826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2827]]

若永除一切所治、永離三界欲時，此道自體究竟圓滿，立為轉依。 ^1-2827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2828]]

麁重轉依者，謂阿賴耶識一切煩惱隨眠永遠離故，名為轉依。 ^1-2828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2829]]

盡智者，謂由因盡所得智，或緣盡為境。 ^1-2829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2830]]

所以者何？ ^1-2830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2831]]

由有盡故而起此智，名為盡智。 或緣盡為境，故名盡智。 ^1-2831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2832]]

此義意言，於此位中由永斷集令無有餘，所得智名盡智。或緣因盡為境，故名盡智。 ^1-2832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2833]]

無生智者，謂由果斷所得智，或緣果不生為境。 ^1-2833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2834]]

所以者何？ ^1-2834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2835]]

由有無生故，所得智名無生智。或緣無生為境，名無生智。 ^1-2835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2836]]

此義意言，由有當來一切苦果畢竟不生法性故而得此智，雖緣餘諦為境，亦名無生智。 ^1-2836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2837]]

或緣苦諦無生為境故，名無生智。 ^1-2837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2838]]

又十無學法， ^1-2838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2839]]

當知依止無學戒蘊、 ^1-2839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2840]]

定蘊、 ^1-2840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2841]]

慧蘊、 ^1-2841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2842]]

解脫蘊、 ^1-2842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2843]]

解脫智見蘊說。 ^1-2843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2844]]

何以故？ ^1-2844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2845]]

無學正語、正業、正命，是無學戒蘊。 ^1-2845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2846]]

無學正念、正定，是無學定蘊。 ^1-2846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2847]]

無學正見、正思惟、正精進，是無學慧蘊。 ^1-2847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2848]]

無學正解脫，是無學解脫蘊。 ^1-2848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2849]]

無學正智，是無學解脫智見蘊。 ^1-2849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2850]]

復次道諦有四行相，謂 ^1-2850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2851]]

道相、 ^1-2851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2852]]

如相、 ^1-2852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2853]]

行相、 ^1-2853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2854]]

出相。 ^1-2854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2855]]

何故名道相？ ^1-2855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2856]]

因此尋求真實義故。 ^1-2856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2857]]

所以者何？由此聖道是諸聖者證真義路，是故名道。 ^1-2857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2858]]

何故名如相？ ^1-2858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2859]]

以能對治諸煩惱故。 ^1-2859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2860]]

所以者何？一切煩惱皆不如理，道能除此，是故名如。 ^1-2860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2861]]

何故名行相？ ^1-2861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2862]]

善能成辦心，令不顛倒故。 ^1-2862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2863]]

所以者何？心不覺悟真實道理，於無常等法起常等顛倒。善能修治此顛倒心，令離顛倒覺真實義，是故名行。 ^1-2863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2864]]

何故名出相？ ^1-2864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2865]]

趣真常迹故。 ^1-2865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2866]]

所以者何？由此聖道能趣出離究竟常迹，是故名出。 ^1-2866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2867]]

問：於諸諦中有十六行，皆通世間及出世間。 ^1-2867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2868]]

世出世行有何差別？ ^1-2868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2869]]

答：於所知境不善悟入、善悟入性差別故， ^1-2869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2870]]

有障、無障性差別故， ^1-2870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2871]]

有分別、無分別性差別故。 ^1-2871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2872]]

所以者何？ ^1-2872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2873]]

於諸諦中無常苦等十六，世間行於所知境界，不通達真如性故、煩惱所隨眠故、依名言門起戲論故，如其次第不善悟入、有障礙、有分別。 ^1-2873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2874]]

出世間行與此相違，善悟入、無障礙、無分別。由此道理，世出世行互有差別。 ^1-2874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2875]]

云何出世行無有分別，而善悟入所知境界？ ^1-2875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2878]]

由彼諸行現在前時，雖復現證見無常義，然不依名言戲論門見此是無常義。如無常行於無常義，餘行於餘義隨其所應亦如是。 ^1-2878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^1-2880]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十 ^1-2880

## 阿毘達磨雜集論 — 2/4 ^2-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1]]

{云何法決擇？} ^2-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-2]]

{一契經、二應頌、三記別、四諷頌、五自說、六緣起、七譬喻、八本事、九本生、十方廣、十一希法、十二論議。} ^2-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-3]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十一 ^2-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-4]]

安慧菩薩糅釋上集論大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯 ^2-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-5]]

決擇分中法品第二之一 ^2-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-6]]

云何法決擇？ ^2-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-7]]

法者，謂十二分聖教。 ^2-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-8]]

{契經者，謂以長行綴緝略說所應說義。} ^2-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-9]]

何者十二？ ^2-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-10]]

一契經、二應頌、三記別、四諷頌、五自說、六緣起、七譬喻、八本事、九本生、十方廣、十一希法、十二論議。 ^2-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-11]]

契經者，謂以長行綴緝略說所應說義。 ^2-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-12]]

問：何故如來不廣開演所應說義耶？ ^2-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-13]]

答：如來觀察十種勝利， 略說諸法， ^2-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-14]]

謂易可建立、 ^2-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-15]]

易可宣說、 ^2-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-16]]

易可受持、 ^2-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-17]]

恭敬法故、 ^2-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-18]]

菩提資糧速得圓滿、 ^2-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-19]]

速能通達諸法實性、 ^2-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-20]]

於諸佛所得證淨信、 ^2-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-21]]

於法僧所得證淨信、 ^2-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-22]]

觸證第一現法樂住、 ^2-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-23]]

談論決擇悅智者心、 ^2-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-24]]

得預聰明英叡者數。 ^2-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-25]]

云何名為易可建立？ ^2-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-26]]

諸說法者以無量門安立開示所應說義，今以略言，易建立故。 ^2-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-27]]

云何名為易可宣說？ ^2-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-28]]

能以少言詞，廣顯大義故。 ^2-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-29]]

如說能令心住等住，如是廣說。 ^2-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-30]]

易可受持者，令能聞者易受持故、恭敬法故。 ^2-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-31]]

菩提資糧速得圓滿者，了知佛法深慧所證， ^2-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-32]]

即於是法深生敬愛，由敬愛門，信等資糧速圓滿故。 ^2-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-33]]

速能通達諸法實性者，即由敬法方便力故，令其智慧轉復猛利， ^2-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-34]]

漸能通達諸法實性。 ^2-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-35]]

於三寶所得證淨信者， ^2-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-36]]

由悟聖教妙善建立， ^2-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-37]]

於說者等淨信生故。 ^2-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-38]]

觸證第一現法樂住者， ^2-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-39]]

於諸如來密意深義， ^2-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-40]]

猛利加行正思量已，獲得增上證歡喜故。 ^2-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-41]]

談論決擇悅智者心者， ^2-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-42]]

善能開發深隱義故。 ^2-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-43]]

得預聰明英叡者數者，廣大美稱流布十方故。 ^2-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-44]]

{漸能通達諸法實性。} ^2-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-45]]

當知後二種，合為一勝利。 ^2-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-46]]

應頌者，即諸經中，或中或後以頌重頌。 ^2-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-47]]

又不了義經應更頌釋，故名應頌。 ^2-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-48]]

記別者，謂於是處聖弟子等謝往過去，記別德失、生處差別。 ^2-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-49]]

又了義經說名記別，記別開示深密意故。 ^2-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-50]]

諷頌者，謂諸經中以句宣說， ^2-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-51]]

或以二句，或三或四，或五或六。 ^2-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-52]]

自說者，謂諸經中，或時如來悅意自說，如伽他曰：若於如是法，發勇猛精進，靜慮諦思惟，爾時名梵志。 ^2-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-53]]

緣起者，謂因請而說。 ^2-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-55]]

隨依如是補特伽羅，起如是說故。 ^2-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-56]]

又有因緣制立學處，亦名緣起。 ^2-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-57]]

謂依如是因緣、依如是事，乃至廣說。 ^2-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-58]]

譬喻者，謂諸經中有比況說， ^2-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-59]]

為令本義得明了故，說諸譬喻。 ^2-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-60]]

本事者，所謂宣說聖弟子等前世相應事。 ^2-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-61]]

本生者，所謂宣說諸菩薩行本相應事。 ^2-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-62]]

方廣者，謂菩薩藏相應言說。 ^2-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-63]]

如名方廣，亦名廣破，亦名無比。 ^2-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-64]]

為何義故名為方廣？ ^2-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-65]]

一切有情利益安樂所依處故、宣說廣大甚深法故。 ^2-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-66]]

為何義故名為廣破？ ^2-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-67]]

以能廣破一切障故。 ^2-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-68]]

為何義故名為無比？ ^2-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-69]]

無有諸法能比類故。 ^2-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-70]]

此方廣等皆是大乘義差別名。 ^2-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-71]]

由與七種大性相應，故名大乘。 ^2-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-72]]

何等名為七種大性？ ^2-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-74]]

一境大性，以菩薩道緣百千等，無量諸經廣大教法，為境界故。 ^2-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-75]]

二行大性，正行一切自利利他廣大行故。 ^2-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-76]]

三智大性，了知廣大補特伽羅法無我故。 ^2-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-77]]

四精進大性，於三大劫阿僧企耶，方便勤修無量百千難行行故。 ^2-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-78]]

五方便善巧大性，不住生死及涅槃故。 ^2-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-79]]

六證得大性，證得如來諸力、無畏、不共佛法等無量無數大功德故。 ^2-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-80]]

七業大性，窮生死際示現一切成菩提等，建立廣大諸佛事故。 ^2-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-81]]

希法者，若於是處宣說聲聞、諸大菩薩及如來等，最極希有甚奇特法。 ^2-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-82]]

論議者，若於是處，無有顛倒解釋一切深隱法相。 ^2-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-83]]

以無顛倒一切法相，論議經等深隱義故。 ^2-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-84]]

如是契經等十二分聖教，三藏所攝。 ^2-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-85]]

何等為三？ ^2-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-86]]

一素怛纜藏、二毘奈耶藏、三阿毘達磨藏。 ^2-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-87]]

此復有二：一聲聞藏、二菩薩藏。 ^2-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-88]]

契經、應頌、記別、諷頌、自說此五，聲聞藏中素怛纜藏攝。 ^2-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-89]]

緣起、譬喻、本事、本生此四，二藏中毘奈耶藏并眷屬攝。 ^2-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-90]]

緣起者，宣說有因緣建立諸學處，是正毘奈耶藏攝， ^2-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-91]]
