譬喻等三是彼眷屬攝。 ^2-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-92]]

方廣、希法此二，菩薩藏中素怛纜藏攝。 ^2-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-93]]

方廣者，文義廣博， ^2-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-94]]

正菩薩藏攝。 ^2-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-95]]

希法，差別難思廣大威德最勝相應，是故亦是菩薩藏攝。 ^2-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-96]]

論議一種，聲聞、菩薩二藏中阿毘達磨藏攝。 ^2-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-97]]

問：何故如來建立三藏？ ^2-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-98]]

答：為欲對治疑隨煩惱故，建立素怛纜藏。 ^2-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-99]]

為欲斷除所化有情，於種種法發起疑惑，宣說契經應頌等故。 ^2-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-100]]

為欲對治受用二邊隨煩惱故，建立毘柰耶藏。 ^2-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-101]]

二邊者，謂欲樂行邊、自苦行邊。 ^2-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-102]]

對治受用者， 遮彼受用蓄積等故， ^2-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-103]]

開彼受用百千如法衣服等故。 ^2-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-105]]

為欲對治自見取執隨煩惱故，建立阿毘達磨藏， ^2-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-106]]

處處廣釋諸法差別，如實相故。 ^2-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-107]]

復次為欲開示三種學故，建立素怛纜藏。 ^2-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-108]]

所以者何？ 要依此藏，所化有情解了三學， ^2-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-109]]

由此藏中，廣開三種所修學故。 ^2-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-110]]

為欲成立增上戒學、增上心學故，建立毘柰耶藏。 ^2-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-111]]

要依此藏二增上學方得成立。 ^2-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-112]]

所以者何？ ^2-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-113]]

廣釋別解脫律儀，學道聖教為所依止，方能修治淨尸羅故。 ^2-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-114]]

依淨尸羅生無悔等，漸次修學心得定故。 ^2-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-115]]

為欲成立增上慧學故，建立阿毘達磨藏。 ^2-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-116]]

要依此藏，增上慧學方得成立。 ^2-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-117]]

所以者何？ ^2-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-118]]

由此藏中，能廣開示簡擇諸法巧方便故。 ^2-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-120]]

復次為欲開示正法義故，建立素怛纜藏， ^2-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-121]]

依止此藏，文義易了故。 ^2-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-122]]

為顯法義作證安足處故，建立毘柰耶藏， ^2-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-123]]

依止此藏，能修二種作證學行故。 ^2-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-124]]

毘柰耶是法義作證安足處。 ^2-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-125]]

安足處者，是所依義。 ^2-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-126]]

為令智者論議決擇受用法樂住故，建立阿毘達磨藏。 ^2-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-127]]

依止此藏，諸有智者更相問答，論議決擇受法樂住， ^2-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-128]]

由此藏中，以無量門開示諸法自相共相等真實法性故。 ^2-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-129]]

如是三藏具有八萬四千法蘊，謂依聲聞乘尊者阿難常所受持。 ^2-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-130]]

問：一一法蘊其量云何？ ^2-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-131]]

答：十百之數是法蘊量。 ^2-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-132]]

十百者，千數義。 ^2-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-133]]

若爾，何故不直說是千數耶？ ^2-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-134]]

為顯建立一一法蘊千數因故。 ^2-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-135]]

所以者何？ ^2-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-136]]

初一數增以成十數， ^2-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-137]]

十數復增以成百數， ^2-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-138]]

千等數量，因十百數方得成立， ^2-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-139]]

謂十百名千， ^2-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-140]]

百千名百千， ^2-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-141]]

百百千名俱胝。 ^2-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-142]]

如是等一切後後數位，決定用此十百二數隨一為因， ^2-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-143]]

是故此中，唯總取十百兩數，以用建立一一法蘊。 ^2-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-144]]

由此數量，總計八萬四千法蘊，成八俱胝四十洛叉。 ^2-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-145]]

問：如是三藏所攝法，為誰所行境耶？ ^2-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-146]]

答：是聞、思、修所生，諸心心法所行境界。 ^2-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-147]]

所行者，是所緣義。 ^2-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-148]]

復次因此所緣相，依心心法建立有緣等義。 ^2-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-149]]

如經中說：諸心心法，有緣、有行、有依、相應。 ^2-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-150]]

彼於此法為何所緣？ 謂契經等。 ^2-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-151]]

此顯名身、句身、文身所攝，契經等教法為所緣境。 ^2-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-153]]

作何等行？ ^2-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-154]]

謂蘊等相應義。 此顯依蘊等義所起言教法， 彼心心法作此行相。 ^2-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-155]]

何所依止？ ^2-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-156]]

謂他表了憶念習氣。 ^2-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-157]]

此顯正說法時，用他表了為所依止， ^2-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-158]]

如說從他言音故。 ^2-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-159]]

次此後時憶念為所依止， ^2-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-160]]

如所聞已隨念數習故。 ^2-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-161]]

復此後時習氣為所依止，由後串習習氣力強，雖離憶念而似彼顯現故。 ^2-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-162]]

何等相應？ ^2-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-163]]

謂互為助伴，於所緣行平等解了。 ^2-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-164]]

由心心法互為助伴，於契經等所緣境界，以蘊等相應義行平等解了故。 ^2-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-165]]

云何於法所緣差別？ ^2-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-166]]

若略說有四種， ^2-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-167]]

謂遍滿所緣、治行所緣、善巧所緣、淨惑所緣。 ^2-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-168]]

遍滿所緣復有四種， ^2-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-170]]

謂有分別影像所緣、無分別影像所緣、事邊際所緣、所作成就所緣。 ^2-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-172]]

有分別影像所緣者，謂由勝解作意所有奢摩他、毘鉢舍那所緣境。 ^2-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-173]]

勝解作意者， ^2-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-174]]

一向世間作意。 ^2-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-175]]

無分別影像所緣者， ^2-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-176]]

謂由真實作意所有止觀所緣境。 ^2-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-177]]

真實作意者，一向出世間，及此後所得作意。 ^2-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-178]]

事邊際所緣者 ^2-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-179]]

，謂一切法盡所有性、如所有性。 ^2-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-180]]

盡所有性者，謂蘊界處。 為顯所知諸法體事唯有爾所分量邊際， ^2-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-181]]

是故建立蘊、界、處三。 ^2-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-182]]

如所有性者，謂四聖諦、十六行、真如。 ^2-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-183]]

一切行無常、 ^2-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-184]]

一切行苦、 ^2-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-185]]

一切法無我、 ^2-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-186]]

涅槃寂靜、 ^2-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-187]]

空、無願、無相， ^2-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-188]]

由如是等義差別門，了所知境故，名如所有性。 ^2-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-189]]

或以諦門了所知境，謂即前所說諸蘊界處，隨其所應，了知是苦乃至是道。 ^2-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-190]]

或以行門了所知境，謂一一諦各由四行， ^2-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-191]]

及一切法無有差別，皆真如行。 ^2-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-192]]

或以諸法鄔柁南門了所知境，謂諸行無常 ^2-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-193]]

乃至涅槃寂靜。 ^2-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-194]]

或以解脫門了所知境，謂空、無願、無相，如是等。 ^2-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-195]]

所作成就所緣者， ^2-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-196]]

謂轉依。 ^2-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-197]]

已得轉依者，無有顛倒所緣，顯現故。 ^2-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-199]]

如是轉依不可思議。 ^2-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-200]]

前說如所有性中有十六行及三解脫門，如是二種更互相攝。 ^2-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-202]]

問：空攝幾行：答：二，謂空行、無我行。 ^2-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-203]]

問：無願攝幾行？ 答：六， ^2-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-204]]

謂無常行、苦行、因行、集行、生行、緣行， ^2-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-205]]

由彼於三界無所願求故。 ^2-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-206]]

問：無相攝幾行？ 答：八， ^2-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-207]]

謂滅道八行，由彼不能行諸相故。 ^2-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-208]]

治行所緣者，略說有五種，謂多貪行者緣不淨境， ^2-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-209]]

多瞋行者，緣修慈境， ^2-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-210]]

多癡行者，緣眾緣性諸緣起境， ^2-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-211]]

憍慢行者，緣界差別境， ^2-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-212]]

尋思行者，緣入出息念境。 ^2-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-213]]

何故多貪行者等，緣不淨等修治行所緣境耶？ ^2-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-214]]

由此能息除增上貪等故。 ^2-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-215]]

善巧所緣者，略有五種，謂蘊善巧、界善巧、處善巧、緣起善巧、處非處善巧。 ^2-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-216]]

處非處善巧應云何觀？ ^2-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-217]]

應如緣起善巧觀。 ^2-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-218]]

問：緣起善巧、處非處善巧有何差別？ ^2-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-219]]

答：若以諸法流潤諸法， ^2-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-220]]

令離無因不平等因生故，是緣起善巧。 ^2-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-221]]

謂以無明等諸法，流潤行等諸法， ^2-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-222]]

非彼諸法無因而生，亦非自在天等不平等因生， ^2-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-223]]

如是觀智名緣起善巧。 ^2-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-224]]

因果相稱攝受生起故， ^2-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-225]]

是處非處善巧。 ^2-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-226]]

謂雖唯有法為因， ^2-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-227]]

然由攝受相稱因，方能生起相稱果。 ^2-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-228]]

如善行感可愛異熟、惡行感不可愛異熟，如是比。 ^2-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-229]]

如是觀智，名處非處善巧。 ^2-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-230]]

淨惑所緣者， ^2-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-231]]

謂下地麁性 ^2-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-232]]

上地靜性， ^2-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-233]]

真如及四聖諦。 ^2-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-234]]

下地麁性、上地靜性者，依世間道說， ^2-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-235]]

由此制伏諸纏故。 ^2-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-236]]

真如及四聖諦者，依出世道說。 ^2-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-237]]

略故真如，廣故四聖諦，由此永害諸隨眠故。 ^2-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-238]]

復次因辯觀察契經等法，應當解釋諸法道理， ^2-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-239]]

由依此道理能觀彼法故。 ^2-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-240]]

問：若欲於諸法正勤審觀察，由幾種道理能正觀察耶？ ^2-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-241]]

答：由四種道理，謂觀待道理、作用道理、證成道理、法爾道理。 ^2-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-242]]

觀待道理者， ^2-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-243]]

謂諸行生時，要待眾緣， ^2-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-244]]

如芽生時，要待種子、時節、水、田等緣， ^2-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-245]]

諸識生時，要待根、境、作意等緣，如是等。 ^2-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-246]]

作用道理者， ^2-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-247]]

謂異相諸法各別作用， ^2-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-248]]

如眼根等，為眼識等所依作用， ^2-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-249]]

色等境界，為眼識等所緣作用， ^2-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-250]]

眼等諸識了別色等； ^2-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-251]]

金銀匠等，善修造金銀等物，如是比。 ^2-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-252]]

證成道理者， ^2-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-253]]

謂為證成所應成義， 宣說諸量不相違語。 ^2-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-254]]

所應成義者， 謂自體差別所攝所應成義。 諸量不相違語者， 謂現量等不相違立宗等言。 ^2-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-255]]

法爾道理者， ^2-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-256]]

謂無始時來，於自相、共相所住法中，所有成就法性法爾。 ^2-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-257]]

如火能燒、水能爛，如是等諸法成就法性法爾。 ^2-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-258]]

如經言：眼雖圓淨，空、無有常乃至無我。 ^2-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-259]]

所以者何？ ^2-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-260]]

其性法爾。 ^2-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-261]]

復次，於諸法中，正勤觀察四道理已，云何而起尋思？ ^2-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-262]]

謂起四種尋思：一名尋思、二事尋思、三自體假立尋思、四差別假立尋思。 ^2-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-263]]

名尋思者，謂推求諸法名身、句身、文身自相皆不成實。 ^2-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-264]]

由名身等是假有故， ^2-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-265]]

觀彼自相皆不成實。 ^2-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-266]]

事尋思者，謂推求諸法，蘊、界、處相皆不成實。 ^2-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-267]]

由諸蘊等如名身等，所宣說事皆不成實，是故觀彼相不成實。 推求者，是觀察義。 ^2-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-268]]

自體假立尋思者，謂於諸法，能詮、所詮相應中推求自體，唯是假立言說因性。 ^2-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-269]]

能詮、所詮相應者，謂此二互為領解因性。 ^2-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-270]]

所以者何？ ^2-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-271]]

善名言者，但聞能詮， ^2-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-272]]

由憶念門便於所詮得生領解。 ^2-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-273]]

或但得所詮，由憶念門便於能詮得生領解。 ^2-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-274]]

於如是種類共立相應中，眼等自相唯是假立， ^2-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-275]]

但於肉團等名言因中起此名言故。 ^2-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-276]]

若如是觀察，是名自體假立尋思。 ^2-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-277]]

差別假立尋思者， ^2-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-278]]

謂於諸法能詮、所詮相應中，推求差別，唯是假立名言因性。 ^2-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-279]]

所以者何？ ^2-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-280]]

以於能詮、所詮相應中，推求若常、無常、有上、無上、有色、無色、有見、無見等差別相，唯是假立名言因性。 ^2-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-281]]

如是觀察，是名差別假立尋思。 ^2-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-282]]

復次，於法正勤修尋思已，必於諸法得如實智。 云何而起如實智耶？ ^2-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-283]]

謂起四如實智： ^2-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-284]]

一名尋思所引如實智、 ^2-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-285]]

二事尋思所引如實智、 ^2-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-286]]

三自體假立尋思所引如實智、 ^2-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-287]]

四差別假立尋思所引如實智。 ^2-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-288]]

名尋思所引如實智者， ^2-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-289]]

謂如實知名不可得智。 ^2-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-290]]

事尋思所引如實智者， ^2-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-291]]

謂如實知事相亦不可得智。 ^2-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-292]]

自體假立尋思所引如實智者， ^2-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-293]]

謂如實知實有自性不可得智。 ^2-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-294]]

差別假立尋思所引如實智者， ^2-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-295]]

謂如實知實有差別不可得智。 ^2-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-296]]

此四如實智，如前所尋思，了知名等如實皆不可得。 ^2-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-297]]

復次，依法修三摩地者，瑜伽地云何當知？ 此地略有五種， ^2-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-298]]

謂持、任、鏡、明、依。 ^2-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-299]]

持者，謂已積集菩提資糧，於煖等位依諸聖諦所有多聞， ^2-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-300]]

如所多聞，安立止觀所緣境故，說名為持。 ^2-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-301]]

又已積集菩提資糧者，為求諦現觀，聽受契經等法，故名多聞。 ^2-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-302]]

任者，謂緣此境如理作意， ^2-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-303]]

由此作意，依所多聞無倒思惟，所聞義相任持心故。 ^2-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-304]]

鏡者，謂緣此境有相三摩地， ^2-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-305]]

此三摩地即緣多聞， 為境與定相俱，故名有相， ^2-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-306]]

由此三摩地猶帶所知事同分影像相故。 ^2-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-307]]

又此三摩地能審照察所知事質，故譬於鏡。 ^2-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-308]]

明者，謂能取所取無所得智， ^2-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-309]]

由此智見道所攝現觀轉故。 ^2-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-310]]

云何菩薩依瑜伽地方便修學證無所得？ ^2-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-311]]

謂諸菩薩已善積集福德智慧二種資糧， 已過第一無數大劫， ^2-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-312]]

已聞隨順通達真如契經等法， 如理作意發三摩地， ^2-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-313]]

依止定心思惟定中所知影像， ^2-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-314]]

觀此影像不異定心， 依此影像捨外境想， ^2-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-315]]

唯定觀察自想影像。 ^2-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-316]]

爾時菩薩了知諸法唯自心故， ^2-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-317]]

內住其心， 知一切種所取境界皆無所有。 ^2-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-318]]

所取無故，一切能取亦非真實， ^2-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-319]]

故次了知能取非有。 ^2-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-320]]

次復於內捨離所得二種自性，證無所得。 ^2-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-321]]

依此道理，佛薄伽梵妙善宣說： ^2-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-322]]

菩薩於定位， ^2-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-323]]

觀影唯是心， ^2-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-324]]

義想既滅除， ^2-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-325]]

審觀唯自想。 ^2-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-326]]

知所住內心， ^2-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-327]]

知所取非有， ^2-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-328]]

次能取亦無， ^2-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-329]]

後觸無所得。 ^2-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-331]]

{云何菩薩依瑜伽地方便修學證無所得？} ^2-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-332]]

{謂諸菩薩已善積集福德智慧二種資糧，已過第一無數大劫，} ^2-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-333]]

{已聞隨順通達真如契經等法，如理作意發三摩地，} ^2-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-334]]

{依止定心思惟定中所知影像，} ^2-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-335]]

{觀此影像不異定心， 依此影像捨外境想，} ^2-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-336]]

{唯定觀察自想影像。} ^2-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-337]]

{爾時菩薩了知諸法唯自心故，} ^2-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-338]]

{內住其心， 知一切種所取境界皆無所有。} ^2-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-339]]

{所取無故，一切能取亦非真實，故次了知能取非有。} ^2-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-340]]

{次復於內捨離所得二種自性，證無所得。} ^2-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-350]]

依者，謂轉依，捨離一切麁重得清淨轉依故。 ^2-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-351]]

當知此中，以因果兩位釋瑜伽地， ^2-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-352]]

由持等四種釋此地因， ^2-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-353]]

最後一種釋此地果。 ^2-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-354]]

復次，云何於諸法中法善巧？ ^2-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-355]]

云何義善巧？ ^2-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-356]]

云何文善巧？ ^2-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-357]]

云何詞善巧？ ^2-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-358]]

云何前際後際密意善巧？ 如是五問，隨順經中所說諸句。 ^2-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-359]]

如尊者阿難告舍利子：長老當知，若諸比丘成就五法，即能速受、 多受、 善受、 受已不失。 ^2-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-360]]

於此經中，即由五法如其所應成速受等四種句義。 ^2-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-361]]

云何法善巧？ ^2-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-362]]

謂多聞故， ^2-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-363]]

於法善巧便能速受， ^2-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-364]]

由具多聞者，多分能速受文句差別故。 ^2-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-365]]

云何義善巧？ ^2-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-366]]

謂於阿毘達磨、毘奈耶中善知其相故， ^2-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-367]]

於義善巧便能多受。 ^2-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-368]]

若善了知阿毘達磨等相， ^2-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-369]]

乃於蘊、界、處等所說事中，便能攝集眾多文故。 ^2-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-370]]

云何文善巧？ ^2-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-371]]

謂善知訓釋文詞故。 ^2-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-372]]

云何詞善巧？ ^2-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-373]]

謂能善知我我所等世俗言詞，不深執著，隨順說故。 ^2-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-374]]

若於文詞俱得善巧，便能妙善領受所說。 ^2-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-375]]

善知訓釋文詞故，善知我我所等世俗言詞。 ^2-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-376]]

不深執著隨順說故，即能無倒領受文義。 ^2-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-377]]

云何前際後際密意善巧？ ^2-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-378]]

謂能善知於前際領受、於後際出離故。 ^2-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-379]]

若於前際後際密意善巧， ^2-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-380]]

便能受已而不失壞， ^2-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-381]]

依止前際所受法， ^2-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-382]]

後能證得出離故。 ^2-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-383]]

由善了知如來密意，便能證取聖教堅實。 ^2-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-384]]

云何於諸法中安住於法？ ^2-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-385]]

若不得修慧， ^2-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-386]]

唯勤方便修習聞思， 不得名為安住於法。 ^2-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-387]]

若不得聞思， ^2-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-388]]

唯勤方便修習修慧， ^2-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-389]]

亦不得名安住於法。 ^2-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-390]]

若俱得二種方便安住，如是乃名安住於法。 ^2-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-391]]

如經言：大德當知，若諸比丘如是住法，乃可名為住法比丘。 於此經中世尊顯示，若能具依聞思修住，方名住法；非隨住一方便修習得名住法。 ^2-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-392]]

若唯於法受持、讀誦、為他宣說等，是名聞思所生慧。 ^2-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-393]]

如說若於是處多究其文，讀誦宣說又多尋思， ^2-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-394]]

唯修聞思慧，不修習修慧，捨離瑜伽等，不可建立為住法。 ^2-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-395]]

若修三摩地方便不知足，是名修所生慧。 ^2-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-396]]

如說若有不得聞思，唯修修慧，亦不可立為住法。 ^2-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-397]]

是故，世尊因住法比丘說如是言： ^2-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-399]]

若比丘於法究竟，所謂契經、應頌，乃至廣說。 ^2-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-400]]

已後復說言不捨瑜伽。如是等應如理知。 ^2-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-401]]

若有具得聞、思、修慧，依二種住，是名住法不捨瑜伽。 ^2-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-402]]

如是等者，謂修三摩地方便不知足，顯示修所生慧。 三摩地方便者，謂無間殷重方便，及無顛倒方便。 ^2-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-403]]

此則顯示二種方便： ^2-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-404]]

一無間殷重方便所攝，如說不捨瑜伽故。 ^2-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-405]]

二無顛倒方便所攝， ^2-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-406]]

如說不捨作意故。 ^2-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-407]]

{如說不捨內心奢摩他故。} ^2-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-408]]

不知足者，謂不生味著修上奢摩他方便， ^2-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-409]]

如說不捨內心奢摩他故。 ^2-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-410]]

此則顯示不生味著故，及修上奢摩他方便故，名為不捨。 ^2-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-411]]

復次何因緣故，十二分聖教中方廣分名菩薩波羅蜜多藏耶？ ^2-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-412]]

由此分中，廣說一切波羅蜜多數故、相故、次第故、釋詞故、修故、差別故、攝故、所治故、功德故、更互決擇故。 ^2-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-413]]

問：於何處說？ ^2-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-414]]

答：如經中說，大乘者即是菩薩波羅蜜多藏。 ^2-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-415]]

云何宣說波羅蜜多數相次第， ^2-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-416]]

乃至更互決擇？ ^2-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-417]]

數有二種： ^2-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-418]]

一計算數、二決定數。 ^2-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-419]]

計算數者，謂六波羅蜜多。 ^2-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-420]]

決定數者， 謂波羅蜜多數唯有六， 不增不減。 何以故？ 一切菩薩道略有二種：一增上生道、二決定勝道。 如其次第三三攝故。 ^2-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-421]]

所以者何？ ^2-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-422]]

增上生有三種：一大資財、二大自體、三大眷屬。 ^2-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-423]]

施波羅蜜多感大資財果。 ^2-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-424]]

戒波羅蜜多感大自體果， 由持淨戒，生善趣中得尊貴身故。 ^2-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-425]]

忍波羅蜜多感大眷屬果，能行忍者，一切眾生咸所歸附故。 ^2-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-426]]

決定勝道有三種： ^2-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-427]]

一伏諸煩惱 ^2-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-428]]

修習善品方便、 ^2-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-429]]

二成熟有情方便、 ^2-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-430]]

三成熟佛法方便。 ^2-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-431]]

如是三中隨闕一種， ^2-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-432]]

菩薩決定勝道必不成就。 ^2-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-433]]

成熟有情方便者， ^2-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-434]]

謂靜慮波羅蜜多， ^2-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-435]]

依此發神通成熟有情故。 ^2-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-436]]

復次波羅蜜多是無住處涅槃方便故，其數唯六。 ^2-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-437]]

所以者何？ ^2-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-438]]

由諸菩薩為翻住涅槃故， ^2-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-439]]

於生死中攝增上生； ^2-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-440]]

為翻住生死故， ^2-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-441]]

即於生死而不染污。 ^2-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-442]]

是故前三是得增上生方便， ^2-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-443]]

後三是不染污方便， ^2-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-444]]

隨其所應如前應知。 ^2-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-445]]

不染污方便者，由精進故修習對治， ^2-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-446]]

由靜慮故伏諸煩惱， ^2-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-447]]

由智慧故永害一切煩惱隨眠。 ^2-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-448]]

復次為攝益一切有情故、 ^2-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-449]]

對治一切煩惱故， ^2-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-450]]

波羅蜜多唯有六種。 ^2-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-451]]

所以者何？ ^2-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-452]]

菩薩摩訶薩由布施故引攝資財，方便攝益一切有情。 ^2-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-453]]

由持戒故，不起侵損、逼迫、惱亂，方便攝益一切有情， ^2-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-454]]

如其次第，不毀壞他財身心故。 ^2-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-455]]

由忍辱故，堪受侵損、逼迫、惱亂，方便攝益一切有情， ^2-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-456]]

由堪忍他侵損己財等故。 ^2-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-457]]

由此三種，善能攝益一切有情。 ^2-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-458]]

由精進故，雖未永伏一切煩惱，而依善品修彼對治。 ^2-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-459]]

由靜慮故，永伏煩惱。 ^2-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-460]]

由智慧故，永害隨眠。 ^2-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-461]]

由此三種，善能對治一切煩惱。 ^2-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-462]]

相者，謂諸菩薩波羅蜜多相。 ^2-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-463]]

云何施波羅蜜多相？ 謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-464]]

以悲導心，捨一切時所有身、語、意業。 ^2-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-465]]

如是由種性故、願故、意樂故、事故、自體故，顯施波羅蜜多相。 ^2-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-466]]

種性者，謂菩薩法性。 ^2-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-467]]

願者，謂菩提心。 ^2-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-468]]

意樂者，謂悲導心。 ^2-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-469]]

事者，謂捨諸所有。 ^2-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-470]]

自體者，謂身語意業。 ^2-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-472]]

云何戒波羅蜜多相？ ^2-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-473]]

謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-474]]

以悲導心，受持一切菩薩戒時所有身、語、意業。 ^2-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-475]]

云何忍波羅蜜多相？ ^2-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-476]]

謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-477]]

以悲導心，堪忍安受諸怨苦時所有身、語、意業。 ^2-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-478]]

云何精進波羅蜜多相？ ^2-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-479]]

謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-480]]

以悲導心，引發一切勝善法時所有身、語、意業。 ^2-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-481]]

云何靜慮波羅蜜多相？ ^2-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-482]]

謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-483]]

以悲導心，起一切種身、語、意業自在用時，所有一切心恒安住。 ^2-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-484]]

云何慧波羅蜜多相？ ^2-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-485]]

謂諸菩薩安住菩薩法性，菩提心為依止， ^2-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-486]]

以悲導心，起一切種身語意業自在用時，所有一切諸法簡擇。 ^2-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-487]]

復次，若所行施， 依止薩伐若性、 ^2-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-488]]

能感薩伐若性、 ^2-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-489]]

攝受薩伐若性、 ^2-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-490]]

能為一切薩伐若事， ^2-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-491]]

是名施波羅蜜多相。 ^2-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-492]]

如是四句隨其次第，由發起故、習氣故、自體故、等流故，顯波羅蜜多相。 ^2-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-493]]

發起者，謂依止一切智性， ^2-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-494]]

凡所生起一切施行，皆迴向薩伐若性故。 ^2-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-495]]

習氣者，謂能感一切智性。 ^2-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-496]]

即所行施熏修相續，於當來世能感薩伐若性故。 ^2-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-497]]

自體者，謂攝受一切智性。 ^2-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-498]]

即所行施究竟圓滿，爾時能辦佛法身故。 ^2-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-499]]

{自體者，謂攝受一切智性。} ^2-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-500]]

等流者，謂能為一切一切智事。 ^2-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-501]]

從此後時由受用變化身等流門，發起一切薩伐若事故。 ^2-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-502]]

如施波羅蜜多相， ^2-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-503]]

乃至慧波羅蜜多相，皆應如是說。 ^2-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-504]]

次第者，謂前前波羅蜜多，能為後後所依止故。 ^2-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-505]]

所以者何？ ^2-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-506]]

菩薩摩訶薩由施波羅蜜多，串習捨施內外事故， ^2-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-507]]

不顧身命、棄大寶藏、 ^2-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-508]]

受持禁戒。 ^2-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-509]]

由護戒故，他所毀罵終不反報。 ^2-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-510]]

由如是等遂能堪忍， ^2-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-511]]

以能堪忍寒熱等苦，雖遭此緣，加行不息發勤精進。 ^2-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-512]]

精進方便證究竟果，成滿靜慮。 ^2-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-513]]

靜慮滿已，由淨定心如實知故， ^2-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-514]]

證得出世究竟大慧。 ^2-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-515]]

復次，前前波羅蜜多，後後所持故。 ^2-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-516]]

謂戒能持施， ^2-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-517]]

乃至慧能持靜慮。 ^2-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-518]]

由具尸羅施得清淨。 何以故？ 由行布施攝益有情， 由具尸羅不為惱害。 ^2-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-519]]

是故菩薩於受施者，以離惱害，善能施與清淨樂具，故由淨戒力施得清淨。 ^2-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-520]]

如是由忍力故戒得清淨。何以故？由能忍受他不饒益，終不毀犯所學處故。 ^2-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-521]]

由精進故忍得清淨。何以故？ 由勇猛力故，久處生死不以為難，能受眾生違逆等苦。 ^2-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-522]]

由靜慮故精進清淨。何以故？ 由喜樂俱，能勤修習一切善法無休息故。 ^2-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-523]]

由具慧故靜慮清淨。何以故？ 若由無量門數數觀諸法，能證內寂靜，增長三摩地。 ^2-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-524]]

又伽他說：無有靜慮而不因慧。 ^2-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-525]]

復次由麁細故，波羅蜜多前後次第。 ^2-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-526]]

所以者何？ ^2-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-527]]

於諸行中施行最麁，故先建立。 ^2-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-528]]

於忍等行，戒復為麁，故次建立。 ^2-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-529]]

乃至於慧，靜慮為麁， ^2-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-530]]

一切行中慧為最細， ^2-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-531]]

故最後立。 ^2-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-532]]

釋詞者，謂諸菩薩所行布施，所以名施波羅蜜多者， ^2-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-533]]

謂由大施故、離過故、離垢故，名施波羅蜜多。 ^2-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-534]]

大施者，盡捨一切內外事故、 ^2-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-535]]

長時施故。 ^2-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-536]]

離過者，遠離不平等追求等過故。 ^2-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-537]]

離垢者，永斷一切所治慳故。 ^2-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-538]]

如《無盡慧經》施無盡中說：云何離垢？ ^2-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-539]]

^1{此處漢譯缺，《大方等大集經》卷27〈12 無盡意菩薩品〉：「菩薩修行檀波羅蜜不可窮盡。」(CBETA 2024.R3, T13, no. 397, p. 189a18)} ^2-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-540]]

^2{此處漢譯缺，《大方等大集經》卷27〈12 無盡意菩薩品〉：「何以故？菩薩摩訶薩行施無量，所謂須食與食，具足命辯色力樂故；」(CBETA 2024.R3, T13, no. 397, p. 189a18-20)} ^2-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-541]]

永斷所治并習氣故。^3{此處漢譯有異，《大方等大集經》卷27〈12 無盡意菩薩品〉：「須飲與飲，離渴愛故；」(CBETA 2024.R3, T13, no. 397, p. 189a20) } ^2-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-542]]

^4{此處漢譯缺，《大方等大集經》卷27〈12 無盡意菩薩品〉：「無盡意言：「菩薩修行檀波羅蜜不可窮盡。何以故？菩薩摩訶薩行施無量，所謂須食與食，具足命、辯、色、力、樂故；須飲與飲，離渴愛故；須衣與衣，具清淨色除無慚愧故；須乘與乘，得一切樂具神通故；須燈與燈，具足佛眼清淨故；須音樂者施與音樂，具足天耳清徹故；須香與香，身出具足微妙香故；須鬘與鬘，具陀羅尼七覺華故；須塗香末香悉施與之，具戒定慧熏塗身故；須種種味隨意與之，味相成就故；無依止者施與依止，能為眾生具足救護為歸依故；須敷具者悉施與之，具足究竟斷除陰蓋，成就梵天賢聖諸佛妙床座故；須座與座，具足三千大千世界以為道場，金剛座處悉成就故；隨其所須悉能與之，成就菩提諸所須故；隨病施藥得無老死，甘露法藥悉成就故；須僕使者皆給與之，自在智慧得具足故；」(CBETA 2024.R3, T13, no. 397, p. 189a20-b5)} ^2-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-543]]

如是三句，顯波羅蜜多三種最勝。 ^2-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-544]]

何等為三？ 一自體最勝并積習、二方便最勝、三果最勝。 ^2-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-545]]

積習者，謂長時施故。 ^2-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-546]]

如施波羅蜜多有三種，乃至慧波羅蜜多亦爾。 ^2-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-547]]

戒等離過者，謂遠離我增益等， ^2-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-548]]

隨其所應， 如《無盡慧經》廣說。 ^2-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-549]]

復次，由與十二種最勝相應故，名波羅蜜多。 ^2-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-550]]

何等名為十二最勝？ 一廣大最勝，不求一切世間樂故，又最上故。 ^2-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-551]]

二長時最勝，經三大劫阿僧企耶所積習故。 ^2-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-552]]

三所為最勝，為利益安樂一切有情故。 ^2-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-553]]

四無盡最勝，由迴向大菩提究竟無盡故。 ^2-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-554]]

五無間最勝，由得自他平等勝解， ^2-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-555]]

令諸有情於施等波羅蜜多速圓滿故。 ^2-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-556]]

六無難最勝，唯由隨喜他所行施等，令波羅蜜多速圓滿故。 ^2-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-557]]

七大自在最勝，由得虛空藏等諸三摩地，令布施等波羅蜜多速圓滿故。 ^2-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-558]]

八攝受最勝，無分別智所攝受故。 ^2-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-559]]

九發起最勝，謂解行地中上品忍位所行施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-560]]

十證得最勝，謂初地中，所得施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-561]]

十一等流最勝，謂餘八地中，所行施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-562]]

十二圓滿最勝，謂第十地及如來地，所有施等波羅蜜多，如其次第，菩薩圓滿故、佛圓滿故。 ^2-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-563]]

復次最勝所作故、最勝所至故，名波羅蜜多， ^2-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-564]]

一切佛菩薩所為所到故。 ^2-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-565]]

復次，到所知彼岸故，名波羅蜜多，安住佛性故。 ^2-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-566]]

復次，濟度自他最極災橫故，名波羅蜜多， ^2-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-567]]

能令自他越度生死大苦海故。 ^2-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-568]]

共詞已釋， ^2-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-569]]

不共今當說。 ^2-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-570]]

能捨施者當來貧苦，能捨受者現在熱惱，故名為施。 ^2-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-571]]

能令諸根永寂靜故、 ^2-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-572]]

能趣清淨諸善趣故、 ^2-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-573]]

能為清涼所依處故，說名為戒。 ^2-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-574]]

隨其次第，能引守護諸根門故、 ^2-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-575]]

是往清淨善趣因故、 ^2-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-576]]

能為無悔等漸次乃至涅槃所依故。 ^2-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-577]]

遠離一切忿熱灰故、遠離不捨怨害心故、 ^2-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-578]]

顯發損者常安隱故，說名為忍。 ^2-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-579]]

怨害心者，謂起報怨心。 ^2-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-580]]

不捨者，謂不棄此心。 ^2-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-581]]

能壞怨心，名為損者。 ^2-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-582]]

顯此無畏，故名顯發損者常安隱故。 ^2-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-583]]

損害生長作用相應，故名精進。 ^2-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-584]]

損害作用相應者，謂前二正斷，以能損害不善法故。 ^2-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-585]]

生長作用相應者，謂後二正斷，以能生長諸善法故。 ^2-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-586]]

能持、能息、能靜、能調，又能引發，故名靜慮。 ^2-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-587]]

能持者，謂於境繫心。 ^2-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-588]]

能息者，謂息諸散亂。 ^2-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-589]]

能靜者，謂令心寂靜。 ^2-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-590]]

能調者，謂制伏諸纏。 ^2-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-591]]

能引發者，謂能引發自在作用。 ^2-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-592]]

他所發智故、內證智故、種別智故、得寂靜智故、勝德智故，名為慧。 ^2-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-593]]

他所發智者，謂從他言音所生慧，及如理作意相應慧。 ^2-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-594]]

內證智者，謂出世間慧。 ^2-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-595]]

種別智者，謂出世間後所得慧。 ^2-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-596]]

得寂靜智者，謂修道中治煩惱慧。 ^2-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-597]]

勝德智者，謂能引發勝功德慧。 ^2-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-598]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十一大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十二 ^2-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-599]]

安慧菩薩糅釋上集論大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯 ^2-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-600]]

決擇分中法品第二之二 ^2-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-601]]

云何修？ 略有五種，謂依止任持修、依止作意修、依止意樂修、依止方便修、依止自在修。 ^2-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-602]]

依止任持修復有四種： ^2-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-603]]

一依止因修，謂由種性力，於波羅蜜多修習正行。 ^2-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-604]]

二依止報修，謂由勝自體力，於波羅蜜多修習正行。 ^2-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-605]]

三依止願修，謂由本願力，於波羅蜜多修習正行。 ^2-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-606]]

四依止簡擇力修，謂由慧力，於波羅蜜多修習正行。 ^2-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-607]]

依止作意修亦有四種： ^2-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-608]]

一依止勝解作意修，謂於一切波羅蜜多相應經教起增上勝解。 ^2-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-609]]

二依止愛味作意修，謂於已得波羅蜜多見勝功德起深愛味。 ^2-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-610]]

三依止隨喜作意修，謂於一切世界一切有情所行施等深生隨喜。 ^2-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-611]]

四依止憙樂作意修，謂於自他當來勝品波羅蜜多深生願樂。 ^2-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-612]]

依止意樂修復有六種， ^2-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-613]]

謂由無厭意樂、廣大意樂、歡喜意樂、恩德意樂、無染意樂、善好意樂故，修諸波羅蜜多。 ^2-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-614]]

此中菩薩於施波羅蜜多無厭意樂者，謂諸菩薩於一有情一剎那頃，假使殑伽沙等世界滿中七寶以用布施， ^2-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-615]]

又以殑伽沙等身命布施， ^2-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-616]]

如是布施經殑伽沙等大劫。 ^2-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-617]]

如於一有情所，如是乃至於一切有情界如是施時， 皆令彼於阿耨多羅三藐三菩提速得成熟。 ^2-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-618]]

修行如是差別施時，菩薩意樂由不厭足。 ^2-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-619]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多無厭意樂。 ^2-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-620]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是施波羅蜜多時，展轉相續無一剎那有退有斷，乃至究竟坐菩提座。 ^2-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-621]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多廣大意樂。 ^2-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-622]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是施波羅蜜多時，於施所攝諸有情所生大歡喜， ^2-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-623]]

是諸有情施所攝受，雖生歡喜猶不能及。 ^2-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-624]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多歡喜意樂。 ^2-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-625]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是施波羅蜜多時，觀施所攝一切有情，於我己身有大恩德，不見己身於彼有恩， ^2-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-626]]

由資助我阿耨多羅三藐三菩提故。 ^2-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-627]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多恩德意樂。 ^2-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-628]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是施波羅蜜多時，雖於無量諸有情所興大施福， ^2-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-629]]

而不希報恩當來果報。 ^2-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-630]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多無染意樂。 ^2-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-631]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是施波羅蜜多時，以所修行廣大施聚所得果報，施諸有情不自為己， ^2-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-632]]

又以此福共諸有情迴向阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 ^2-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-633]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於施波羅蜜多善好意樂。 ^2-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-634]]

又諸菩薩，修行戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時，無厭意樂者，謂諸菩薩假使經於殑伽沙等生， ^2-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-635]]

是一一生殑伽沙等大劫壽量， ^2-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-636]]

於此長時，諸資生具常所匱乏， ^2-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-637]]

三千大千世界滿中熾火，恒在其中行住坐臥， ^2-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-638]]

唯能修習一剎那戒波羅蜜多， ^2-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-639]]

或乃至慧波羅蜜多，如是展轉差別修習， ^2-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-640]]

所有戒聚乃至慧聚，究竟滿足，現能證得阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 ^2-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-641]]

是諸菩薩，修行如是戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時， ^2-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-642]]

於此戒聚乃至慧聚，修習意樂猶不滿足。 ^2-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-643]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修習戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多無厭意樂。 ^2-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-644]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時，展轉相續無一剎那有退有斷，乃至究竟坐菩提座。 ^2-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-645]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修行戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多廣大意樂。 ^2-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-646]]

又諸菩薩，修行如是戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時，於此所攝諸有情所生大歡喜， ^2-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-647]]

是諸有情由此所攝雖生歡喜，猶不能及。 ^2-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-648]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修行戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多歡喜意樂。 ^2-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-649]]

又諸菩薩修行如是戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時， ^2-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-650]]

觀此所攝一切有情，於我己身有大恩德，不見己身於彼有恩， ^2-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-651]]

由資助我阿耨多羅三藐三菩提故。 ^2-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-652]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修行戒波羅蜜多 ^2-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-653]]

乃至慧波羅蜜多恩德意樂。 ^2-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-654]]

又諸菩薩修行如是戒波羅蜜多乃至慧波羅蜜多時，雖於無量諸有情所興大戒福乃至慧福，而不希報恩當來果報。 ^2-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-655]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修行戒波羅蜜多 ^2-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-656]]

乃至慧波羅蜜多無染意樂。 ^2-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-657]]

又諸菩薩修行如是戒波羅蜜多 ^2-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-658]]

乃至慧波羅蜜多時，以所修行廣大戒聚乃至慧聚所得果報，施諸有情不自為己， ^2-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-659]]

又以此福共諸有情 ^2-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-660]]

迴向阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 ^2-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-661]]

如是意樂，是名菩薩於所修行戒波羅蜜多 ^2-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-662]]

乃至慧波羅蜜多善好意樂。 ^2-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-663]]

依止方便修復有三種， ^2-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-664]]

謂由無分別智觀察三輪皆清淨故。 ^2-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-665]]

所以者何？ ^2-665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-666]]

由此方便，一切作意所修諸行速成滿故。 ^2-666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-667]]

依止自在修亦有三種， ^2-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-668]]

謂身自在故、 ^2-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-669]]

行自在故、 ^2-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-670]]

說自在故。 ^2-670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-671]]

身自在者，謂諸如來自性受用。 二身行自在者，謂諸如來變化身， ^2-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-672]]

由此能示現一切有情一切種同法行故。 ^2-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-673]]

說自在者，謂能宣說六波羅蜜多一切種差別無有滯礙故。 ^2-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-674]]

差別云何？ 謂由十八種任持以顯六波羅蜜多差別。 ^2-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-675]]

何等十八？ 謂身任持、心任持、善法任持、善任持、 ^2-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-676]]

菩提任持、悲任持、不捨有情任持、捨下劣心任持、 ^2-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-677]]

無生法忍任持、善根方便任持、善根圓證任持、善根無盡任持、 ^2-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-678]]

無厭倦任持、諸所思事成滿任持、御眾業任持、證入大地任持、 ^2-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-679]]

引發佛性任持、建立佛事任持。 ^2-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-680]]

施等六種各三差別，如其次第三三所攝。 ^2-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-681]]

施三種者，謂財施、無畏施、法施。 ^2-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-682]]

戒三種者，謂律儀戒、攝善法戒、饒益有情戒。 ^2-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-683]]

忍三種者，謂耐怨害忍、安受苦忍、諦察法忍。 ^2-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-684]]

精進三種者，謂被甲精進、方便精進、饒益有情精進。 ^2-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-685]]

靜慮三種者，謂現法樂住靜慮、引發神通靜慮、饒益有情靜慮。 ^2-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-686]]

慧三種者，謂緣世俗慧、緣勝義慧、緣有情慧。 ^2-686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-688]]

當知財施能任持身，由飲食等諸資生具，攝益受者所依身故。 ^2-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-689]]

無畏施能任持心，安慰他心離憂怖故。 ^2-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-690]]

如是餘句，隨義應思。 ^2-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-691]]

下劣心者，謂諸菩薩厭生死苦同二乘心， ^2-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-692]]

由安受苦忍所任持故方捨此心。 ^2-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-693]]

善根無盡者，謂窮生死際，恒作一切有情利益安樂事， ^2-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-694]]

乃至於無餘涅槃界亦不棄捨， ^2-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-695]]

由饒益有情精進所任持故。 ^2-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-696]]

御眾業者，謂依止內證故， ^2-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-697]]

教授教誡所化有情，心未定者令其得定、 ^2-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-698]]

心已定者令其解脫， ^2-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-700]]

由饒益有情靜慮所任持故。 ^2-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-701]]

證入大地者，謂先信解甚深教法，資糧圓滿 ^2-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-702]]

速能證入初極喜地， ^2-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-703]]

由緣世俗慧所任持故。 ^2-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-704]]

所餘易了故不重釋。 ^2-704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-705]]

又差別者，謂施有七種： ^2-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-706]]

一根本施，謂種性位，菩薩所有施波羅蜜多， ^2-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-707]]

依止種性而行施故。 ^2-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-708]]

二弘誓施，謂發心位，菩薩所有施波羅蜜多， 依受大願而行施故。 ^2-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-709]]

三攝受施，謂自他利行位，菩薩所有施波羅蜜多。 ^2-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-710]]

四無執受施，謂觀真實義位，菩薩所有施波羅蜜多， ^2-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-711]]

以無施者等分別執受故。 ^2-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-712]]

五無攝受施，謂威德位， 菩薩所有施波羅蜜多，雖不攝受外資生具， ^2-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-713]]

但由虛空藏等三摩地力，舉手麾空隨欲皆雨珍寶等物。 ^2-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-714]]

六隨所應施，謂成熟位菩薩所有施波羅蜜多，隨所化宜而行施故。 ^2-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-715]]

七廣大施，謂最勝菩提位，所有施波羅蜜多，以無上故。 ^2-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-716]]

如施有七種，乃至慧亦爾，隨其所應。 ^2-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-717]]

攝云何？ 謂為攝菩薩地故， ^2-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-718]]

於中略說施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-719]]

此攝略有四種： ^2-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-720]]

一種性攝，謂施波羅蜜多等種性相應隨順知。 ^2-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-721]]

二發心攝，謂差別發心所攝故。 ^2-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-722]]

發心有二種， ^2-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-723]]

謂無差別、差別。 ^2-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-724]]

無差別者， ^2-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-725]]

謂願我當證阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 差別者，謂願我施波羅蜜多速得圓滿，乃至慧波羅蜜多速得圓滿。 ^2-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-726]]

當知此中由差別發心攝諸波羅蜜多，此是彼因故。 ^2-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-727]]

三自他利攝，謂由施攝故，攝受一切富樂自在，是名自利攝。 ^2-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-728]]

由此施故引攝財物為饒益他，是名他利攝。 ^2-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-729]]

如是所餘攝相，隨義應知。 ^2-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-730]]

四勝義攝，此復多種，謂或依法界說，以真如是施等共相故。 ^2-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-731]]

或依智資糧說，以能成辦一切智器故。 ^2-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-732]]

或依智攝受說，以能成滿一切智故。 ^2-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-733]]

或依知隨轉說，以五波羅蜜多隨慧波羅蜜多轉故。 ^2-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-734]]

或依智相說，以慧波羅蜜多是正智自體故。 ^2-734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-735]]

如是勝義，以真如及正智為體故，能攝施等。 ^2-735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-736]]

由此略說，所餘攝義如理應思。 ^2-736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-737]]

所治者，謂施等六如其次第，以慳悋、犯戒、忿恚、懈怠、散亂、惡慧為所治。 ^2-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-738]]

復次乃至一切波羅蜜多所攝善法，彼所對治及所知障，皆是波羅蜜多所治。 ^2-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-739]]

功德者，謂依五果，無量無邊稱讚勝利皆名功德。 ^2-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-740]]

謂能永斷自所對治，是諸波羅蜜多離繫果。 ^2-740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-741]]

於現法中由此施等攝受自他，是士用果。 ^2-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-742]]

於當來世，後後增勝展轉生起，是等流果。 ^2-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-743]]

大菩提是增上果。 ^2-743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-744]]

感大財富，往生善趣， ^2-744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-745]]

無怨、無壞、多諸喜樂， ^2-745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-746]]

有情中尊，身無損害， ^2-746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-747]]

廣大宗族，隨其次第，是施等波羅蜜多異熟果。 ^2-747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-748]]

更互決擇者略有三種： ^2-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-749]]

一方便、二差別、三差別顯示。 ^2-749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-750]]

方便者，謂施方便中一切可得。 ^2-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-751]]

如捨內外一切身財，是施方便； ^2-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-752]]

此方便中若捨一切，是施波羅蜜多。 ^2-752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-753]]

即於此中，若慈悲心遮防一切損害、逼迫、惱亂他性，是戒波羅蜜多。 ^2-753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-754]]

即於此中，忍受遮礙、損害、疲倦， ^2-754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-755]]

是忍波羅蜜多。 ^2-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-756]]

即於此中，數數發起勇勵施心， 是精進波羅蜜多。 ^2-756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-757]]

即於此中，其心純善，繫心一境不外流散，是靜慮波羅蜜多。 ^2-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-758]]

即於此中，善取施行如實因果，不取異見，是慧波羅蜜多。 如是乃至慧波羅蜜多方便中，隨其所應當善建立。 ^2-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-759]]

由無畏施，一切處有施故。 ^2-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-760]]

差別者略有四種：一自體差別、二助伴差別、三勸讚差別、四種殖差別。 ^2-760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-761]]

施等波羅蜜多自體差別者，如其次第，以棄捨、防護、堪耐、策勤、心住、決擇為體。 ^2-761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-762]]

助伴差別者，謂施等方便中，餘波羅蜜多悉皆隨轉，如前廣說。 ^2-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-763]]

勸讚差別者，謂於施等勸勵讚美隨喜慶悅。 ^2-763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-764]]

種殖差別者，謂於他相續中，建立施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-765]]

此中施波羅蜜多是財施， ^2-765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-766]]

餘五波羅蜜多是無畏施， ^2-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-767]]

一切六是法施， ^2-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-768]]

皆於他相續中種殖故。 ^2-768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-769]]

差別顯示者，謂由一行等差別顯示施等波羅蜜多。 ^2-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-770]]

如有問言：若施波羅蜜多亦戒波羅蜜多耶？ ^2-770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-771]]

設戒波羅蜜多亦施波羅蜜多耶？ ^2-771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-772]]

為答此問，應作順後句，謂所有戒波羅蜜多皆是施波羅蜜多， ^2-772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-773]]

或有施波羅蜜多非戒波羅蜜多。 ^2-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-774]]

謂戒波羅蜜多所不攝施波羅蜜多， ^2-774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-775]]

如是乃至以慧波羅蜜多，對施波羅蜜多，皆應作順後句。 ^2-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-776]]

餘互相望，亦如理應思。 此中依始業地漸次修者，說後必待前、前不待後，是故皆作順後句。 若已串習六種頓修皆互相攝，如〈菩薩地〉說。 攝善法戒者，謂六波羅蜜多若依純雜相資助說，應作四句。 ^2-776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-777]]

復次諸所有施皆波羅蜜多耶？ ^2-777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-778]]

設波羅蜜多皆是施耶？ ^2-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-779]]

為答此問應作三句。 ^2-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-780]]

或有是施非波羅蜜多，謂所行施不迴向大菩提。 ^2-780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-781]]

或有亦施亦波羅蜜多， ^2-781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-782]]

謂所行施等迴向大菩提。 ^2-782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-783]]

或有非施非波羅蜜多，謂除上所說法。 ^2-783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-784]]

如是乃至依慧波羅蜜多， ^2-784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-785]]

一一皆應作四句，如理當思。 ^2-785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-786]]

復次，一切行施皆能生施波羅蜜多種類福耶？ ^2-786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-787]]

此應作四句。 ^2-787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-788]]

初句，謂所行施不迴向大菩提。 ^2-788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-789]]

第二句，謂於施波羅蜜多勸勵讚美隨喜慶悅。 ^2-789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-790]]

第三句，謂所行施迴向大菩提。 ^2-790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-791]]

第四句，謂除上爾所相。 ^2-791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-792]]

如是乃至依慧波羅蜜多， ^2-792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-793]]

各作四句，如理應思。 ^2-793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-794]]

復次，何緣說方廣分為廣大甚深耶？ ^2-794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-795]]

由一切種智性廣大甚深故。 ^2-795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-796]]

謂此所得一切種智性果最廣大甚深故，因受果名，是故別說方廣分為廣大甚深。 ^2-796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-797]]

復次，何緣一分眾生於方廣分廣大甚深，不生勝解，反懷怖畏耶？ ^2-797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-798]]

由遠離法性故、 ^2-798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-799]]

未種善根故、 ^2-799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-800]]

惡友所攝故。 ^2-800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-801]]

法性者，謂菩薩種性是彼自體故。 ^2-801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-802]]

由一分眾生無菩薩種性故， ^2-802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-803]]

心性下劣， ^2-803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-804]]

於廣大甚深教不能勝解，是故怖畏。 ^2-804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-805]]

又一分眾生雖有菩薩種性， ^2-805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-806]]

而於大菩提，未種正願等諸善根故，於此不能勝解，是故怖畏。 ^2-806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-807]]

又一分眾生雖已種善根，而為誹謗大乘眾生惡友所攝故，於此不能勝解，是故怖畏。 ^2-807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-809]]

復次，何緣一分眾生於方廣分廣大甚深雖生勝解，而不得出離耶？ ^2-809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-810]]

由深安住自見取故、 ^2-810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-811]]

常堅執著如言義故。 ^2-811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-812]]

深安住自見取者， ^2-812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-813]]

更不進求了義經故。 ^2-813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-814]]

常堅執著如言義者， ^2-814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-815]]

恒堅封執不了義經故。 ^2-815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-816]]

如聞一切法畢竟無自性言故，便撥一切諸法性相皆無所有， ^2-816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-817]]

如是於餘不了義經堅執如言義亦爾。 是故雖信大乘而不得出離。 ^2-817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-818]]

以大乘經由種種意說故， ^2-818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-819]]

依此密意，薄伽梵於《大法鏡經》中說如是言： ^2-819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-820]]

若諸菩薩隨言取義，不如正理思擇法故， ^2-820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-821]]

便生二十八不正見。 ^2-821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-822]]

何等名為二十八不正見耶？ ^2-822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-823]]

謂相見、損減施設見、損減分別見、損減真實見、 ^2-823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-824]]

攝受見、轉變見、無罪見、出離見、 ^2-824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-825]]

輕毀見、憤發見、顛倒見、出生見、 ^2-825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-826]]

不立宗見、矯亂見、敬事見、堅固愚癡見、 ^2-826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-827]]

根本見、於見無見見、捨方便見、不出離見、 ^2-827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-828]]

障增益見、生非福見、無功果見、受辱見、 ^2-828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-829]]

誹謗見、不可與言見、廣大見、增上慢見。 ^2-829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-830]]
