相見者，謂聞大乘經中所說一切諸法皆無自性、 ^2-830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-831]]

無生無滅、本來寂靜、自性涅槃等言， ^2-831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-832]]

不善密意，但隨此言義便生勝解， ^2-832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-833]]

謂佛所說一切諸法定無自性、定無生等。 執著如是無性等相，是名相見。 ^2-833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-834]]

彼執著如是無性等相時，便謗三自性，謂遍計所執自性、依他起自性、圓成實自性。 ^2-834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-835]]

遍計所執自性者，謂諸愚夫於色等相，周遍計度起增益執，謂此是色，乃至此是涅槃。 ^2-835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-836]]

此所執義無實無體，唯有名言之所施設。 ^2-836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-837]]

依他起自性者，謂即此色等，唯是虛妄分別自體， ^2-837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-838]]

又因果性或異不異。 ^2-838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-839]]

圓成實自性者，謂一切法真如實性。 ^2-839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-840]]

於此三性起誹謗行，即次三見， ^2-840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-841]]

謂損減施設見、 ^2-841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-842]]

損減分別見、 ^2-842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-843]]

損減真實見。 ^2-843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-844]]

彼如是誹謗一切種一切法時， ^2-844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-845]]

為欲成立此邪見故，便復攝受少分道理。 ^2-845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-846]]

又於所有開示離言諸法實性了義契經，廣設方便皆悉轉變令順己見。 如是二種，即次二見， ^2-846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-847]]

謂攝受見、轉變見。 ^2-847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-848]]

彼又起如是見： ^2-848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-849]]

若依此見行善不善， ^2-849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-850]]

定皆無罪無有過失；一切所行皆歸妙善， ^2-850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-851]]

先所積習一切障垢皆得出離。 如是二種，即次二見， ^2-851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-852]]

謂無罪見、出離見。 ^2-852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-853]]

彼如是執自見已，便於此見相違安立蘊等諸法聲聞藏中妄生輕毀， ^2-853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-854]]

又於不信如是邪見聲聞人等深生憎嫉。 如是二種， 即次二見， ^2-854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-855]]

輕毀見、憤發見。 ^2-855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-856]]

彼又隨順自惡邪見，謂我當建立如實空、無相、無願， ^2-856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-857]]

於非彼相起彼相想，而於彼相顛倒建立。 ^2-857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-858]]

又作是思惟：若能悟入如是法性，或令他入，一切皆生無量功德。 如是二種，即次二見， ^2-858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-859]]

謂顛倒見、出生見。 ^2-859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-860]]

若他於彼所起惡見如理詰責，彼於爾時，竟不樂欲建立自宗， ^2-860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-861]]

反以譏弄妄理詰責於他。 如是二種，即次二見， ^2-861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-862]]

謂不立宗見、矯亂見。 ^2-862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-863]]

彼又起如是增上慢， ^2-863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-864]]

謂若能如是修行，是真供養恭敬諸佛世尊。 ^2-864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-865]]

如是見者，名敬事見。 ^2-865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-866]]

諸有善達無倒法性者，為令捨離彼惡見故， ^2-866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-867]]

雖以種種真實成立道理方便開悟，堅守愚見曾無捨心，謂唯此真餘並邪妄。 ^2-867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-868]]

如是見者，名堅固愚癡見。 ^2-868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-869]]

如上所說諸見，所有習氣麁重，是名根本見。 ^2-869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-870]]

為欲開示如上所說十七種見諸過失門，復說餘見， ^2-870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-871]]

謂即相見，名於見無見見， ^2-871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-872]]

此實堅執無性等相而起，不執一切相想故。 ^2-872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-873]]

即損減施設見、 ^2-873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-874]]

損減分別見、 ^2-874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-875]]

損減真實見，名捨方便見， ^2-875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-876]]

由彼誹謗一切法性，於勤精進起無用想故。 ^2-876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-877]]

即攝受見、轉變見，名不出離見， ^2-877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-878]]

非方便修學不能證果故，即無罪見、出離見，名障增益見。 ^2-878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-879]]

所行邪僻無容盡障故，即輕毀見、憤發見，名生非福見。 ^2-879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-880]]

由於正法同梵行所，起邪行門，便能引發大衰損故， ^2-880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-881]]

即顛倒見、出生見，名無功果見。 ^2-881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-882]]

由所安立非正法性，授者、受者俱不能證勝進果故， ^2-882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-883]]

即不立宗見、矯亂見，名受辱見。 ^2-883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-884]]

非理興論無宜得勝故， ^2-884

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-885]]

即敬事見，名誹謗見。 ^2-885

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-886]]

所不應說強增益故，即堅固愚癡見，名不可與言見。 ^2-886

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-887]]

邪執空者不應與言，徒設多詞終無所益故， ^2-887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-888]]

即根本見，名廣大見。 ^2-888

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-889]]

由此當來諸惡見類，轉增廣故。 ^2-889

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-890]]

即上所說二十七見，皆名增上慢見， ^2-890

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-891]]

並能發起虛妄無實增上慢故。 ^2-891

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-892]]

此云何知？ ^2-892

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-893]]

由彼經中即次後說， 如是諸見十七即十。 ^2-893

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-894]]

十即十七。 ^2-894

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-895]]

二十七即一。 ^2-895

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-896]]

一即二十七故。 ^2-896

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-897]]

復次如方廣分說， 一切諸法皆無自性。 此言依何密意說？ ^2-897

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-898]]

謂無自然性故、無自體性故、無住自體故， ^2-898

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-899]]

無如愚夫所取相性故。 ^2-899

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-900]]

無自然性者， ^2-900

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-901]]

由無自然性，故說無自性， ^2-901

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-902]]

不遮待緣性，故說無自性。 ^2-902

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-903]]

無自體性者， ^2-903

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-904]]

由此自體，曾所經有， ^2-904

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-905]]

即此自體不可復有故，說為無性。 ^2-905

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-906]]

無住自體者，體雖現在未至壞相，次必當滅。 體無住義，故說無性。 ^2-906

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-907]]

無如愚夫所取相性者， ^2-907

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-908]]

如諸愚夫未見諦者，依止名言戲論熏習門，妄取諸法性相。 ^2-908

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-909]]

遠離如是所取自性，故說無性。 ^2-909

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-910]]

復次，於遍計所執自性，相無性故； ^2-910

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-911]]

於依他起自性，生無性故； ^2-911

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-912]]

於圓成實自性， 勝義無性故， ^2-912

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-913]]

更依異門顯無性義故言。 復次一切法者，即三自性， 謂遍計所執自性、 依他起自性、 圓成實自性。 ^2-913

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-914]]

遍計所執自性定無自相， 自相無故名相無性， 相無性故名為無性。 ^2-914

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-915]]

依他起自性，待眾緣故非自然生， ^2-915

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-916]]

無自然生性故，名生無性， 生無性故名為無性。 ^2-916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-917]]

圓成實自性清淨所緣故， ^2-917

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-918]]

於依他起中，無遍計所執相所顯自體故， ^2-918

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-919]]

勝義為自體故、無性所顯故， ^2-919

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-920]]

名勝義無性，勝義無性故名為無性。 ^2-920

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-921]]

由此道理，是故如來說一切法皆無自性。 ^2-921

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-922]]

非一切種，性相俱無說為無性。 ^2-922

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-923]]

又彼說言：一切諸法無生無滅、本來寂靜、自性涅槃。 此依何密意說？ ^2-923

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-924]]

如無自性，無生亦爾。 ^2-924

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-925]]

如無生，無滅亦爾。 ^2-925

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-926]]

如無生無滅，本來寂靜亦爾。 ^2-926

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-927]]

如本來寂靜，自性涅槃亦爾。 ^2-927

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-928]]

復次有四種意趣，由此意趣故，方廣分中一切如來所有意趣應隨決了。 ^2-928

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-929]]

何等為四？ ^2-929

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-930]]

謂平等意趣、 ^2-930

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-931]]

別時意趣、 ^2-931

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-932]]

別義意趣、 ^2-932

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-933]]

眾生意樂意趣。 ^2-933

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-935]]

平等意趣者，如說我於爾時曾名勝觀如來應正等覺， ^2-935

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-936]]

與彼法身無差別故。 ^2-936

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-937]]

別時意趣者，如說若有願生極樂世界皆得往生； ^2-937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-938]]

若暫得聞無垢月光如來名者，即於阿耨多羅三藐三菩提決不退轉。 ^2-938

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-939]]

如是等言，意在別時故。 ^2-939

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-940]]

別義意趣者，如說一切諸法皆無自性， ^2-940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-941]]

如是等言不可如文便取義故。 ^2-941

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-943]]

眾生意樂意趣者，謂於一善根，或時稱讚，為令歡喜勇猛修故；或時毀呰，為遮得少善生喜足故。 ^2-943

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-944]]

為貪行者稱讚佛土富樂莊嚴， ^2-944

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-945]]

為慢行者稱讚諸佛或有增勝。 ^2-945

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-946]]

為恒悔惱障修善者說如是言，於佛菩薩雖行輕毀，然彼眾生亦生天趣。 ^2-946

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-947]]

為不定種性者，捨離聲聞下劣意樂故， ^2-947

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-948]]

記大聲聞當得作佛，又說一乘更無第二。 ^2-948

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-949]]

復次有四種祕密，由此祕密故， ^2-949

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-950]]

於方廣分中，一切如來所有祕密應隨決了。 ^2-950

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-951]]

何等為四？ ^2-951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-952]]

謂令入祕密、相祕密、對治祕密、轉變祕密。 ^2-952

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-953]]

如是四種，於大乘中略攝如來一切所說祕密道理。 ^2-953

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-955]]

令入祕密者，謂於聲聞乘說色等諸法皆有自性， ^2-955

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-956]]

為令無怖畏漸入聖教故。 ^2-956

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-957]]

相祕密者，謂於三自性說一切法皆無自性、無生無滅等。 ^2-957

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-958]]

對治祕密者，謂為調伏諸過失者， ^2-958

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-959]]

如來宣說種種密教， ^2-959

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-960]]

如為對治八種障故說最上乘。 ^2-960

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-961]]

何等為八？ 謂輕佛法、懈怠、少善、生喜足、貪慢行、惡作、不定性、差別。 廣說指事，隨其所應，如四意趣說。 ^2-961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-962]]

轉變祕密者，謂經所說隱密名言。 ^2-962

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-963]]

如說：於不堅堅覺， ^2-963

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-964]]

深住於顛倒， ^2-964

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-965]]

極煩惱所惱， ^2-965

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-966]]

得最上菩提。 ^2-966

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-967]]

此中密意者， ^2-967

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-968]]

謂於不散動，起堅固勝覺。 ^2-968

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-969]]

所以者何？ ^2-969

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-970]]

堅有二義：一貞實、二散動。 ^2-970

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-971]]

由此散動令心剛逸，故亦名堅。 ^2-971

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-972]]

深住於顛倒者， ^2-972

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-973]]

謂翻常、樂、我、淨四倒為無常等， ^2-973

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-974]]

故名顛倒。 於此不退，故名深住。 ^2-974

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-975]]

極煩惱所惱者，謂於長時精勤苦行，極為勞倦所逼惱故。 ^2-975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-976]]

得最上菩提者，若具如上所說三事，定速當證無上菩提。 ^2-976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-977]]

復次方廣分中，於法三摩地善巧菩薩相，云何可知？ ^2-977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-978]]

謂由五種因故。 一剎那剎那消除一切麁重所依； ^2-978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-979]]

二出離種種想，得樂法樂； ^2-979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-980]]

三了知無量無分別相，大法光明； ^2-980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-981]]

四順清淨分無分別相，恒現在前； ^2-981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-982]]

五能攝受轉上轉勝，圓滿成就佛法身因。 ^2-982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-983]]

如是五種，依諸菩薩三種稱讚功德說， ^2-983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-984]]

謂奢摩他稱讚功德、毘鉢舍那稱讚功德、此二俱分稱讚功德。 ^2-984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-985]]

奢摩他稱讚功德復有二種： ^2-985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-986]]

一剎那剎那勝進輕安無有間缺，遍所依故， ^2-986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-987]]

剎那剎那消除一切麁重所依。 ^2-987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-988]]

二勝解諦觀，一切教法無有差別，同一味故。 ^2-988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-989]]

遠離種種蘊界處等諸義相想，得契經等憙樂法樂。 ^2-989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-990]]

毘鉢舍那稱讚功德亦有二種： ^2-990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-991]]

一隨所擇法無有間缺，不忘失故， ^2-991

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-992]]

由憶念門，於無量無分別相契經等法，以慧照了。 ^2-992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-993]]

二轉依前所有色像，無分別無加行相恒現在前。 ^2-993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-994]]

第五一種，是此二俱分稱讚功德法身者，謂所知障永斷轉依所攝。 ^2-994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-995]]

此於第十地名圓滿，於如來地名成就。 ^2-995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-996]]

為令法身速得圓滿成就故， ^2-996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-997]]

引殖轉上轉勝等流習氣，故名攝受。 ^2-997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-998]]

彼因如是五種，即顯五修能得五果。 ^2-998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-999]]

何等為五？ ^2-999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1000]]

謂息相修、和合修、無相修、無功用修、轉相修。 ^2-1000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1001]]

問：聲聞藏法、菩薩藏法等從法身所流，何故眾生以香鬘等供養菩薩藏法，便生廣大無邊福聚，非聲聞藏法耶？ ^2-1001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1002]]

答：以菩薩藏法是一切眾生利益安樂所依處故、 ^2-1002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1003]]

能建大義故、無上無量大功德聚所生處故。 ^2-1003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^2-1004]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十二 ^2-1004

## 阿毘達磨雜集論 — 3/4 ^3-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十三安慧菩薩糅釋上集論大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中得品第三之一 ^3-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-2]]

云何得決擇？ ^3-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-3]]

略說有二種，謂建立補特伽羅、建立現觀。 ^3-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-4]]

前為能證，後是所證。 ^3-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-5]]

補特伽羅雖非實有，由四種緣是故建立， ^3-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-6]]

謂言說易故、 ^3-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-7]]

順世間故、 ^3-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-8]]

離怖畏故、 ^3-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-10]]

顯示自他具德失故。 ^3-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-11]]

言說易者， ^3-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-12]]

若於無量色等差別、無量差別相想法中，總合建立一假有情， ^3-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-13]]

即呼召往來等種種言說遂不為難。 ^3-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-14]]

順世間者， ^3-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-15]]

非諸世間唯依法想而起言說，多分依有情想而起言說， ^3-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-16]]

是故聖者為化世間， ^3-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-17]]

必應同彼方便建立補特伽羅。 ^3-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-18]]

離怖畏者， ^3-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-19]]

世間有情未會甚深緣起法性， ^3-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-20]]

若聞一切有情無我， ^3-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-21]]

便生怖畏不受正化。 ^3-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-23]]

顯示自他具德失者， ^3-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-24]]

若離假立有情差別， ^3-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-25]]

唯說諸法染淨相者， ^3-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-26]]

是則一切無有差別， ^3-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-27]]

不可了知如是身中如此過失若斷未斷、如是身中如此功德若證未證， ^3-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-28]]

是故建立補特伽羅。 ^3-28

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-29]]

云何建立？ ^3-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-30]]

略有七種， ^3-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-31]]

謂病行差別故、 ^3-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-32]]

出離差別故、 ^3-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-33]]

任持差別故、 ^3-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-34]]

方便差別故、 ^3-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-35]]

果差別故、 ^3-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-37]]

界差別故、 ^3-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-38]]

修行差別故。 ^3-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-40]]

病行差別復有七種， ^3-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-42]]

謂貪行、 ^3-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-43]]

瞋行、 ^3-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-44]]

癡行、 ^3-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-45]]

慢行、 ^3-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-46]]

尋思行、 ^3-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-47]]

等分行、 ^3-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-48]]

薄塵行。 ^3-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-49]]

出離差別有三種， ^3-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-51]]

謂聲聞乘、 ^3-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-52]]

獨覺乘、 ^3-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-53]]

大乘。 ^3-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-55]]

任持差別有三種， ^3-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-56]]

謂未具資糧、 ^3-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-57]]

已具未具資糧、 ^3-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-58]]

已具資糧。 ^3-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-60]]

方便差別有二種， ^3-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-61]]

謂隨信行、 ^3-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-62]]

隨法行。 ^3-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-64]]

果差別有二十七， ^3-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-65]]

謂信解、 ^3-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-66]]

見至、 ^3-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-67]]

身證、 ^3-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-68]]

慧解脫、 ^3-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-69]]

俱解脫、 ^3-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-70]]

預流向、 ^3-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-71]]

預流果、 ^3-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-72]]

一來向、 ^3-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-73]]

一來果、 ^3-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-74]]

不還向、 ^3-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-75]]

不還果、 ^3-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-76]]

阿羅漢向、 ^3-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-77]]

阿羅漢果、 ^3-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-78]]

極七返有、 ^3-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-79]]

家家、 ^3-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-80]]

一間、 ^3-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-81]]

中般涅槃、 ^3-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-82]]

生般涅槃、 ^3-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-83]]

無行般涅槃、 ^3-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-84]]

有行般涅槃、 ^3-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-85]]

上流、 ^3-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-86]]

退法阿羅漢、 ^3-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-87]]

思法阿羅漢、 ^3-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-88]]

護法阿羅漢、 ^3-88

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-89]]

住不動阿羅漢、 ^3-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-90]]

堪達阿羅漢、 ^3-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-91]]

不動法阿羅漢。 ^3-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-93]]

界差別者， ^3-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-94]]

謂欲界異生有學無學。 ^3-94

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-95]]

如欲界有，三色界無色界亦爾。 ^3-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-96]]

又有欲色界菩薩， ^3-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-97]]

又有欲界獨覺， ^3-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-98]]

不可思議如來。 ^3-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-100]]

修行差別略有五種： ^3-100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-101]]

一勝解行菩薩、 ^3-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-102]]

二增上意樂行菩薩、 ^3-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-103]]

三有相行菩薩、 ^3-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-104]]

四無相行菩薩、 ^3-104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-105]]

五無功用行菩薩。 ^3-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-106]]

如是等補特伽羅無量差別。 ^3-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-107]]

如貪行者，乃至尋思行者亦爾，各隨自境猛利長時，如理配釋。 ^3-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-108]]

等分行補特伽羅者， ^3-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-109]]

謂住自性位煩惱， ^3-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-110]]

遠離猛劣，住平等位諸煩惱故， ^3-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-111]]

隨境界勢力煩惱現行故。 ^3-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-112]]

薄塵行補特伽羅者， ^3-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-113]]

謂住自性位微薄煩惱。 ^3-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-114]]

如前所說自性位煩惱相，今此煩惱望彼是微薄故。 ^3-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-116]]

雖於增上所緣境界，而微薄性煩惱現行，昔所修習勝對治力所摧伏故。 ^3-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-117]]

聲聞乘補特伽羅者， ^3-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-118]]

謂住聲聞法性，若定不定性， ^3-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-119]]

是鈍根， ^3-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-120]]

自求解脫發弘正願， ^3-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-121]]

修厭離貪解脫意樂， ^3-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-122]]

以聲聞藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行，得盡苦際。 ^3-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-123]]

當知此中以種性、根、願、意樂、境界、行果差別說聲聞乘。 ^3-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-124]]

對獨覺菩薩根性，說此為鈍， ^3-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-125]]

若不爾即與隨法行等利根言相違。 ^3-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-126]]

獨覺乘補特伽羅者， ^3-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-127]]

謂住獨覺法性，若定不定性， ^3-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-128]]

是中根， ^3-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-129]]

自求解脫發弘正願， ^3-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-130]]

修厭離貪解脫意樂及修獨證菩提意樂， ^3-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-131]]

即聲聞藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行。 ^3-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-132]]

或先未起順決擇分、 ^3-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-133]]

或先已起順決擇分， ^3-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-134]]

或先未得果、或先已得果， ^3-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-135]]

出無佛世，唯內思惟聖道現前， ^3-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-136]]

或如麟角獨住、 ^3-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-137]]

或復獨勝部行， ^3-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-138]]

得盡苦際。 ^3-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-139]]

若先未起順決擇分亦不得果，如是方成麟角獨住， ^3-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-140]]

所餘當成獨勝部行。 ^3-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-141]]

大乘補特伽羅者， ^3-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-142]]

謂住菩薩法性，若定不定性， ^3-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-143]]

是利根， ^3-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-144]]

為求解脫一切有情，發弘大願，修無住處涅槃意樂， ^3-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-145]]

以菩薩藏為所緣境，精進修行法隨法行，成熟眾生 ^3-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-146]]

修淨佛土，得受大記， ^3-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-147]]

證成無上正等菩提。 ^3-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-148]]

得受大記者，謂住第八菩薩地，證得無生法忍故。 ^3-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-149]]

未具資糧補特伽羅者， ^3-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-150]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起軟品清信勝解， ^3-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-151]]

成就軟品順解脫分，未定生時。 ^3-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-152]]

已具未具資糧補特伽羅者， ^3-152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-153]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起中品清信勝解， ^3-153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-154]]

成就中品順解脫分，已定生時。 ^3-154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-155]]

已具資糧補特伽羅者， ^3-155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-156]]

謂緣諦增上法為境，發起上品清信勝解， ^3-156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-157]]

成就上品順解脫分，即此生時。 ^3-157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-158]]

又未具資糧者，謂緣諦增上法為境， ^3-158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-159]]

於諸諦中成就下品諦察法忍， ^3-159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-160]]

成就下品順決擇分，未定生時。 ^3-160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-161]]

已具未具資糧者， ^3-161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-162]]

謂緣諦增上法為境， ^3-162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-163]]

於諸諦中成就中品諦察法忍， ^3-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-164]]

成就中品順決擇分，已定生時。 ^3-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-165]]

已具資糧者，謂緣諦增上法為境， ^3-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-166]]

於諸諦中成就上品諦察法忍， ^3-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-167]]

成就上品順決擇分，即此生時。 ^3-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-168]]

如是三種補特伽羅， ^3-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-170]]

由成就順解脫分、順決擇分各三品故。 ^3-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-171]]

約能引生順決擇分及諦現觀，如其次第，未定、已定、即此生時。 ^3-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-172]]

於諦增上法清信勝相，是順解脫分。 ^3-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-173]]

即於此法諦察法忍相，是順決擇分。 ^3-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-174]]

如其次第，信增上故、慧增上故。 ^3-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-175]]

此中三品順決擇分者，謂除世第一法， ^3-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-176]]

由世第一法性唯一剎那必不相續， ^3-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-177]]

即此生時定入現觀， ^3-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-178]]

非前位故。 ^3-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-179]]

從下中品順解脫分、順決擇分有可退義， ^3-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-180]]

此唯退現行非退習氣， ^3-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-181]]

已依涅槃先起善根者不復新起故。 ^3-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-182]]

依此下品順解脫分善根， ^3-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-183]]

薄伽梵說： ^3-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-184]]

若有具世間，增上品正見， ^3-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-185]]

雖經歷千生，不墮三惡趣。 ^3-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-186]]

又有四種順解脫分： ^3-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-187]]

一者依憑順解脫分、 ^3-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-188]]

二者勝解順解脫分、 ^3-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-189]]

三者愛樂順解脫分、 ^3-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-190]]

四者趣證順解脫分。 ^3-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-191]]

從善法欲乃至為求解脫所有善根，皆名依憑順解脫分。 ^3-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-192]]

於彼相應教法所有勝解俱行善根，是名勝解順解脫分。 ^3-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-193]]

緣解脫境作意相續清淨喜俱所有善根，是名愛樂順解脫分。 ^3-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-194]]

即於此生決定發起順決擇分所有善根，是名趣證順解脫分。 ^3-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-195]]

復有六種順決擇分， ^3-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-196]]

謂隨順順決擇分、 ^3-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-197]]

勝進順決擇分、 ^3-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-198]]

通達順決擇分、 ^3-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-199]]

餘轉順決擇分、 ^3-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-200]]

一生順決擇分、 ^3-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-201]]

一座順決擇分。 ^3-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-202]]

若最初所起緣諦境行下品善根，是名隨順順決擇分。 ^3-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-203]]

即此善根轉成中品，是名勝進順決擇分， ^3-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-204]]

望前下品是增勝故。 ^3-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-205]]

即此善根增至上品， ^3-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-206]]

於此生中決定堪能通達諦理，是名通達順決擇分。 ^3-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-207]]

又即此位中，不定種性者為迴向最勝菩提， ^3-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-208]]

是名餘轉順決擇分。 ^3-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-209]]

若於此生定能通達，是名一生順決擇分。 ^3-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-210]]

若於此座定能通達，是名一座順決擇分。 ^3-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-211]]

隨信行補特伽羅者， ^3-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-212]]

謂資糧已具，性是鈍根，隨順他教修諦現觀。 ^3-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-213]]

隨法行補特伽羅者， ^3-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-214]]

謂資糧已具，性是利根， ^3-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-215]]

自然隨順諦增上法修諦現觀。 ^3-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-216]]

信解補特伽羅者， ^3-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-217]]

謂隨信行已至果位。 ^3-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-218]]

見至補特伽羅者， ^3-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-219]]

謂隨法行已至果位。 ^3-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-220]]

身證補特伽羅者， ^3-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-221]]

謂諸有學已具證得八解脫定， ^3-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-222]]

即不還果說名身證， ^3-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-223]]

由身證得八解脫定具足住故。 ^3-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-224]]

八解脫者，謂有色觀諸色等， ^3-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-225]]

後當廣說。 ^3-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-226]]

慧解脫補特伽羅者， ^3-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-227]]

謂已盡諸漏，而未具證八解脫定， ^3-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-228]]

唯究竟斷慧所對治煩惱障故。 ^3-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-229]]

俱分解脫補特伽羅者， ^3-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-230]]

謂已斷諸漏及具證八解脫定， ^3-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-231]]

由煩惱障分及定障分俱得解脫故。 ^3-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-232]]

預流果向補特伽羅者， ^3-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-233]]

謂住順決擇分位，及住見道十五心剎那位。 ^3-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-234]]

此中意說， ^3-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-235]]

始從一座順決擇分乃至未得初果， ^3-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-236]]

皆名預流果向。 ^3-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-237]]

豫流果補特伽羅者， ^3-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-238]]

謂住見道第十六心剎那位。 ^3-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-239]]

即此見道，亦名入正性決定， ^3-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-240]]

亦名於法現觀。 ^3-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-241]]

問：誰於見道最後心位得初果耶？ ^3-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-242]]

答：若於欲界未離欲者，後入正性決定得預流果。 ^3-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-243]]

謂次第者，雖少分離欲亦名未離欲，彼後入正性決定， ^3-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-244]]

至第十六心位得預流果。 ^3-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-245]]

若倍離欲者，後入正性決定得一來果。 ^3-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-246]]

謂先用世間道已斷欲界修道所斷六品煩惱，名倍離欲。 ^3-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-247]]

彼後入正性決定，至第十六心位得一來果。 ^3-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-248]]

若已離欲者，後入正性決定得不還果。 ^3-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-249]]

謂先用世俗道已斷欲界修道所斷九品煩惱，名已離欲。 ^3-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-250]]

彼後入正性決定，至第十六心位得不還果。 ^3-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-251]]

問：若已永斷見道所斷一切煩惱得預流果， ^3-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-252]]

何故但言永斷三結得預流果耶？ ^3-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-253]]

答：最勝所攝故， ^3-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-254]]

由此三種障解脫得最為殊勝。 ^3-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-255]]

所以者何？ ^3-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-256]]

於解脫是不發趣因故、 ^3-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-257]]

雖已發趣復為邪出離因故、 ^3-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-258]]

及不正出離因故、 ^3-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-259]]

由薩迦耶見執五取蘊為我我所深生愛樂故， ^3-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-260]]

於大苦聚不生厭背、 ^3-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-261]]

於勝解脫無發趣心。 ^3-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-262]]

或有眾生雖已發趣解脫， ^3-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-263]]

然由戒禁取及疑，僻執邪道疑正道故， ^3-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-264]]

便邪出離及不正出離。 ^3-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-265]]

又此三結 ^3-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-266]]

是迷所知境因故、 ^3-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-267]]

迷見因故、 ^3-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-268]]

迷對治因故。 ^3-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-269]]

所以者何？ ^3-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-270]]

由薩迦耶見迷所知境， ^3-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-271]]

於大苦聚虛妄增益我我所相故。 ^3-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-273]]

由戒禁取迷能知見， 於顛倒見謂為清淨出離因故。 ^3-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-275]]

由疑迷正對治，於三寶所不決定故。 ^3-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-276]]

一來果向補特伽羅者， ^3-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-277]]

謂於修道中已斷欲界五品煩惱安住彼道。 ^3-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-278]]

所以者何？ ^3-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-279]]

由見道後已斷欲界乃至中中品煩惱及住彼斷道故。 ^3-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-280]]

一來果補特伽羅者， ^3-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-281]]

謂於修道中已斷欲界第六品煩惱安住彼道。 ^3-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-282]]

所以者何？ ^3-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-283]]

由已永斷中軟品煩惱，斷道究竟建立此故。 ^3-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-284]]

不還果向補特伽羅者， ^3-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-285]]

謂於修道中已斷欲界第七第八品煩惱安住彼道。 ^3-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-286]]

所以者何？ ^3-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-287]]

由一來果後已斷欲界軟上軟中品煩惱， ^3-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-288]]

及住彼斷道建立此故。 ^3-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-289]]

不還果補特伽羅者， ^3-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-290]]

謂於修道中已斷欲界第九品煩惱安住彼道。 ^3-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-291]]

所以者何？ ^3-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-292]]

由彼永斷欲界軟軟品煩惱，斷道究竟建立此故。 ^3-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-293]]

問：若已永斷一切見道所斷煩惱，及已永斷欲界修道所斷一切煩惱得不還果， ^3-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-294]]

何故但言永斷五順下分結得不還果耶？ ^3-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-295]]

答：最勝所攝故。 ^3-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-296]]

云何最勝？ ^3-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-297]]

由此五結能為下趣下界勝因，故名最勝。 ^3-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-298]]

所以者何？ ^3-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-299]]

下趣者，謂地獄、畜生、餓鬼。 ^3-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-300]]

下界者，謂欲界。 ^3-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-301]]

以薩迦耶見、 ^3-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-302]]

戒禁取、 ^3-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-303]]

疑為最勝因， 令諸有情不越下趣故。 ^3-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-304]]

以貪欲瞋恚為最勝因，令諸有情下越下界故。 ^3-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-305]]

阿羅漢果向補特伽羅者， ^3-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-306]]

謂已永斷有頂八品煩惱安住彼道。 ^3-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-307]]

阿羅漢果補特伽羅者，謂已永斷有頂第九品煩惱安住彼究竟道。 ^3-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-308]]

問：若阿羅漢永斷三界一切煩惱， ^3-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-309]]

何故但言永斷一切五順上分結得阿羅漢果耶？ ^3-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-310]]

答：最勝所攝故。 ^3-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-311]]

云何最勝？ ^3-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-312]]

由此五結是取上分因及不捨上分因， ^3-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-313]]

故名最勝。 ^3-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-314]]

所以者何？ ^3-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-315]]

由色無色愛取欲界 ^3-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-316]]

上色無色界生故， ^3-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-317]]

由掉慢無明不捨此上生故， ^3-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-318]]

以愛慢疑上靜慮者為彼所惱故。 ^3-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-319]]

極七返有補特伽羅者， ^3-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-320]]

謂即豫流。 於人天生往來雜受，極至七返得盡苦際。 ^3-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-321]]

家家補特伽羅者，謂即預流。 或於天上或於人中，從家至家得盡苦際。 ^3-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-322]]

所以者何？ ^3-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-323]]

即預流果進至一來果向，或於天上或於人中，決定往來極受二有方般涅槃故。 ^3-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-324]]

一間補特伽羅者，謂即一來。 或於天上唯受一有，得盡苦際。 ^3-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-325]]

所以者何？ ^3-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-326]]

即一來果進至不還果向，或於天上唯受一有，得般涅槃故。 ^3-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-327]]

唯有一隙，容此一生，故名一間。 ^3-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-328]]

中般涅槃補特伽羅者， ^3-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-330]]

謂生結已斷、起結未斷， ^3-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-331]]

或中有纔起，即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-332]]

或中有起已，為趣生有，纔起思惟，即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-333]]

或思惟已發趣生有，未到生有即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-334]]

此中顯示三種中般， ^3-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-335]]

由煩惱力往趣生處令生有相續， ^3-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-336]]

此煩惱已盡；唯由隨眠力令命終後諸蘊續起， ^3-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-337]]

此隨眠餘猶未盡。 或中有纔起， ^3-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-338]]

由串習力聖道現前 ^3-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-339]]

斷餘隨眠， ^3-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-341]]

即於此位入般涅槃。 ^3-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-342]]

或中有起已，為往生有纔發思惟，聖道現前斷餘隨眠，入般涅槃。 ^3-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-343]]

或思惟已往生有處， ^3-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-344]]

未得生有， ^3-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-345]]

聖道現前斷餘隨眠，入般涅槃。 ^3-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-346]]

如是三種，望生有處，未發、纔發、已遠去位差別建立， ^3-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-347]]

隨順《七善丈夫趣經》。 ^3-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-348]]

生般涅槃補特伽羅者， ^3-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-349]]

謂二結俱未斷，纔生色界已，即便聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-350]]

無行般涅槃補特伽羅者， ^3-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-351]]

謂生彼已，不由加行聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-352]]

不由加行者， ^3-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-353]]

由宿串習力， 無漏聖道任運現前， ^3-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-354]]

無功用故。 ^3-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-355]]

上流補特伽羅者， ^3-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-356]]

謂於色界地地中皆受生已，乃至最後入色究竟， ^3-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-357]]

於彼無漏聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-358]]

復有乃至往到有頂，聖道現前，得盡苦際。 ^3-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-359]]

此中顯示二種上流，一極至色究竟、二極至有頂。 ^3-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-360]]

極至色究竟者，謂多愛味補特伽羅，由多生起軟等靜慮差別愛味故， ^3-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-361]]

始從梵眾天乃至色究竟，於一切處次第各受一生，乃至最後入色究竟得般涅槃。 ^3-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-363]]

極至有頂者，謂不雜修第四靜慮，唯避淨居，如前次第生一切處，乃至有頂方般涅槃。 ^3-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-364]]

又雜修第四靜慮， ^3-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-365]]

有五品差別： ^3-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-366]]

一下品修、 ^3-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-367]]

二中品修、 ^3-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-368]]

三上品修、 ^3-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-369]]

四上勝品修、 ^3-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-370]]

五上極品修。 ^3-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-371]]

由此五品雜修第四靜慮故，如其次第生五淨居。 ^3-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-372]]

退法阿羅漢者， ^3-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-373]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散、 ^3-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-374]]

若思惟若不思惟， ^3-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-375]]

皆可退失現法樂住。 ^3-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-376]]

思惟者，欲害自身。 不思惟者，不欲害自身。 ^3-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-377]]

退現法樂住者， ^3-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-378]]

謂退世間靜慮等定。 ^3-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-379]]

思法阿羅漢者， ^3-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-380]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散、 ^3-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-381]]

若不思惟，即可退失現法樂住。 若思惟已，能不退失。 ^3-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-382]]

護法阿羅漢者， ^3-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-383]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散便可退失現法樂住， ^3-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-384]]

若不遊散即能不退住。 ^3-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-385]]

不動阿羅漢者， ^3-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-386]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散， ^3-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-387]]

皆能不退現法樂住，亦不能練根。 ^3-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-388]]

練根者，謂轉下鈍根成上利根。 ^3-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-389]]

是故不動法不說能練根， ^3-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-390]]

性是利根故。 ^3-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-391]]

堪達阿羅漢者， ^3-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-392]]

謂鈍根性，若遊散若不遊散， ^3-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-393]]

皆能不退現法樂住，堪能練根。 ^3-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-394]]

不動法阿羅漢者，謂利根性， ^3-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-395]]

若遊散若不遊散，皆能不退現法樂住。 ^3-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-396]]

欲界異生補特伽羅者，謂於欲界若生若長，不得聖法。 ^3-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-397]]

欲界有學補特伽羅者，謂於欲界若生若長， ^3-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-398]]

已得聖法，猶有餘結。 ^3-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-399]]

欲界無學補特伽羅者，謂於欲界若生若長， ^3-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-400]]

已得聖法，無有餘結。 ^3-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-401]]

如欲界有三，如是色無色界各有三種，隨相應知。 ^3-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-402]]

欲色界菩薩者， ^3-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-403]]

謂與滅離無色界生靜慮相應住靜慮樂， ^3-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-404]]

而生欲界或生色界。 ^3-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-405]]

問：何緣菩薩不生無色界？ ^3-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-406]]

答：若已證得最勝威德菩薩， ^3-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-407]]

凡所受生皆欲利益安樂眾生，以無色界非成熟眾生處故。 ^3-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-408]]

滅離無色界生靜慮者，謂能除遣無色界生所有勝定。 ^3-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-409]]

住靜慮樂者，謂不退靜慮。 ^3-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-410]]

由此菩薩善巧迴轉故，為欲成熟所化有情，或生欲界或生色界。 ^3-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-411]]

欲界獨覺者，謂無佛出世時生於欲界， ^3-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-412]]

自然證得獨覺菩提。 ^3-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-413]]

不思議如來者，謂且於欲界始從示現安住覩史多天妙寶宮殿，乃至示現大般涅槃， ^3-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-414]]

示現一切諸佛菩薩所行大行。 ^3-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-415]]

一切菩薩所行者，謂從示現覩史多天宮，乃至現大神變降伏魔軍。 ^3-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-416]]

諸佛所行者，謂從示現成等正覺， ^3-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-417]]

乃至示現大般涅槃。 ^3-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-418]]

勝解行菩薩者， ^3-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-419]]

謂住勝解行地中，成就菩薩下中上忍。 ^3-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-420]]

由其安住菩薩種姓，始從初發大菩提願，乃至未入極歡喜地， ^3-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-421]]

未得出世真實內證故，名勝解行菩薩。 ^3-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-422]]

增上意樂行菩薩者， ^3-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-423]]

謂十地中所有菩薩， ^3-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-424]]

由已證得出世內證清淨意樂故。 ^3-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-425]]

有相行菩薩者， ^3-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-426]]

謂住極喜、離垢、發光、焰慧、極難勝、現前地中所有菩薩， ^3-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-427]]

由此六地雖不喜樂，而為諸相所間雜故。 ^3-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-428]]

無相行菩薩者， ^3-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-429]]

謂住遠行地中所有菩薩， ^3-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-430]]

由此菩薩若作功用乃至隨其欲樂，能令諸相不現行故。 ^3-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-431]]

無功用行菩薩者， ^3-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-432]]

謂住不動、善慧、法雲地中所有菩薩， ^3-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-433]]

由此菩薩已得純熟無分別智故。 ^3-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-434]]

復次如說預流補特伽羅，此有二種：一漸出離、二頓出離。 ^3-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-435]]

漸出離者，如前廣說。 ^3-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-436]]

頓出離者， ^3-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-437]]

謂入諦現觀已，依止未至定發出世間道， ^3-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-438]]

頓斷三界一切煩惱， ^3-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-439]]

品品別斷唯立二果，謂預流果、阿羅漢果。 ^3-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-440]]

品品別斷者，謂先頓斷欲色無色界修道所斷上上品隨眠， ^3-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-441]]

如是乃至軟軟品。 ^3-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-442]]

頓斷三界者，如見道所斷， ^3-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-443]]

非如世間道界地漸次品品別斷。 ^3-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-444]]

此義以何為證？ ^3-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-445]]

如《指端經》說：諸所有色乃至識，若過去若未來若現在， ^3-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-446]]

廣說乃至若遠若近，總此一切 ^3-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-447]]

略為一分一團一積一聚。 ^3-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-448]]

如是略已， ^3-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-450]]

應觀一切皆是無常、一切皆苦，乃至廣說。 ^3-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-451]]

依如是觀，但可建立初後二果。 ^3-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-452]]

由此二果如其次第，永斷三界一切見修所斷煩惱無餘所顯故， ^3-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-453]]

不立第二第三兩果。 ^3-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-454]]

由此二果，已見諦者唯斷欲界修道所斷，有餘無餘所顯故。 ^3-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-455]]

又依如是頓出離者，如來於分別經中，預流果無間即建立阿羅漢果， ^3-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-456]]

如是補特伽羅多於現法或臨終時善辦聖旨。 ^3-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-458]]

設不能辦，由願力故， ^3-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-459]]

即以願力還生欲界， ^3-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-461]]

出無佛世成獨勝果。 ^3-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-462]]

設不辦者，未能無餘離諸欲故，即以願力生欲界者，彼能速證般涅槃故。 ^3-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-463]]

建立現觀略有十種， ^3-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-464]]

謂法現觀、 ^3-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-465]]

義現觀、 ^3-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-466]]

真現觀、 ^3-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-467]]

後現觀、 ^3-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-468]]

寶現觀、 ^3-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-469]]

不行現觀、 ^3-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-470]]

究竟現觀、 ^3-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-471]]

聲聞現觀、 ^3-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-472]]

獨覺現觀、 ^3-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-473]]

菩薩現觀。 ^3-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-474]]

法現觀者，謂於諸諦增上法中已得上品清信勝解隨信而行。 ^3-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-476]]

所以者何？ ^3-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-479]]

由於諸諦增上契經等法中， ^3-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-480]]

從聞他音增上緣力， ^3-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-481]]

已得最後順解脫分善根所攝上品清信勝解。 ^3-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-482]]

由得如是清信勝解故， 說名以法現觀現觀諸諦。 ^3-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-483]]

義現觀者， ^3-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-485]]

謂即於諸諦增上法中已得上品， ^3-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-486]]

於諸諦境諦察法忍。 ^3-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-487]]

此忍居順決擇分位。 ^3-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-488]]

所以者何？ ^3-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-491]]

由即於如上所說法中如理作意增上緣力， ^3-491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-492]]

於苦等諦境已得最後順決擇分善根所攝上品諦察法忍。 ^3-492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-493]]

此諦察法忍，由三種如理作意所顯發故，復成三品， ^3-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-494]]

謂上軟、上中、上上。 ^3-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-495]]

上軟者，謂即此生時軟(煖)位。 ^3-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-496]]

上中者，謂頂、忍位。 ^3-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-497]]

上上者，謂世第一法位。 ^3-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-498]]

真現觀者，謂已得見道十六心剎那位所有聖道， ^3-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-499]]

又於見道中得現觀邊安立諦世俗智。 ^3-499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-504]]

由出世智增上緣力長養彼種子故，名得此智而不現前， ^3-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-505]]

以見道十六心剎那無有間斷，不容現起世間心故。 ^3-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-506]]

於修道位，此世俗智方現在前。 ^3-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-507]]

後現觀者，謂一切修道， ^3-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-508]]

由見道後一切世間出世間道皆名後現觀故。 ^3-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-509]]

寶現觀者， ^3-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-510]]

謂於佛證淨、 ^3-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-511]]

於法證淨、 於僧證淨。 ^3-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-512]]

由佛聖弟子於三寶所已得決定證清淨信， ^3-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-514]]

謂薄伽梵是真正等覺者、 ^3-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-515]]

法毘奈耶是真善妙說、 ^3-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-516]]

聖弟子眾是真淨行者。 ^3-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-518]]

不行現觀者， ^3-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-519]]

謂已證得無作律儀故，雖居學位，而謂我今已盡地獄、畜生、餓鬼顛墜惡趣， ^3-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-520]]

我不復能造惡趣業感惡趣異熟。 ^3-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-521]]

已得無作律儀者，謂已證得聖所愛戒所攝律儀。 ^3-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-522]]

由得此故，此所對治地獄異熟等必不復行。 ^3-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-523]]

由地獄等永盡不行故，名不行現觀。 ^3-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-524]]

究竟現觀者，如道諦中究竟道說， ^3-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-525]]

謂已息一切麁重、已得一切離繫得，如是等。 ^3-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-526]]

聲聞現觀者，謂前所說七種現觀， ^3-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-527]]

從聞他音而證得故，名聲聞現觀。 ^3-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-528]]

獨覺現觀者， ^3-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-529]]

謂前所說七種現觀，不由他音而證得故，名獨覺現觀。 ^3-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-530]]

菩薩現觀者， ^3-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-531]]

謂諸菩薩於前所說七現觀中， ^3-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-532]]

起修集忍而不作證， ^3-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-533]]

為於聲聞獨覺調伏方便中得善巧故、 ^3-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-534]]

哀戀眾生不於下乘而出離故， ^3-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-535]]

然於菩薩極喜地中入諸菩薩正性決定，是名菩薩現觀。 ^3-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-536]]

已說現觀，差別今當說。 ^3-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-537]]

問：聲聞菩薩現觀有何差別？ ^3-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-538]]

答：略說有十一種， ^3-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-539]]

謂境界差別、 ^3-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-540]]

任持差別、 ^3-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-541]]

通達差別、 ^3-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-542]]

誓願差別、 ^3-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-543]]

出離差別、 ^3-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-544]]

攝受差別、 ^3-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-545]]

建立差別、 眷屬差別、 ^3-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-546]]

勝生差別、 ^3-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-547]]

生差別、 ^3-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-548]]

果差別。 ^3-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-549]]

境界差別者，謂緣方廣大乘為境故。 ^3-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-550]]

任持差別者， ^3-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-551]]

謂滿大劫阿僧企耶，福智資糧圓滿故。 ^3-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-552]]

通達差別者， ^3-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-553]]

謂由補特伽羅法無我理增上法方便所引出世間智，俱通達二無我故。 ^3-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-554]]

誓願差別者， ^3-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-555]]

謂能通達一切有情與己平等，猶如自身誓願攝益故。 ^3-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-556]]

出離差別者， ^3-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-557]]

謂依十地而出離故。 ^3-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-558]]

攝受差別者， ^3-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-559]]

謂無住涅槃所攝受故。 ^3-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-560]]

建立差別者， ^3-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-561]]

謂善修治諸佛淨土故。 ^3-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-563]]

眷屬差別者， ^3-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-564]]

謂攝受一切所化眾生為眷屬故。 ^3-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-565]]

勝生差別者， ^3-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-566]]

謂如世間腹所孕子， ^3-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-567]]

生差別者， ^3-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-568]]

謂於如來大集會中生故。 ^3-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-569]]

果差別者，復有十種，謂轉依差別、 ^3-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-570]]

功德圓滿差別、 ^3-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-571]]

五相差別、 ^3-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-572]]

三身差別、 ^3-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-573]]

涅槃差別、 ^3-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-574]]

證得和合智用差別、 ^3-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-575]]

障清淨差別、 ^3-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-576]]

和合作業差別、 ^3-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-577]]

方便示現成等正覺入般涅槃差別、 ^3-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-578]]

五種拔濟差別。 ^3-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-579]]

轉依差別者， ^3-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-580]]

謂染不染一切種所依麁重永斷故、 ^3-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-581]]

一切無上功德所依永轉故。 ^3-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-582]]

功德圓滿差別者， ^3-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-583]]

謂力、無所畏、不共佛法等無邊功德永成滿故。 ^3-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-584]]

五相差別者， ^3-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-585]]

謂清淨等五相差別。 ^3-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-586]]

一清淨差別， ^3-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-587]]

謂永斷一切煩惱并習氣故。 ^3-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-588]]

二圓淨差別， ^3-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-589]]

謂遍修治佛淨土故。 ^3-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-590]]

三身差別，謂法身圓滿故。 ^3-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-591]]

四受用差別， ^3-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-592]]

謂一切時處大集會與諸菩薩受用種種大法樂故。 ^3-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-593]]

五業差別， ^3-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-594]]

謂隨其所應起種種變化，遍於十方無量無邊諸世界中作諸佛事故。 ^3-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-595]]

三身差別者， ^3-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-596]]

謂證得圓滿自性、受用、變化身故。 ^3-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-597]]

涅槃差別者， ^3-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-598]]

謂於無餘涅槃界為欲利樂一切有情，一切功德無斷絕故。 ^3-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-599]]

證得和合智用差別者， ^3-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-600]]

謂證得最極清淨法界一味故，於彼能依一切種妙智用一一佛功能，等一切佛功能故。 ^3-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-601]]

障清淨差別者， ^3-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-602]]

謂永斷一切煩惱障、所知障故。 ^3-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-603]]

和合作業差別者， ^3-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-604]]

謂化導一一有情作用，皆一切佛增上力故。 ^3-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-605]]

方便示現成等正覺入般涅槃差別者， ^3-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-606]]

謂於十方一切世界，隨其所應，乃至後際數數示現成正覺等， ^3-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-607]]

令一切所化眾生成熟解脫故。 ^3-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-608]]

五種拔濟差別者， ^3-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-609]]

謂拔濟災橫等五事。 ^3-609
