![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-610]]

一拔濟災橫， ^3-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-611]]

謂如來入城邑等時，令盲聾等得眼耳等。 ^3-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-612]]

二拔濟非方便， ^3-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-613]]

謂令得世間正見，遠離一切邪惡見故。 ^3-613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-614]]

三拔濟惡趣， ^3-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-615]]

謂令生見道，越諸惡趣故。 ^3-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-616]]

四拔濟薩迦耶，謂令證阿羅漢果，永脫三界故。 ^3-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-617]]

五拔濟乘， ^3-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-618]]

謂令諸菩薩不樂下乘故。 ^3-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-619]]

問：如經說四無量等最勝功德，何現觀所攝耶？ ^3-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-620]]

答：後現觀、究竟現觀所攝。 ^3-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-621]]

所以者何？ ^3-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-622]]

如是最勝功德， ^3-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-623]]

諸聖弟子等或於修道或究竟道之所發起， ^3-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-624]]

是故二現觀所攝。 ^3-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-625]]

彼復云何？ ^3-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-626]]

謂無量解脫、 勝處、 遍處、 ^3-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-627]]

無諍、願、智、無礙解、神通、 ^3-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-628]]

相隨好、清淨、力、無畏、念住、 ^3-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-629]]

不護、無忘失法、永斷習氣、大悲、 ^3-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-630]]

十八不共佛法、 ^3-630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-631]]

一切種妙智， ^3-631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-632]]

如是等功德， ^3-632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-633]]

如來於諸經中， 或依聲聞乘說、 ^3-633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-634]]

或依大乘說。 ^3-634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-635]]

此諸功德隨其所應，略以五門顯示其相， ^3-635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-636]]

謂所依、境界、行相、自體、助伴。 ^3-636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-637]]

無量者，謂四無量：一慈無量、二悲無量、三喜無量、四捨無量。 ^3-637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-638]]

慈云何？ ^3-638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-639]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-640]]

於諸有情與樂相應意樂住具足中， ^3-640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-641]]

若定若慧及彼相應諸心心法。 ^3-641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-642]]

此中顯慈無量，以靜慮為所依， ^3-642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-643]]

有情為境界， ^3-643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-644]]

願彼與樂相應為行相， ^3-644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-645]]

定慧為自體， ^3-645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-646]]

一切功德皆奢摩他、毘鉢舍那所攝故， ^3-646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-647]]

諸心心法為助伴。 ^3-647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-648]]

當知悲等一切功德，隨其所應亦爾。 ^3-648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-649]]

悲云何？ ^3-649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-650]]

謂於諸有情離苦意樂住具足中， ^3-650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-651]]

若定若慧，餘如前說，所依、自體、助伴與慈相似故。 ^3-651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-652]]

喜云何？ ^3-652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-653]]

謂於諸有情不離樂意樂住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-654]]

捨云何？ ^3-654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-655]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-656]]

於諸有情利益意樂住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-657]]

利益意樂者， ^3-657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-658]]

謂於與樂相應等有情所棄捨愛等作是思惟， ^3-658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-659]]

當令彼解脫煩惱。 如是意樂，名捨行相。 ^3-659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-660]]

利益意樂行相圓滿， ^3-660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-661]]

名住具足。 ^3-661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-662]]

解脫者，謂八解脫，廣說如經。 ^3-662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-663]]

云何有色觀諸色解脫？ ^3-663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-664]]

謂依止靜慮，於內未伏見者色想，或現安立見者色想，觀所見色住具足中，若定若慧及彼相應諸心心法，乃至為解脫變化障。 ^3-664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-667]]

有色者，謂於內身未依無色定伏除見者色想故。 ^3-667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-668]]

或見者色想， ^3-668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-669]]

安立現前故。 ^3-669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-671]]

觀諸色者， ^3-671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-672]]

謂以意解觀見好惡等色故。 ^3-672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-673]]

解脫者， ^3-673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-674]]

謂能解脫一切變化障故。 ^3-674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-675]]

云何內無色想觀外諸色解脫？ ^3-675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-676]]

謂於內已伏見者色想，或現安立見者無色想， ^3-676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-677]]

觀所見色住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-678]]

內無色想者， ^3-678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-679]]

謂於內身已依無色定 ^3-679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-680]]

伏除見者色想故。 ^3-680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-681]]

或見者無色想， ^3-681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-682]]

安立現前故。 ^3-682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-683]]

謂見者名想現在前行， ^3-683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-684]]

餘如前釋。 ^3-684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-685]]

云何淨解脫身作證具足住？ ^3-685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-687]]

謂於內淨不淨諸色， ^3-687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-688]]

已得展轉相待想、展轉相入想、展轉一味想故。 ^3-688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-689]]

於彼已得具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說， ^3-689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-690]]

乃至為解脫淨不淨變化煩惱生起障。 ^3-690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-691]]

此中顯示於淨不淨諸色，依展轉相待想、展轉相入想， ^3-691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-692]]

得展轉一味想。 ^3-692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-693]]

所以者何？ ^3-693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-694]]

待諸淨色 ^3-694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-695]]

於餘色中謂為不淨， ^3-695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-696]]

待不淨色於餘色中謂為清淨， ^3-696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-697]]

非不相待。 ^3-697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-698]]

何以故？ ^3-698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-699]]

唯見一類時， ^3-699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-700]]

淨不淨覺無故。 ^3-700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-701]]

又於淨中不淨性所隨入， ^3-701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-702]]

於不淨中淨性所隨入。 ^3-702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-703]]

何以故？ ^3-703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-705]]

於薄皮所覆共謂為淨中， ^3-705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-706]]

現有髮毛等三十六種不淨物故。 ^3-706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-707]]

如是展轉總一切色， ^3-707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-708]]

合為一味清淨想。 ^3-708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-709]]

解如是已，得隨所樂色解脫自在者， ^3-709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-710]]

能斷淨不淨色變化障 ^3-710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-711]]

及於此中煩惱生起障。 ^3-711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-712]]

何等名於變化煩惱？ ^3-712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-713]]

謂於淨色變化加行功用， ^3-713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-714]]

與不淨色變化相違故。 ^3-714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-715]]

云何無邊虛空處解脫？ ^3-715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-716]]

謂於隨順解脫無邊虛空處住具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-717]]

如無邊虛空處解脫，無邊識處、 ^3-717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-718]]

無所有處、 ^3-718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-719]]

非想非非想處解脫亦爾。 ^3-719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-720]]

乃至為解脫、寂靜解脫無滯礙障。 ^3-720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-721]]

如是四種， ^3-721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-722]]

若聖弟子所得，能順無漏、 ^3-722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-723]]

是清淨性， ^3-723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-724]]

方名解脫， ^3-724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-725]]

解脫愛味故。 ^3-725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-726]]

寂靜解脫者， ^3-726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-727]]

謂超色無色，於中清淨，名無滯礙。 ^3-727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-728]]

味著無色是此障。 ^3-728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-729]]

云何想受滅解脫？ ^3-729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-730]]

謂依止非想非非想處解脫， ^3-730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-731]]

超過諸餘寂靜解脫， ^3-731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-732]]

住於似真解脫具足住中心心法滅， ^3-732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-733]]

為解脫想受滅障。 ^3-733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-737]]

此顯想受滅解脫，以非想非非想處為所依， ^3-737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-738]]

無境界、行相、助伴，心心法無故， ^3-738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-739]]

以心心法滅為自體。 ^3-739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-740]]

又此解脫似真解脫， ^3-740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-741]]

圓滿為性， ^3-741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-742]]

以聖弟子由出世間道已得轉依，諸心心法暫不現起，於此位中極寂靜故、 ^3-742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-743]]

染污意不現行故。 ^3-743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-744]]

此八解脫亦名聖住，諸聖所住故。 ^3-744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-745]]

然諸聖者多依二住， ^3-745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-746]]

謂第三第八，以最勝故。 ^3-746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-747]]

是故經中於此二解脫有身作證具足住言，非餘。 ^3-747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-748]]

由此二種， 如其次第， 有色無色解脫障斷無餘故、證得圓滿轉依故， ^3-748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-749]]

說名最勝。 ^3-749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-750]]

前四勝處由二解脫所建立， ^3-750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-751]]

後四勝處由一解脫所建立， ^3-751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-753]]

從彼所流故。 ^3-753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-754]]

所以者何？ ^3-754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-755]]

謂內有色想觀外色少，若好若惡 ^3-755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-756]]

若劣若勝，於彼諸色勝知勝見 ^3-756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-757]]

得如實想，是初勝處。 ^3-757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-758]]

內有色想觀外色多，若好若惡， ^3-758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-759]]

廣說乃至得如實想， ^3-759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-760]]

是第二勝處。 ^3-760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-761]]

此二勝處從有色觀諸色解脫所出。 ^3-761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-762]]

內無色想 ^3-762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-763]]

觀外色少，廣說乃至得如實想， ^3-763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-764]]

是第三勝處。 ^3-764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-765]]

內無色想 ^3-765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-766]]

觀外色多，廣說乃至得如實想， ^3-766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-767]]

是第四勝處。 ^3-767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-768]]

此二勝處從內無色想觀外諸色解脫所出。 ^3-768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-769]]

是故前四勝處，由二解脫所建立。 ^3-769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-770]]

內無色想觀外諸色，若青青顯青現青光， ^3-770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-771]]

猶如烏莫迦花、 ^3-771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-772]]

或如婆羅痆斯深染青衣， ^3-772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-773]]

若青青顯青現青光。 ^3-773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-774]]

如是內無色想觀外諸色，若青乃至青光亦爾。 ^3-774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-775]]

於彼諸色勝知勝見得如實想， ^3-775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-776]]

是第五勝處。 ^3-776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-777]]

內無色想觀外諸色，若黃乃至黃光， ^3-777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-778]]

猶如羯尼迦花、 ^3-778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-779]]

或如婆羅痆斯深染黃衣， ^3-779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-780]]

若黃廣說乃至得如實想， ^3-780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-781]]

是第六勝處。 ^3-781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-782]]

內無色想觀外諸色，若赤乃至赤光， ^3-782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-783]]

猶如般豆時縛迦花、或如婆羅痆斯深染赤衣， ^3-783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-784]]

若赤廣說乃至得如實想， ^3-784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-785]]

是第七勝處。 ^3-785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-786]]

內無色想觀外諸色，若白白顯白現白光， ^3-786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-787]]

猶如烏沙斯星色、或如婆羅痆斯極鮮白衣， ^3-787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-788]]

若白白顯白現白光。 ^3-788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-789]]

如是內無色想觀外諸色，若白白顯白現白光亦爾。 於彼諸色勝知勝見得如實想， ^3-789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-791]]

是第八勝處。 ^3-791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-792]]

如是四勝處，從淨解脫身作證具足住所出。 ^3-792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-793]]

此中解脫是意解所緣， ^3-793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-794]]

勝處是勝伏所緣， ^3-794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-795]]

少多等境隨意自在，或令隱沒故、 ^3-795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-797]]

或隨欲轉故。 ^3-797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-799]]

少色者，有情數色，其量小故。 ^3-799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-800]]

多色者，非有情數色， ^3-800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-801]]

舍林地山等，其量大故。 ^3-801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-803]]

好色惡色者，淨不淨顯色所攝。 ^3-803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-804]]

劣色勝色者，若人若天隨其次第， ^3-804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-805]]

於彼諸色勝者自在轉故。 ^3-805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-806]]

知者，由奢摩他道故。 ^3-806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-807]]

見者，由毘鉢舍那道故。 ^3-807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-808]]

得如實想者，謂於已勝未勝中 ^3-808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-809]]

得無增上慢想故。 ^3-809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-810]]

若青者，是總句。 ^3-810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-811]]

青顯者，是俱生青。 ^3-811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-812]]

青現者，是和合青。 ^3-812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-813]]

青光者，謂彼二所放鮮淨光青。 ^3-813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-814]]

如青，黃赤白廣說亦爾。 ^3-814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-815]]

於一處說二譬喻者，為顯俱生、和合二顯色故。 ^3-815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-816]]

謂若青者，總舉花衣二青。 ^3-816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-817]]

青顯者，依花青說， ^3-817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-818]]

以俱生故。 ^3-818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-819]]

青現者，依衣青說， ^3-819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-820]]

以和合方成故。 ^3-820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-821]]

青光者， ^3-821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-822]]

依二種說，由彼二種俱有鮮淨光故。 ^3-822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-823]]

如是二譬喻中若青青顯等，總句釋句如相應知。 ^3-823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-824]]

如青，黃等亦爾， ^3-824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-825]]

餘如解脫中說。 ^3-825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-826]]

何等為餘？ ^3-826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-827]]

謂內有色想觀外色等。 ^3-827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-828]]

如有色觀諸色等，隨相應釋。 ^3-828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-829]]

已說勝處勝所緣境界。 ^3-829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-830]]

遍處者，謂於遍滿住具足中，若定若慧， ^3-830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-831]]

及彼相應心心法，是名遍處。 ^3-831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-832]]

遍滿者，其量廣大周普無邊。 ^3-832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-833]]

此復十種， ^3-833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-834]]

謂地、水、火、風、 ^3-834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-835]]

青、黃、赤、白、 ^3-835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-836]]

無邊空處、無邊識處， ^3-836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-837]]

皆悉遍滿。 ^3-837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-838]]

問：何故於遍處建立地等？ ^3-838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-839]]

答：由此遍處觀所依能依色皆遍滿故。 ^3-839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-840]]

若於此中不建立地等， ^3-840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-841]]

遍處者即離所依大種，亦不能觀青等所造色為遍滿相。 ^3-841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-842]]

是故為觀所依能依皆悉遍滿， ^3-842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-843]]

建立地等。 ^3-843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-844]]

餘隨所應，如解脫中說， ^3-844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-845]]

謂無邊空處等。 ^3-845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-847]]

當知此中依解脫故造修， ^3-847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-848]]

由勝處故起方便， ^3-848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-849]]

由遍處故成滿。 ^3-849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-850]]

若於彼得成滿，即於解脫究竟。 ^3-850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-851]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十三大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十四安慧菩薩糅釋上集論大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中得品第三之二 ^3-851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-852]]

復次無諍者，謂依止靜慮， ^3-852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-853]]

於防護他所應起煩惱住具足中，若定若慧， ^3-853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-854]]

及彼相應諸心心法。 ^3-854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-855]]

所以者何？ ^3-855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-856]]

住無諍者 ^3-856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-857]]

若欲往詣一切有情所應見處， ^3-857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-858]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-860]]

願智者， ^3-861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-862]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-880]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-881]]

名法無礙解。 ^3-881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-882]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-883]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-885]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-886]]

訓詞無礙解者， ^3-886

謂於諸方言音及訓釋諸法言詞無礙具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-888]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-889]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-891]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-892]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-914]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-921]]

四智清淨。 ^3-951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-952]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-960]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-961]]

變化智具足者， ^3-961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-970]]

一處非處智力、 ^3-975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-976]]

二自業智力、 ^3-976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-977]]

三靜慮解脫三摩地三摩鉢底智力、 ^3-977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-978]]

四根上下智力、 ^3-978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-979]]

五種種勝解智力、 ^3-979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-980]]

六種種界智力、 ^3-980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-981]]

七遍趣行智力、 ^3-981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-982]]

八宿住隨念智力、 ^3-982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-983]]

九死生智力、 ^3-983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-984]]

十漏盡智力。 ^3-984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-985]]

處非處智力者， ^3-985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-986]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-987]]

於一切種處非處智具足中，若定若慧，及彼相應諸心心法。 ^3-987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-988]]

一切種處非處智具足者， ^3-988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-989]]

謂於一切種因非因智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-990]]

自業智力者， ^3-990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-991]]

謂於一切種自業智具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-991

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-992]]

以於一切種自業智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-993]]

如是餘力，隨其所應當正建立。 ^3-993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-994]]

云何隨其所應？ ^3-994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-995]]

靜慮解脫三摩地三摩鉢底智力者， ^3-995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-996]]

謂於一切種靜慮、解脫、等持、等至智具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-997]]

由於一切種靜慮解脫等持等至智無著無礙現行中，所有三摩地等為體故。 ^3-997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-998]]

如是根上下智力者， ^3-998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-999]]

謂於一切種根上下智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1000]]

種種勝解智力者， ^3-1000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1001]]

謂於一切種差別勝解智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1002]]

種種界智力者， ^3-1002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1003]]

謂於一切種差別界智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1004]]

遍趣行智力者， ^3-1004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1005]]

謂於一切種遍趣行智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1006]]

宿住隨念智力者， ^3-1006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1007]]

謂於一切種宿住隨念智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1008]]

死生智力者， ^3-1008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1009]]

謂於一切種死生智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1010]]

漏盡智力者， ^3-1010

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1011]]

謂於一切種漏盡智無著無礙現行中所有三摩地等。 ^3-1011

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1012]]

無畏者，謂四無畏： ^3-1012

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1013]]

一正等覺無畏、 ^3-1013

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1014]]

二漏盡無畏、 ^3-1014

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1015]]

三障法無畏、 ^3-1015

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1016]]

四出苦道無畏。 ^3-1016

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1017]]

正等覺無畏者， ^3-1017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1018]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-1018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1019]]

由自利門， ^3-1019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1020]]

於一切種所知境界正等覺自稱德號建立具足中，若定若慧， ^3-1020

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1021]]

及彼相應諸心心法。 ^3-1021

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1022]]

如經言：我是正等覺者， ^3-1022

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1023]]

設有世間沙門婆羅門若天魔梵，依法立難或令憶念，言於是法非正等覺。 我於是事正見無緣。 ^3-1023

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1024]]

以於此事正見無由故，得安隱住無怖無畏， ^3-1024

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1025]]

自稱我處大仙尊位， ^3-1025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1026]]

於大眾中正師子吼轉大梵輪， ^3-1026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1027]]

一切世間沙門婆羅門若天魔梵所不能轉。 ^3-1027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1028]]

漏盡無畏者， ^3-1028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1029]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-1029

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1030]]

由自利門， ^3-1030

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1031]]

於一切種漏盡自稱德號建立具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-1031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1032]]

如經言：我諸漏永盡，如是等，廣說如前。 ^3-1032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1033]]

障法無畏者， ^3-1033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1034]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-1034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1035]]

由利他門， ^3-1035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1036]]

於一切種說障礙法自稱德號建立具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-1036

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1037]]

如經言：又我為諸弟子說障礙法，染必為障，乃至廣說。 ^3-1037

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1038]]

出苦道無畏者， ^3-1038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1039]]

謂依止靜慮， ^3-1039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1040]]

由利他門， ^3-1040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1041]]

於一切種說出離道法自稱德號建立具足中，若定若慧，餘如前說。 ^3-1041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1042]]

如經言：又我為諸弟子說出離道，諸聖修習決定出離決定通達。 ^3-1042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1043]]

設有世間沙門婆羅門若天魔梵，依法立難或令憶念，言修此道非正出離，不正盡苦及證苦邊。 我於是事正見無緣，乃至廣說。 ^3-1043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1044]]

如是四無畏，略說有二，謂自利、利他。 ^3-1044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1045]]

前二是自利， ^3-1045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1046]]

由智斷差別故； ^3-1046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1047]]

後二是利他， ^3-1047

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1048]]

由遠離所治法修習能治法故。 ^3-1048

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1049]]

以正等覺無畏， ^3-1049

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1050]]

由內智自利門言。 ^3-1050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1051]]

我於一切種所知境界差別邊際皆正等覺， ^3-1051

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1052]]

於一切世間前自稱德號立正無難具足中所有定慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1052

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1053]]

當知餘無畏如應亦爾。 ^3-1053

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1054]]

一切種漏盡者，謂諸煩惱并習氣永盡。 ^3-1054

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1055]]

一切種障礙法者，謂一切雜染所對治法。 ^3-1055

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1056]]

一切種出離道者，謂從方便道乃至究竟道。 ^3-1056

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1057]]

念住者， ^3-1057

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1058]]

即諸如來三不共念住， ^3-1058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1059]]

謂御大眾時於一切種煩惱不現行具足中，若定若慧，廣說如前。 ^3-1059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1060]]

何等為三念住？ ^3-1060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1061]]

所謂大師哀愍一切欲求義利， ^3-1061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1062]]

起大悲心，為諸弟子宣說法要，告諸比丘：汝等當知，此能利益、 ^3-1062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1063]]

此能安樂、 ^3-1063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1064]]

此能利益安樂。 ^3-1064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1065]]

爾時若有諸弟子眾恭敬聽聞， ^3-1065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1066]]

聞已諦受住奉教心，精進修行法隨法行。 ^3-1066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1067]]

如來於彼不生歡喜心不踊躍， ^3-1067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1068]]

但起大捨住念正知， ^3-1068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1069]]

隨諸聖眾所應修習教誡教授。 ^3-1069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1070]]

是名初不共念住。 ^3-1070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1072]]

又復大師哀愍一切欲求義利， ^3-1072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1073]]

起大悲心，為諸弟子宣說法要，乃至此能利益安樂。 ^3-1073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1074]]

爾時若有諸弟子眾不恭敬聽聞，乃至不精進修行法隨法行。 ^3-1074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1075]]

如來於彼不生恚恨， ^3-1075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1076]]

不捨保任 ^3-1076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1077]]

心無悵恨， ^3-1077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1078]]

但起大捨，乃至廣說。 ^3-1078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1079]]

是名第二不共念住。 ^3-1079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1081]]

又復大師哀愍一切欲求義利，起大悲心，為諸弟子宣說法要，乃至此能利益安樂。 ^3-1081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1082]]

爾時一分弟子恭敬聽聞，乃至精進修行法隨法行；一分弟子不恭敬聽聞，乃至不精進修行法隨法行。 ^3-1082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1083]]

如來於彼不生歡喜， ^3-1083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1084]]

乃至心不悵恨。 ^3-1084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1085]]

如是三念住， ^3-1085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1086]]

顯大師御眾時隨其次第，於一切種愛恚俱煩惱并習氣不現行具足中，所有定慧等為體。 ^3-1086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1087]]

不護者，即三不護。 ^3-1087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1088]]

謂大師御眾時， ^3-1088

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1089]]

於隨所欲教授教誡方便具足中，若定若慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1089

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1090]]

何等為三？ ^3-1090

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1091]]

如經言：如來身業清淨現行， ^3-1091

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1093]]

無不清淨現行身業可須覆藏， ^3-1093

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1094]]

謂勿他知我之所有語業意業，現行亦爾。 ^3-1094

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1095]]

由彼大師心無懼慮， ^3-1095

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1096]]

善御所化一切大眾 ^3-1096

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1097]]

隨其所欲，自然強力折伏攝受教誡教授方便具足。 ^3-1097

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1098]]

無忘失法者， ^3-1098

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1099]]

謂於一切種隨其所作所說明記具足中，若定若慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1099

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1100]]

此中顯示依化事門，於隨所作等念具足中所有三摩地等，是無忘失法。 ^3-1100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1101]]

永斷習氣者， ^3-1101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1102]]

謂一切智者於非一切智所作不現行具足中，若定若慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1103]]

此中顯示一切智者於所有能表有餘煩惱所知障身語所作不現行具足中所有三摩地等， ^3-1103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1104]]

是永斷習氣。 ^3-1104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1105]]

大悲者， ^3-1105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1106]]

謂於緣無間苦境大悲住具足中，若定若慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1107]]

此中顯示於緣一切三界有情無間一切種苦境 ^3-1107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1108]]

大悲住具足中所有三摩地等，是名大悲。 ^3-1108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1109]]

不共佛法者， ^3-1109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1110]]

即十八不共佛法。 ^3-1110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1111]]

彼復云何？ ^3-1111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1112]]

謂於不共身語意業清淨具足中， ^3-1112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1113]]

於所依及果根未得不退具足中， ^3-1113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1114]]

於不共業現行具足中， ^3-1114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1115]]

於不共智住具足中， 若定若慧，乃至廣說。 ^3-1115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1116]]

何等十八？ ^3-1116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1117]]

如經言： ^3-1117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1118]]

如來無有誤失、 ^3-1118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1119]]

無卒暴音、 ^3-1119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1120]]

無忘失念、 ^3-1120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1121]]

無不定心、 ^3-1121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1122]]

無種種想、 ^3-1122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1123]]

無不擇捨、 ^3-1123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1124]]

志欲無退、 ^3-1124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1125]]

精進無退、 ^3-1125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1126]]

念無退、 ^3-1126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1127]]

定無退、 ^3-1127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1128]]

慧無退、 ^3-1128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1129]]

解脫無退、 ^3-1129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1130]]

一切身業智為前導隨智而轉、 ^3-1130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1131]]

一切語業智為前導隨智而轉、 ^3-1131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1132]]

一切意業智為前導隨智而轉、 ^3-1132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1133]]

知過去世無著無礙、 ^3-1133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1134]]

知未來世無著無礙、 ^3-1134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1135]]

知現在世無著無礙。 ^3-1135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1136]]

建立彼相， ^3-1136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1137]]

如經廣說。 ^3-1137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1138]]

如來無有誤失者， ^3-1138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1139]]

謂阿羅漢比丘雖漏已盡， ^3-1139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1140]]

為乞食故出遊城邑， ^3-1140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1141]]

或於一時與惡象惡馬惡牛惡狗等共為遊止， ^3-1141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1142]]

或於一時踐躡叢刺齊足越坑， ^3-1142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1143]]

或於一時入女人家不依正理而作語言， ^3-1143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1144]]

或於林野捨棄正道而行邪徑， ^3-1144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1145]]

或與盜賊猛惡獸等共為遊止。 ^3-1145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1146]]

如是等誤失事阿羅漢猶有， ^3-1146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1147]]

如來永無。 ^3-1147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1148]]

無卒暴音者， ^3-1148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1149]]

謂阿羅漢或於一時遊行林野迷失道路， ^3-1149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1150]]

或入空宅揚聲叫喚發大暴音， ^3-1150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1151]]

因不染污習氣過失聚露脣齒而現大笑。 ^3-1151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1152]]

如是等卒暴音，阿羅漢猶有，如來永無。 ^3-1152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1153]]

無忘失念者，謂阿羅漢猶有不染污，久遠所作久遠所說忘失憶念，如來永無。 ^3-1153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1154]]

無不定心者， ^3-1154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1155]]

謂阿羅漢斂心方定， ^3-1155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1156]]

出即不定， ^3-1156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1157]]

如來於一切位無不定。 ^3-1157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1158]]

心無種種想者， ^3-1158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1159]]

謂阿羅漢於有餘生死起違逆想， ^3-1159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1160]]

於無餘涅槃起寂靜想， ^3-1160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1161]]

如來於生死涅槃無差別想， ^3-1161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1162]]

由住第一大捨故。 ^3-1162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1163]]

無不擇捨者， ^3-1163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1164]]

謂阿羅漢不以智慧簡擇，棄捨有情利益事， ^3-1164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1165]]

如來無此等事故，無不擇捨。 ^3-1165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1166]]

又阿羅漢於所知障淨有未得退， ^3-1166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1167]]

謂志欲退、 ^3-1167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1168]]

精進退、念退、定退、慧退、解脫退， ^3-1168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1169]]

如是六退如來永無。 ^3-1169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1170]]

又阿羅漢或於一時善身業轉， ^3-1170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1171]]

或於一時無記身業轉， ^3-1171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1172]]

語業意業亦爾； ^3-1172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1173]]

如來三業智為前導故、 ^3-1173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1174]]

隨智而轉故， ^3-1174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1175]]

無有無記。 ^3-1175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1176]]

智為前導者，智所等起故。 ^3-1176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1177]]

隨智而轉者，與智俱行故。 ^3-1177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1178]]

又阿羅漢比丘於三世所知事，不能起心即解故， ^3-1178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1179]]

智見有著， ^3-1179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1180]]

不能一切悉解故， ^3-1180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1181]]

智見有礙； ^3-1181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1182]]

如來於三世境暫起心時即遍知一切， ^3-1182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1183]]

是故智見無著無礙。 ^3-1183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1185]]

十八中前六 ^3-1185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1186]]

於不共身語意業清淨具足中所有三摩地等為體。 ^3-1186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1187]]

無有誤失， ^3-1187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1188]]

依身清淨說。 ^3-1188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1189]]

無卒暴音， ^3-1189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1190]]

依語業清淨說。 ^3-1190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1191]]

無忘失念、 ^3-1191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1193]]

無不定心、 ^3-1193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1194]]

無種種想、 ^3-1194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1195]]

無不擇捨， ^3-1195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1196]]

此四依意業清淨說。 ^3-1196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1197]]

志欲無退乃至解脫無退，此六於所依及果根未得不退具足中所有三摩地等為體， ^3-1197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1198]]

所依謂志欲， ^3-1198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1199]]

果謂解脫， ^3-1199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1200]]

根謂精進等。 ^3-1200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1201]]

一切身語意業智為前導 ^3-1201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1202]]

隨智而轉， ^3-1202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1203]]

此三於不共業現行具足中所有三摩地等為體。 ^3-1203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1204]]

知去來今無著無礙，此三於不共智住具足中所有三摩地等為體。 ^3-1204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1205]]

一切種妙智者， ^3-1205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1206]]

謂於蘊界處 ^3-1206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1207]]

一切種妙智性具足中，若定若慧， ^3-1207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1208]]

及彼相應諸心心法。 ^3-1208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1209]]

云何於蘊界處一切種妙智性具足？ ^3-1209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1210]]

謂於蘊等自性差別相通達一切差別， ^3-1210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1211]]

邊際智成滿故。 ^3-1211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1212]]

云何引發如是等功德？ ^3-1212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1213]]

謂依止清淨四靜慮。 若外道若聲聞若菩薩等，引發四無量、五神通， ^3-1213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1214]]

多分依止邊際第四靜慮，若聲聞若菩薩若如來等引發所餘功德。 ^3-1214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1215]]

何因引發如是功德？ ^3-1215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1216]]

謂依止靜慮數數思惟，隨所建立法故。 ^3-1216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1217]]

此中顯示如是等功德引發所依止， ^3-1217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1218]]

能引發補特伽羅 ^3-1218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1219]]

能引發方便。 ^3-1219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1220]]

云何能引發方便？ ^3-1220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1221]]

謂於隨所建立教法，以眾多作意定心起數數思惟行相。 ^3-1221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1222]]

如欲引發無量時，依止靜慮，於慈俱心無恨無怨等教法，以修慧相應作意數數思惟。 ^3-1222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1223]]

欲引發神通等時，依止靜慮，於變一為多等教法，以修慧相應作意數數思惟。 ^3-1223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1224]]

如是於一切處數數思惟，如所建立隨相應知。 ^3-1224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1225]]

又如是等功德，略有二種： ^3-1225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1226]]

一現前發起自所作用、 ^3-1226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1227]]

二安住自性。 ^3-1227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1228]]

若現前發起自所作用， ^3-1228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1229]]

以出世間後所得世俗智為體。 ^3-1229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1230]]

若安住自性， ^3-1230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1231]]

用出世間智為體。 ^3-1231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1232]]

又現前發起自所作用者， ^3-1232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1233]]

謂諸聖者隨其所應發起斷所治障等種種作業。 ^3-1233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1234]]

安住自性者， ^3-1234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1235]]

謂最勝寂靜無分別智所攝無緣無量等現法樂住。 ^3-1235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1236]]

復次無量作何業？ ^3-1236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1237]]

謂捨所治障 ^3-1237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1238]]

哀愍住故，能速圓滿福德資糧， ^3-1238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1239]]

成熟有情心無懈倦。 ^3-1239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1240]]

捨所治障者， ^3-1240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1241]]

謂如其次第，四無量能捨瞋、害、不樂、愛恚故。 ^3-1241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1242]]

哀愍住者，謂四無量 ^3-1242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1243]]

於利益有情事隨順轉住。 ^3-1243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1244]]

由於一切有情哀愍住故，能速圓滿福德資糧。 ^3-1244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1245]]

成熟有情心無懈倦者， ^3-1245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1246]]

由愍諸有情， ^3-1246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1247]]

不顧自身故。 ^3-1247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1248]]

解脫作何業？ ^3-1248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1249]]

謂引發變化事， ^3-1249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1250]]

於淨不淨變化無有艱難。 ^3-1250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1251]]

於寂靜解脫無有滯礙， ^3-1251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1252]]

能住第一寂靜聖住，由勝解思惟故。 ^3-1252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1253]]

此中顯初二解脫能引發變化事。 ^3-1253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1254]]

由第三解脫，於淨不淨變化無有艱難。 ^3-1254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1255]]

由四無色解脫，於寂靜解脫無有滯礙。 ^3-1255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1256]]

由最後解脫， ^3-1256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1257]]

能住第一寂靜聖住。 ^3-1257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1258]]

由勝解思惟故者， ^3-1258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1259]]

顯如是如是勝解義是解脫義。 ^3-1259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1260]]

勝處作何業？ ^3-1260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1261]]

謂能令前三解脫所緣境界自在而轉，由勝伏所緣故。 ^3-1261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1263]]

遍處作何業？ ^3-1263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1264]]

謂善能成辦解脫所緣，遍滿流布故。 ^3-1264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1265]]

無諍作何業？ ^3-1265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1266]]

謂所發語言聞皆信伏， ^3-1266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1267]]

愛護他心最為勝故，如其所應發語言故。 ^3-1267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1268]]

願智作何業？ ^3-1268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1269]]

謂能善記別三世等事， ^3-1269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1270]]

一切世間咸所恭敬， ^3-1270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1271]]

由達一切眾所歸仰故。 ^3-1271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1273]]

無礙解作何業？ ^3-1273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1274]]

謂善說法要悅眾生心， ^3-1274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1275]]

能絕一切諸疑網故。 ^3-1275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1276]]

神通作何業？ ^3-1276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1277]]

謂以身業語業記心，化導有情令入聖教。 ^3-1277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1278]]

善知有情一切心行及過未已，如應教授令永出離。 ^3-1278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1279]]

此中顯示神境、天耳乃至漏盡通， ^3-1279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1280]]

如其次第能起身業化導等用， ^3-1280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1281]]

由天耳通解了一切言音差別能引語業故。 ^3-1281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1282]]

相及隨好作何業？ ^3-1282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1283]]

謂能令暫見謂大丈夫，心生淨信。 ^3-1283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1285]]

清淨作何業？ ^3-1285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1286]]

謂由此勢力故取生有， ^3-1286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1287]]

隨其樂欲，或住一劫、或復劫餘、 ^3-1287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1288]]

或捨壽行、 ^3-1288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1289]]

或於諸法自在而轉、 ^3-1289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1290]]

或於諸定自在而轉、 ^3-1290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1291]]

或復任持諸佛正法。 ^3-1291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1292]]

此中顯示由所依清淨， ^3-1292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1293]]

隨其所樂於所依身取住捨自在。 ^3-1293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1294]]

即攝三句，謂故取生有等， ^3-1294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1295]]

由境界清淨於諸法中得自在轉， ^3-1295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1296]]

由心清淨於三摩地得自在轉， ^3-1296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1297]]

由智清淨任持如來無上正法。 ^3-1297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1298]]

力作何業？ ^3-1298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1299]]

謂為除捨無因惡因論、 ^3-1299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1300]]

不作而得論， ^3-1300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1301]]

無倒宣說增上生道， ^3-1301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1302]]

悟入一切有情心行， ^3-1302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1303]]

正說法器、 ^3-1303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1304]]

意樂、 隨眠、 境界、 資糧， ^3-1304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1306]]

當能出離。 ^3-1306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1307]]

隨其所應， ^3-1307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1308]]

宣說決定勝道， ^3-1308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1309]]

降伏諸魔。 ^3-1309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1310]]

善能記別一切問論。 ^3-1310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1311]]

此中顯初二力能說增上生道， ^3-1311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1312]]

餘八力能說決定勝道。 ^3-1312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1313]]

如是二種具足， ^3-1313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1314]]

顯示諸佛所作。 ^3-1314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1315]]

所以者何？ ^3-1315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1316]]

世尊由處非處智力 ^3-1316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1317]]

折伏一切世間無因論者惡因論者，宣說無倒增上生道。 ^3-1317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1318]]

諸外道等於增上生，或謂無因，或謂自性、自在等為因故，名無因惡因論。 ^3-1318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1319]]

由自業智力折伏一切世間不作而得論者， ^3-1319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1320]]

無倒宣說善趣正道。 ^3-1320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1321]]

諸外道等謂不作業自然得報，故名不作而得論。 ^3-1321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1322]]

由靜慮解脫等持等至智力，悟入一切有情心行。 ^3-1322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1323]]

心所修行，故名心行。 ^3-1323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1324]]

由根上下智力， ^3-1324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1325]]

悟入一切正說法器，以信等根若善成熟能為法器故。 ^3-1325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1326]]

由種種勝解智力， ^3-1326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1327]]

悟入一切勝劣意樂。 ^3-1327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1328]]

由種種界智力， ^3-1328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1329]]

悟入一切可破隨眠諸煩惱性。 ^3-1329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1330]]

由遍趣行智力， ^3-1330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1331]]

悟入一切大小乘教法所攝境界。 ^3-1331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1332]]

由宿住隨念智力， ^3-1332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1333]]

悟入一切資糧。 前生所集聖道因緣，是名資糧。 ^3-1333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1334]]

由死生智力， ^3-1334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1335]]

悟入一切當來功能性。 ^3-1335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1336]]

由漏盡智力， ^3-1336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1337]]

悟入一切三界出離。 ^3-1337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1338]]

由如是悟入已，隨其所應宣說解脫出世聖道。 ^3-1338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1339]]

此十名力者， ^3-1339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1340]]

善能降伏諸魔故、善能記別一切問論故。 ^3-1340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1341]]

降伏諸魔者，由此十力 ^3-1341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1342]]

能降伏蘊魔、煩惱魔、天魔、死魔，為最勝故。 ^3-1342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1343]]

雖斷所知障亦不能為礙， ^3-1343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1344]]

故名最勝。 ^3-1344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1345]]

記別一切問論者， ^3-1345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1346]]

謂於處非處乃至漏盡一切處，所有問論記別無滯故。 ^3-1346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1347]]

無畏作何業？ ^3-1347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1348]]

謂處大眾中自正建立我為大師， ^3-1348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1349]]

摧伏一切邪難外道。 ^3-1349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1350]]

大師者， ^3-1350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1351]]

自利利他眾德圓滿故。 ^3-1351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1352]]

摧伏邪難外道者， ^3-1352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1353]]

謂能摧伏於如來所說成等正覺永斷諸漏障道法中邪難外道故。 ^3-1353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1354]]

念住作何業？ ^3-1354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1355]]

謂能不染污攝御大眾， ^3-1355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1356]]

由於恭敬聽聞等， ^3-1356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1357]]

無愛恚等諸煩惱故。 ^3-1357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1358]]

不護作何業？ ^3-1358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1359]]

謂能無間斷教授教誡所化徒眾， ^3-1359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1360]]

由無藏護自過慮顯彰故。 ^3-1360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1361]]

無忘失法作何業？ ^3-1361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1362]]

謂能不捨離一切佛事。 ^3-1362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1363]]

所以者何？ ^3-1363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1364]]

由此於諸有情現前應利益事，能無放逸不越一剎那故。 ^3-1364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1365]]

永斷習氣作何業？ ^3-1365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1366]]

謂離諸煩惱亦不顯現似諸煩惱所作事業， ^3-1366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1367]]

非如阿羅漢比丘猶現誤失等事。 ^3-1367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1368]]

大悲作何業？ ^3-1368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1369]]

謂日夜六時遍觀世間， ^3-1369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1370]]

誰減誰退誰增誰進，如是等種種觀察。 ^3-1370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1371]]

不共佛法作何業？ ^3-1371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1372]]

謂由身語意業清淨， ^3-1372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1373]]

以得不退，若行若住映蔽一切聲聞獨覺。 ^3-1373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1374]]

如此諸句，依前所說， ^3-1374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1375]]

於不共三業清淨具足等相，如應配釋。 ^3-1375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1376]]

一切種妙智作何業？ ^3-1376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1377]]

謂能絕一切有情一切疑網， ^3-1377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1378]]

令正法眼長時得住。 ^3-1378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1379]]

由此有情未成熟者令成熟， ^3-1379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1380]]

已成熟者令解脫。 ^3-1380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1381]]

絕疑網者，於一切處智無礙故。 ^3-1381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1382]]

令正法眼得久住者，於彼彼時方，為斷所化有情疑惑，宣說種種法門差別。 ^3-1382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1383]]

諸結集者，次第結集，令不滅故。 ^3-1383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1384]]

依此法眼，未成熟有情令速成熟， ^3-1384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1385]]

已成熟者速令解脫。 ^3-1385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1386]]

復次於諸現觀位證得後後勝品道時， ^3-1386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1387]]

捨前所得下劣品道。 ^3-1387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1391]]

如證得此果所攝道時， 即捨此向所攝道， ^3-1391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1392]]

以不復現前故。 ^3-1392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1393]]

又即此時集斷作證， ^3-1393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1394]]

由得果時 ^3-1394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1395]]

永斷此所治種類煩惱品麁重令無餘故、 ^3-1395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1396]]

證得勝品轉依故。 ^3-1396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1397]]

復次於無餘涅槃界， ^3-1397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1398]]

聲聞獨覺一切聖道由頓捨所捨， ^3-1398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1399]]

非諸菩薩， ^3-1399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1400]]

是故唯說諸菩薩為無盡善根者、 ^3-1400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1401]]

無盡功德者。 ^3-1401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1402]]

頓捨所捨者， ^3-1402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1403]]

是究竟不現行捨義。 ^3-1403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1404]]

非諸菩薩所得聖道有如是捨， ^3-1404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1405]]

為欲利益一切有情皆得涅槃故。 ^3-1405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1406]]

由此因緣，《無盡慧經》等說諸菩薩為無盡善根者、無盡功德者。 ^3-1406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1407]]

復次何故建立諸無記事？ ^3-1407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1408]]

由彼所問不如理故。 ^3-1408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1409]]

何故所問不如理耶？ ^3-1409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1410]]

遠離因果染淨所應思處故。 ^3-1410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1411]]

此中顯示如來於諸外道所問世間常無常等事中建立十四不可記事，由彼所問不如正理，能引無義利故。 ^3-1411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1412]]

何等問論能引義利？ ^3-1412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1413]]

謂依四聖諦所有問論， ^3-1413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1414]]

由此問論因果染淨應思處所攝故。 ^3-1414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1415]]

復次何緣菩薩已入菩薩超昇離生位，而非預流耶？ ^3-1415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1417]]

由得不住道，一向預流行不成就故。 ^3-1417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1418]]

何緣亦非一來耶？ ^3-1418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1419]]

故受諸有，無量生故。 ^3-1419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1420]]

何緣亦非不還耶？ ^3-1420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1421]]

安住靜慮，還生欲界故。 ^3-1421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1422]]

又諸菩薩已得諦現觀， ^3-1422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1423]]

於十地修道位唯修所知障對治道，非煩惱障對治道。 ^3-1423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1424]]

若得菩提時頓斷煩惱障及所知障， ^3-1424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1425]]

頓成阿羅漢及如來。 ^3-1425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1426]]

此諸菩薩雖未永斷煩惱，然此煩惱猶如呪藥所伏諸毒， ^3-1426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1427]]

不起一切煩惱過失，一切地中如阿羅漢已斷煩惱。 ^3-1427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1428]]

復次諸菩薩於所知境應修善巧， ^3-1428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1429]]

於諸方便應修善巧， ^3-1429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1430]]

於虛妄分別應修善巧， ^3-1430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1431]]

於無分別應修善巧， ^3-1431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1432]]

於時時中應修練根。 ^3-1432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1433]]

所知境者，略有六種： ^3-1433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1434]]

一迷亂、 ^3-1434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1435]]

二迷亂所依、 ^3-1435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1436]]

三不迷亂所依、 ^3-1436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1437]]

四迷亂不迷亂、 ^3-1437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1438]]

五不迷亂、 ^3-1438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1439]]

六不迷亂等流。 ^3-1439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1440]]

迷亂者，謂能取所取執。 ^3-1440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1441]]

迷亂所依者，謂聖智所行， ^3-1441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1442]]

唯有行相虛妄分別為體，由有此故一切愚夫迷亂執轉。 ^3-1442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1443]]

不迷亂所依者， ^3-1443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1444]]

謂真如是無分別智所依處故。 ^3-1444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1445]]

迷亂不迷亂者， ^3-1445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1446]]

謂隨順出世智所有聞慧等諸善法， ^3-1446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1447]]

分別所知境故、隨順無分別智故。 ^3-1447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1448]]

不迷亂者，謂無分別智。 ^3-1448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1449]]

不迷亂等流者，謂聖道後所得善法。 ^3-1449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1450]]

方便善巧者， ^3-1450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1451]]

略有四種： ^3-1451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1452]]

一成熟有情善巧、 ^3-1452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1453]]

二圓滿佛法善巧、 ^3-1453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1454]]

三速證神通善巧、 ^3-1454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1455]]

四道無斷善巧。 ^3-1455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1457]]

成熟有情善巧者，謂四攝事， ^3-1457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1458]]

由攝受彼令處善法故。 ^3-1458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1459]]

圓滿佛法善巧者，謂慧波羅蜜多。 ^3-1459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1460]]

如經言：若菩薩摩訶薩欲得圓滿施波羅蜜多乃至一切種妙智性，當學般若波羅蜜多故。 ^3-1460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1461]]

速證神通善巧者， ^3-1461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1462]]

謂日夜六時發露諸惡、 ^3-1462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1463]]

隨喜功德、 ^3-1463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1464]]

勸請諸佛、 ^3-1464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1465]]

迴向善根等， ^3-1465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1466]]

廣說如聖者彌勒所問經。 ^3-1466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1467]]

道無斷善巧者， ^3-1467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1468]]

謂無住處涅槃。 ^3-1468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1469]]

由此數數究竟無斷，周遍十方一切世界，隨所應化示現一切佛菩薩行。 ^3-1469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1470]]

虛妄分別者， ^3-1470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1471]]

略有十種， ^3-1471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1472]]

謂根本分別、 ^3-1472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1473]]

相分別、 ^3-1473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1474]]

相顯現分別、 ^3-1474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1475]]

相變異分別、 ^3-1475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1476]]

相顯現變異分別、 ^3-1476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1477]]

他引分別、 ^3-1477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1478]]

不如理分別、 ^3-1478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1479]]

如理分別、 ^3-1479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1480]]

執著分別、 ^3-1480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1481]]

散亂分別。 ^3-1481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1483]]

根本分別者， ^3-1483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1484]]

謂阿賴耶識， ^3-1484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1485]]

是一切分別種子故。 ^3-1485
