![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1486]]

相分別者， ^3-1486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1487]]

謂身所居處所受用識， ^3-1487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1488]]

是所取相故。 ^3-1488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1489]]

彼復如其次第，以諸色根器世界色等境界為相。 ^3-1489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1490]]

相顯現分別者， ^3-1490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1491]]

謂六識身及意， ^3-1491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1492]]

如前所說所取相而顯現故。 ^3-1492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1493]]

相變異分別者， ^3-1493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1494]]

謂如前所說身等相變異生起。 ^3-1494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1495]]

相顯現變異分別者， ^3-1495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1496]]

謂如前所說眼識等相顯現，於苦樂等位差別生起。 ^3-1496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1497]]

他引分別者， ^3-1497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1498]]

謂教法所攝名句文身相。 ^3-1498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1499]]

此復二種： ^3-1499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1500]]

一惡說法律為體、二善說法律為體。 ^3-1500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1501]]

由此增上力， ^3-1501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1502]]

如其次第引二作意所攝，謂不如理分別、如理分別。 ^3-1502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1503]]

執著分別者， ^3-1503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1504]]

謂不如理分別所起六十二見所攝所有分別。 ^3-1504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1505]]

散亂分別者， ^3-1505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1506]]

謂如理分別所起無性等執為相所有分別。 ^3-1506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1508]]

此復十種， ^3-1508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1509]]

謂無性分別、 ^3-1509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1510]]

有性分別、 ^3-1510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1511]]

增益分別、 ^3-1511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1512]]

損減分別、 ^3-1512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1513]]

一性分別、 ^3-1513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1514]]

異性分別、 ^3-1514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1515]]

自性分別、 ^3-1515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1516]]

差別分別、 ^3-1516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1517]]

隨名義分別、 ^3-1517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1518]]

隨義名分別。 ^3-1518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1519]]

如是十種分別， ^3-1519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1520]]

依般若波羅蜜多初分宣說。 ^3-1520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1521]]

如經言：舍利子！是菩薩實有菩薩， ^3-1521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1522]]

正不隨觀菩薩。 ^3-1522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1523]]

正不隨觀菩薩名 ^3-1523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1524]]

正不隨觀般若波羅蜜多、 ^3-1524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1525]]

正不隨觀菩提、 ^3-1525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1526]]

正不隨觀行、 ^3-1526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1527]]

正不隨觀不行。 ^3-1527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1528]]

所以者何？ ^3-1528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1529]]

名自性空 ^3-1529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1530]]

非空性、 ^3-1530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1531]]

色自性空 ^3-1531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1532]]

非空性， ^3-1532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1533]]

乃至識自性空 ^3-1533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1534]]

非空性。 ^3-1534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1535]]

何以故？ ^3-1535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1536]]

此色空性非即色， ^3-1536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1537]]

亦不離色別有空性。 ^3-1537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1538]]

色即是空性、 ^3-1538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1539]]

空性即是色， ^3-1539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1540]]

乃至識亦爾。 ^3-1540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1541]]

何以故？ ^3-1541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1542]]

此唯有名， ^3-1542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1543]]

所謂此是菩薩名。 ^3-1543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1544]]

此是菩薩、 ^3-1544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1545]]

此是般若波羅蜜多、 ^3-1545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1546]]

此是菩提、 ^3-1546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1547]]

此是色乃至此是識。 ^3-1547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1548]]

由彼自性無生無滅無染無淨， ^3-1548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1549]]

菩薩行般若波羅蜜多時 ^3-1549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1550]]

正不隨觀生， ^3-1550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1551]]

乃至正不隨觀淨。 ^3-1551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1552]]

何以故？ ^3-1552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1553]]

於所計度彼彼諸法假立客名，由隨客名而起言說， ^3-1553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1554]]

隨如是如是言說起如是如是執著。 ^3-1554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1555]]

菩薩於如是一切名正不隨觀， ^3-1555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1556]]

正不隨觀故不生執著。 ^3-1556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1557]]

於此經中，為對治無性分別故說如是言： 是菩薩實有菩薩，如是等。 ^3-1557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1558]]

由實有言，是有性義故。 ^3-1558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1559]]

為對治有性分別故， ^3-1559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1560]]

說如是言：正不隨觀菩薩， 乃至正不隨觀不行。 ^3-1560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1561]]

由遣補特伽羅及法二性故。 ^3-1561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1562]]

為對治增益分別故，說如是言：所以者何？ 名自性空。 ^3-1562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1563]]

由遣不實遍計所執自性故。 ^3-1563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1564]]

為對治損減分別故，說如是言：非空性。 ^3-1564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1565]]

由於此名，遍計所執自性遠離，性一切時有故。 ^3-1565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1566]]

為對治一性分別故，說如是言：此色空性非即色， ^3-1566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1567]]

乃至此識空性非即識。 ^3-1567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1568]]

由自性異故。 ^3-1568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1569]]

所以者何？ ^3-1569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1570]]

色等是遍計所執自性， ^3-1570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1571]]

空性是圓成實自性故。 ^3-1571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1572]]

為對治異性分別故，說如是言：亦不離色別有空性， ^3-1572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1574]]

乃至空性即是識。 ^3-1574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1575]]

由遍計所執自性無相故， ^3-1575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1576]]

離彼彼無性不可得故。 ^3-1576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1577]]

為對治自性分別故， ^3-1577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1578]]

說如是言：此唯有名，所謂此是色，乃至此是識等。 ^3-1578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1579]]

由離能詮無有決定所詮自性故。 ^3-1579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1580]]

為對治差別分別故，說如是言：由彼自性無生，乃至正不隨觀淨。 ^3-1580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1581]]

由遣生等差別相故。 ^3-1581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1582]]

為對治隨名義分別故， ^3-1582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1583]]

說如是言：於所計度彼彼諸法假立客名，由隨客名而起言說如是等。 ^3-1583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1584]]

為對治隨義名分別故， ^3-1584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1585]]

說如是言：菩薩於如是一切名正不隨觀， ^3-1585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1586]]

正不隨觀故不生執著。 ^3-1586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1587]]

由隨義於名不見不執故。 ^3-1587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1588]]

無分別者， ^3-1588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1589]]

略有三種： ^3-1589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1590]]

一知足無分別、 ^3-1590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1591]]

二無顛倒無分別、 ^3-1591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1592]]

三無戲論無分別。 ^3-1592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1593]]

如此三種， ^3-1593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1594]]

異生、 ^3-1594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1595]]

聲聞、 ^3-1595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1596]]

菩薩， ^3-1596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1597]]

如次第應知。 ^3-1597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1598]]

由諸異生隨於一無常等法性，究竟思已便生喜足。 ^3-1598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1599]]

謂是事必然，更無異望， ^3-1599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1600]]

是名知足無分別， ^3-1600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1601]]

爾時一切尋思分別皆止息故。 ^3-1601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1602]]

由諸聲聞於諸蘊中為對治常等顛倒故，如理觀察唯有色等法時， ^3-1602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1603]]

便得出世間智通達無我性， ^3-1603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1604]]

是名無顛倒無分別。 ^3-1604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1605]]

由諸菩薩知色等法唯戲論已， ^3-1605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1606]]

遂能除泯一切法相， ^3-1606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1607]]

得最極寂靜出世間智，通達遍滿真如， ^3-1607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1608]]

是名無戲論無分別。 ^3-1608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1609]]

此無分別智復離五相， ^3-1609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1610]]

謂非無作意故、 ^3-1610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1611]]

非超過故、 ^3-1611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1612]]

非寂止故、 ^3-1612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1613]]

非自性故、 ^3-1613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1614]]

非於所緣作加行故， ^3-1614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1615]]

名無分別。 ^3-1615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1616]]

所以者何？ ^3-1616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1617]]

若無作意故名無分別，熟眠醉等應是無分別智， ^3-1617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1618]]

由彼不思惟諸法相故。 ^3-1618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1619]]

若超過故名無分別， ^3-1619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1620]]

從第二靜慮已上一切地應是無分別智， ^3-1620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1621]]

由彼超過尋伺故。 ^3-1621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1622]]

若爾，三界心心法是分別體言，即為相違。 ^3-1622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1623]]

若寂止故名無分別，滅受想定應是無分別智， ^3-1623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1624]]

分別心心法於彼寂止故。 ^3-1624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1625]]

若爾，智亦應無。 ^3-1625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1626]]

若自性故名無分別，色等應是無分別智， ^3-1626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1627]]

彼非分別自性故。 ^3-1627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1628]]

若於所緣作加行故名無分別，即分別性應是無分別智。 ^3-1628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1629]]

若謂此是無分別，此加行相即分別相故。 ^3-1629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1630]]

是故無分別智非彼五相。 ^3-1630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1631]]

若爾，當云何觀無戲論無分別相？ ^3-1631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1632]]

謂於所緣不起加行。 ^3-1632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1633]]

此復云何？ ^3-1633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1634]]

若諸菩薩過隨順教觀察諸法，若性若相皆不真實。 ^3-1634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1635]]

由此觀察串習力所持故， ^3-1635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1636]]

不由加行，於如實無戲論界一切法真如中內心寂定， ^3-1636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1637]]

如是乃名無戲論無分別智。 ^3-1637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1638]]

復次若諸菩薩性是利根，云何復令修練根耶？ ^3-1638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1639]]

謂令依利軟根引 ^3-1639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1640]]

發利中根， ^3-1640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1641]]

復依利中根 ^3-1641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1642]]

引發利利根故。 ^3-1642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1643]]

前已說一切菩薩性是利根， ^3-1643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1644]]

而復說於時時中應修練根者， ^3-1644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1645]]

由於自種類復有軟等三品後後相引發故，說名練根。 ^3-1645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1646]]

若異此者，諸利根種性補特伽羅應根唯一品， ^3-1646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1647]]

諸菩薩等根品差別應不可得。 ^3-1647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1648]]

然有可得， ^3-1648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1649]]

是故利根復有差別。 ^3-1649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^3-1651]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十四 ^3-1651

## 阿毘達磨雜集論 — 4/4 ^4-0

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-1]]

大乘阿毘達磨雜集論卷第十五安慧菩薩糅釋上集論大唐三藏法師玄奘奉詔譯決擇分中論品第四之一 ^4-1

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-2]]

云何論決擇？ ^4-2

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-3]]

略說有七種， ^4-3

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-4]]

謂義決擇、 ^4-4

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-5]]

釋決擇、 ^4-5

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-6]]

分別顯示決擇、 ^4-6

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-7]]

等論決擇、 ^4-7

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-8]]

攝決擇、 ^4-8

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-9]]

論軌決擇、 ^4-9

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-10]]

祕密決擇。 ^4-10

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-11]]

義決擇者， ^4-11

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-12]]

謂依六義而起決擇。 ^4-12

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-13]]

何等六義？ ^4-13

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-14]]

謂自性義、 ^4-14

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-15]]

因義、 ^4-15

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-16]]

果義、 ^4-16

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-17]]

業義、 ^4-17

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-18]]

相應義、 ^4-18

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-19]]

轉義。 ^4-19

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-20]]

自性義者， ^4-20

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-21]]

謂遍計所執等三自性。 ^4-21

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-22]]

因義者， ^4-22

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-23]]

謂三因： ^4-23

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-24]]

一生因、 ^4-24

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-25]]

二轉因、 ^4-25

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-26]]

三成因。 ^4-26

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-27]]

生因者，謂因等四緣， ^4-27

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-29]]

由此能生諸有為故。 ^4-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-30]]

轉因者， ^4-30

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-31]]

謂由此次第彼法轉， ^4-31

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-32]]

如無明緣行等； ^4-32

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-33]]

乃至集滅， ^4-33

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-34]]

由此次第染淨轉故。 ^4-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-35]]

成因者，謂現量可得不可得等，正說所攝， ^4-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-36]]

由此能成立先所未了所成義故。 ^4-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-37]]

果義者， ^4-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-38]]

謂五果。 ^4-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-39]]

何等為五？ ^4-39

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-40]]

一異熟果、 ^4-40

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-41]]

二等流果、 ^4-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-42]]

三增上果、 ^4-42

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-43]]

四士用果、 ^4-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-44]]

五離繫果。 ^4-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-45]]

異熟果者， ^4-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-46]]

謂阿賴耶識等。 ^4-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-47]]

等流果者， ^4-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-48]]

謂前生諸善法所起自相續後諸善法。 ^4-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-49]]

增上果者， ^4-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-50]]

謂一切有情共業增上力所感器世間。 ^4-50

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-51]]

士用果者， ^4-51

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-52]]

謂稼穡等。 ^4-52

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-53]]

離繫果者，謂由聖道，隨眠永滅。 ^4-53

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-54]]

業義者， ^4-54

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-55]]

謂五種業： ^4-55

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-56]]

一取受業、 ^4-56

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-57]]

二作用業、 ^4-57

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-58]]

三加行業、 ^4-58

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-59]]

四轉變業、 ^4-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-60]]

五證得業。 ^4-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-61]]

此五業義如前業染中釋。 ^4-61

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-62]]

相應義者，謂五種相應。 ^4-62

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-63]]

何等為五？ ^4-63

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-64]]

一聚結相應、 ^4-64

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-65]]

二隨逐相應、 ^4-65

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-66]]

三連綴相應、 ^4-66

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-67]]

四分位相應、 ^4-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-68]]

五轉變相應。 ^4-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-69]]

聚結相應者， ^4-69

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-70]]

謂於舍等有木石等。 ^4-70

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-71]]

隨逐相應者， ^4-71

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-72]]

謂隨眠等因， ^4-72

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-73]]

由有此故，雖煩惱等不現行而說與彼相應。 ^4-73

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-74]]

連綴相應者， ^4-74

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-75]]

謂親屬等展轉相應。 ^4-75

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-76]]

分位相應者， ^4-76

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-77]]

謂攝受益等相續分位， ^4-77

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-78]]

由此分位，現前說名與樂相應，乃至與不苦不樂相應故。 ^4-78

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-79]]

轉變相應者， ^4-79

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-80]]

謂客塵煩惱等現前， ^4-80

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-81]]

由有此故，說與貪等信等相應。 ^4-81

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-82]]

轉義者，謂五種轉： ^4-82

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-83]]

一相轉、 ^4-83

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-84]]

二安住轉、 ^4-84

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-85]]

三顛倒轉、 ^4-85

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-86]]

四不顛倒轉、 ^4-86

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-87]]

五差別轉。 ^4-87

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-89]]

相轉者， ^4-89

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-90]]

謂生等三有為相， ^4-90

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-91]]

由彼三相差別轉故。 ^4-91

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-92]]

安住轉者， ^4-92

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-93]]

謂所持法住能持中而轉故。 ^4-93

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-95]]

顛倒轉者， ^4-95

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-96]]

謂雜染法不如實轉故。 ^4-96

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-97]]

不顛倒轉者， ^4-97

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-98]]

謂清淨法如實轉故。 ^4-98

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-99]]

差別轉者， ^4-99

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-101]]

謂一切行過去未來現在內外等差別轉故。 ^4-101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-102]]

釋決擇者， ^4-102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-103]]

謂能解釋諸經宗要開發彼義故。 ^4-103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-105]]

此復云何？ ^4-105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-106]]

略有六種， ^4-106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-107]]

謂所遍知事、 ^4-107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-108]]

所遍知義、 ^4-108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-109]]

遍知因緣、 ^4-109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-110]]

遍知自性、 ^4-110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-111]]

遍知果 ^4-111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-112]]

彼證受。 ^4-112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-113]]

由此六義，隨其所應遍釋諸經故，名釋決擇。 ^4-113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-114]]

所遍知事，謂蘊等。 ^4-114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-115]]

所遍知義，謂無常等。 ^4-115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-116]]

遍知因緣，謂淨尸羅守根門等。 ^4-116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-117]]

遍知自性，謂菩提分法。 ^4-117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-118]]

遍知果，謂解脫。 ^4-118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-119]]

彼證受，謂解脫智見。 ^4-119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-120]]

又十四門辯釋決擇。 ^4-120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-121]]

何等十四？ ^4-121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-122]]

謂攝釋門、 ^4-122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-123]]

攝事門、 ^4-123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-124]]

總別分門、 ^4-124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-125]]

後後開引門、 ^4-125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-126]]

遮止門、 ^4-126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-127]]

轉變字門、 ^4-127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-128]]

壞不壞門、 ^4-128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-129]]

安立數取趣門、 ^4-129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-130]]

安立差別門、 ^4-130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-131]]

理趣門、 ^4-131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-132]]

遍知等門、 ^4-132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-133]]

力無力門、 ^4-133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-134]]

別別引門、 ^4-134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-135]]

引發門。 ^4-135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-136]]

攝釋門者， ^4-136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-137]]

謂若於是處宣說諸經緣起所以，句義次第意趣釋難。 ^4-137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-138]]

攝事門者， ^4-138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-139]]

謂若於是處約學事、聖諦事等辯釋諸經。 ^4-139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-140]]

如說諸惡者莫作、 ^4-140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-141]]

諸善者奉行、 ^4-141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-142]]

善調伏自心、 ^4-142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-143]]

是諸佛聖教， ^4-143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-144]]

此伽他中依三學說如是等。 ^4-144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-145]]

總別分門者， ^4-145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-146]]

謂若處顯示先以一句總標，後以餘句別釋。 ^4-146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-147]]

如《十二暇總集經》中， ^4-147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-148]]

先說自圓淨、 ^4-148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-149]]

他圓淨二句， ^4-149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-150]]

後如其次第五句五句別釋， ^4-150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-151]]

如是等。 ^4-151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-163]]

後後開引門者， ^4-163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-164]]

謂若處顯示能為後後開引所依故， ^4-164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-165]]

此諸法如是次第說。 ^4-165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-166]]

如信等五根先後次第， ^4-166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-167]]

必先信受乃發精進， ^4-167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-168]]

發精進已然後念住， ^4-168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-169]]

既念住已心得安定， ^4-169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-170]]

心安定已方如實知，如是等。 ^4-170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-171]]

遮止門者， ^4-171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-172]]

謂若處顯示依止此事、遮止此事。 ^4-172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-173]]

如《斤柯喻經》中依止漏盡遮止四種補特伽羅： ^4-173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-174]]

一處正法外、 ^4-174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-175]]

二處正法中但得聞思便生喜足、 ^4-175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-176]]

三於修慧中心生怯弱、 ^4-176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-177]]

四資糧未滿。 告諸比丘： ^4-177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-178]]

我知我見我說漏盡，如是等一段經文遮止第一。 ^4-178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-179]]

不勤精進修習觀行者，遮止第二。 ^4-179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-180]]

說斤柯喻，遮止第三。 ^4-180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-181]]

說船筏喻，遮止第四。 ^4-181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-182]]

轉變字門者， ^4-182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-183]]

謂若處顯示轉餘顯了字義變成餘義， ^4-183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-184]]

如不信不知恩等伽他，後當說。 ^4-184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-185]]

壞不壞門者， ^4-185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-186]]

謂若處顯示失壞 ^4-186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-187]]

不失壞， ^4-187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-188]]

彼二方便 ^4-188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-189]]

彼二差別。 ^4-189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-190]]

如《善生經》說： ^4-190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-191]]

失壞者，謂染著內外依事。 ^4-191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-192]]

{外依事四宅妻子等為相。} ^4-192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-193]]

內依事五取蘊為相， ^4-193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-194]]

外依事四宅妻子等為相。 ^4-194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-195]]

不失壞者， ^4-195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-196]]

謂遠離此二種染著。 ^4-196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-197]]

失壞方便者， ^4-197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-198]]

謂不出家， ^4-198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-199]]

雖復出家而行放逸不得漏盡， ^4-199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-200]]

與此相違是無失壞方便。 ^4-200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-201]]

佛告善生：族姓子！有二種事俱為美妙， ^4-201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-202]]

若落鬚髮乃至趣於非家； ^4-202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-203]]

若盡諸漏，乃至自稱不受後有者。 ^4-203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-204]]

此正顯不失壞及彼方便， ^4-204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-205]]

兼顯失壞及彼方便， 由與此相相違故。 ^4-205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-206]]

不失壞差別者，如伽他中顯， ^4-206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-207]]

謂諸比丘美妙寂靜離諸漏。 ^4-207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-208]]

此顯出家及漏盡。 ^4-208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-209]]

為顯漏盡復說餘句， ^4-209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-210]]

謂離欲離繫縛， ^4-210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-211]]

無執受涅槃， ^4-211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-212]]

{摧伏魔所使者。} ^4-212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-213]]

任持最後身， ^4-213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-214]]

摧伏魔所使者。 ^4-214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-215]]

此顯由世間道離欲， ^4-215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-216]]

由出世道永斷順下分結、 ^4-216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-217]]

永斷順上分結、 ^4-217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-218]]

永斷內依事。 ^4-218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-219]]

此則略說因盡果盡， ^4-219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-220]]

亦兼顯失壞差別。 由與此相相違故。 ^4-220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-221]]

安立數取趣門者， ^4-221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-222]]

謂若處顯示依爾所補特伽羅說如是言。 ^4-222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-223]]

如《水喻經》中 ^4-223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-224]]

依二數取趣說三種四種差別言。 ^4-224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-225]]

何等為二？ ^4-225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-226]]

謂異生 ^4-226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-227]]

及見諦異生。 ^4-227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-228]]

差別有三， ^4-228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-229]]

謂無白法、 ^4-229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-230]]

少白法、 ^4-230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-231]]

多白法。 ^4-231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-232]]

見諦差別有四， ^4-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-233]]

謂住四果， ^4-233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-234]]

三有學、 ^4-234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-235]]

一無學。 ^4-235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-236]]

安立差別門者， ^4-236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-237]]

謂若處顯示四句等所問義。 ^4-237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-238]]

如《無常經》說： ^4-238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-239]]

若正觀者一切觀色耶？ ^4-239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-240]]

設觀色者一切正觀耶？ 應作四句。 ^4-240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-241]]

初句， ^4-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-242]]

謂於受等四蘊 ^4-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-243]]

無有常淨樂我顛倒增益， ^4-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-244]]

又觀彼為應知應斷。 ^4-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-245]]

第二句， 謂於色蘊 ^4-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-246]]

有常淨樂我顛倒增益， ^4-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-247]]

又觀彼為不應知不應斷。 ^4-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-248]]

第三句，謂於色蘊 ^4-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-249]]

無有常淨樂我顛倒增益， ^4-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-250]]

又觀彼為應知應斷。 ^4-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-251]]

第四句，謂於受等四蘊 ^4-251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-252]]

有常淨樂我顛倒增益， ^4-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-253]]

又觀彼為不應知不應斷。 ^4-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-254]]

如因色作四句，如是因受等一切處應廣說。 ^4-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-255]]

乃至說言：若所作已辦者，一切自謂不受後有耶？ ^4-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-256]]

設自謂不受後有者，一切所作已辦耶？ ^4-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-257]]

此應作四句。 ^4-257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-258]]

初句，謂諸異生乃至命終恒行妙行。 ^4-258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-259]]

第二句，謂斷見者。 ^4-259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-260]]

第三句，謂無學。 ^4-260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-261]]

第四句，謂除上爾所相。 ^4-261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-262]]

理趣門者， ^4-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-263]]

謂若處顯示六理趣義。 ^4-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-264]]

何等為六？ ^4-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-265]]

一真義理趣、 ^4-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-266]]

二證得理趣、 ^4-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-267]]

三教導理趣、 ^4-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-268]]

四離二邊理趣、 ^4-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-269]]

五不思議理趣、 ^4-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-270]]

六意樂理趣。 ^4-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-271]]

如是六種，前三如其次第應隨後三決了。 ^4-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-272]]

如《愛味經》中 ^4-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-273]]

佛告諸比丘： ^4-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-274]]

於色有味乃至廣說。 ^4-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-275]]

此中顯示由遠離增益損減二邊理趣， ^4-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-276]]

決了真義理趣。 ^4-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-277]]

有味有患有出離者， ^4-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-278]]

顯離損減邊。 ^4-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-279]]

於色乃至於識者， ^4-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-280]]

顯離增益邊。 ^4-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-281]]

由顯示染污清淨唯依諸蘊不依我故。 ^4-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-282]]

乃至告諸比丘： ^4-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-283]]

我自證知由此故，乃至已證覺無上正等菩提者。 ^4-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-284]]

顯由不思議理趣決了證得理趣。 ^4-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-285]]

此顯真證內自所受故。 ^4-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-286]]

如是一切經皆是教導理趣， ^4-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-287]]

應隨意樂理趣決了。 ^4-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-288]]

謂依所遍知事、 ^4-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-289]]

所遍知義、 ^4-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-290]]

遍知、 ^4-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-291]]

遍知果， ^4-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-292]]

彼證受， ^4-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-293]]

意樂說此經。 ^4-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-294]]

所遍知事者， ^4-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-295]]

謂色等。 ^4-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-296]]

所遍知義者， ^4-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-297]]

謂有味等。 ^4-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-298]]

由此差別義， ^4-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-299]]

遍知色等事故。 ^4-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-300]]

遍知者， ^4-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-301]]

謂於五取蘊， ^4-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-302]]

由如是三轉如實遍知。 ^4-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-303]]

遍知果者， ^4-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-304]]

謂從此諸天世間 ^4-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-305]]

乃至并天人皆得解脫乃至極解脫。 ^4-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-306]]

彼證受者， ^4-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-307]]

謂自證知我已證覺無上正等菩提。 ^4-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-308]]

遍知等門者， ^4-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-309]]

謂若處顯示依真實相宣說遍知相義、 ^4-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-310]]

永斷相義、 ^4-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-311]]

作證相義、 ^4-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-312]]

修習相義， ^4-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-313]]

即此真實相等品類差別相義、 ^4-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-314]]

能依所依相屬相義、 ^4-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-315]]

遍知等障礙法相義、 ^4-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-316]]

遍知等隨順法相義、 ^4-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-317]]

於不遍知等 ^4-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-318]]

及遍知等過失功德相義， ^4-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-319]]

此亦如《愛味經》廣說。 ^4-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-320]]

真實相者，謂取蘊所攝苦諦相。 ^4-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-321]]

遍知相者，謂即於此有味等如實知。 ^4-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-322]]

永斷相作證相者， ^4-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-323]]

謂從一切世間得解脫， ^4-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-324]]

由永斷諸障證得轉依故。 ^4-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-325]]

修習相者， ^4-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-326]]

謂離顛倒心多修習住。 ^4-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-327]]

品類差別相者， ^4-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-328]]

真實相有五種差別， ^4-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-329]]

謂色乃至識。 ^4-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-330]]

遍知相有三種差別， ^4-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-331]]

謂味，由味故乃至出離， ^4-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-332]]

由出離故如實知。 ^4-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-333]]

永斷相、作證相各有二種差別， ^4-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-334]]

謂煩惱解脫、苦解脫。 ^4-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-335]]

從此諸天世間 ^4-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-336]]

乃至并天人皆得解脫者，顯煩惱解脫。 ^4-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-337]]

為顯此差別義故， ^4-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-338]]

次說出離言。 ^4-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-339]]

何以故？ ^4-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-340]]

由餘經言：出離云何？ ^4-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-341]]

謂若於是處貪欲永滅、貪欲永斷，超過貪欲故。 ^4-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-342]]

如是由能生未來苦煩惱得離繫故， ^4-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-343]]

苦亦解脫。 為顯此差別義故， ^4-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-344]]

次說離繫縛極解脫。 ^4-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-345]]

修習相有二種差別， ^4-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-346]]

謂見道、 ^4-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-347]]

修道。 ^4-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-348]]

離顛倒心者顯示見道， ^4-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-349]]

多修習住者顯示修道。 ^4-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-350]]

能依所依相屬相者， ^4-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-351]]

謂顯示真實相等為後後所依性。 ^4-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-352]]

遍知等障礙法相者， ^4-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-353]]

謂如是三轉不如實知。 ^4-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-354]]

遍知等隨順法相者， ^4-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-355]]

謂觀察如所安立色等法中味等相。 ^4-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-356]]

於不遍知等過失相者， ^4-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-357]]

謂不解脫乃至不證覺無上正等菩提。 ^4-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-358]]

與此相違，名於遍知等功德相。 ^4-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-359]]

力無力門者， ^4-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-360]]

謂若處顯示諸一一句皆有功能，若不說一句義即不了。 ^4-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-361]]

如《緣起經》說： ^4-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-362]]

此有故彼有， ^4-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-363]]

此生故彼生， ^4-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-364]]

所謂無明緣行等。 ^4-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-365]]

如是諸句一一皆有功能， ^4-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-366]]

如前緣起相中說。 ^4-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-367]]

別別引門者， ^4-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-368]]

謂若處顯示先標經一句， ^4-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-369]]

後以無量義門廣釋。 ^4-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-370]]

如經言：若比丘成就六法， ^4-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-371]]

尚能口風吹碎高廣大雪山王， ^4-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-372]]

況無明死屍。 ^4-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-373]]

何等為六？ ^4-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-375]]

若諸比丘心生善巧乃至方便善巧。 ^4-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-377]]

云何比丘心生善巧？ ^4-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-378]]

所謂比丘離欲惡不善法， ^4-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-379]]

乃至第四靜慮具足住， ^4-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-380]]

如是比丘心生善巧。 ^4-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-381]]

云何比丘心住善巧？ ^4-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-382]]

所謂比丘善修習故，所有順退分靜慮轉為順住分， ^4-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-383]]

如是比丘心住善巧。 ^4-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-384]]

云何比丘心起善巧？ ^4-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-385]]

所謂比丘善修習故，所有順住分靜慮 ^4-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-386]]

轉為順勝進分， ^4-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-387]]

如是比丘心起善巧。 ^4-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-388]]

云何比丘生長善巧？ ^4-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-389]]

所謂比丘未生善法方便令生，乃至廣說二正斷， ^4-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-390]]

如是比丘生長善巧。 ^4-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-391]]

云何比丘損減善巧？ ^4-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-392]]

所謂比丘已生惡法方便令斷，乃至廣說二正斷， ^4-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-393]]

如是比丘損減善巧。 ^4-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-394]]

云何比丘方便善巧？ ^4-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-395]]

所謂比丘欲三摩地斷行成就修如意足，乃至廣說四如意足， ^4-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-396]]

如是比丘方便善巧。 ^4-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-397]]

引發門者， ^4-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-398]]

謂若處顯示一一句中宣說四句， ^4-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-399]]

是一一句復分四句， ^4-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-400]]

如是展轉無邊引發。 ^4-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-401]]

如引佛經言： ^4-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-402]]

諸菩薩有四種淨修菩提法， ^4-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-403]]

一者善修空性、 ^4-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-404]]

二者於諸眾生心無罣礙、 ^4-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-405]]

三者常攝利益諸菩薩眾、 ^4-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-406]]

四者以無染心廣開法施。 ^4-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-407]]

如是四法於自利利他門 ^4-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-408]]

淨修菩提， ^4-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-409]]

為欲對治四種所治障故。 ^4-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-410]]

何等為四？ ^4-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-411]]

一貪著定味、 ^4-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-412]]

二瞋恚、 ^4-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-413]]

三慢、 ^4-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-414]]

四愛著利養。 ^4-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-415]]

又有差別， ^4-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-416]]

初顯煩惱斷對治， ^4-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-417]]

餘顯遠離下劣乘。 ^4-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-418]]

由諸菩薩三種因緣遠下劣乘： ^4-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-419]]

一攝受一切有情、 ^4-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-420]]

二已入法者令成熟、 ^4-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-421]]

三未入法者令入正法。 ^4-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-422]]

又有差別， ^4-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-423]]

初顯智資糧， ^4-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-424]]

餘顯福資糧。 ^4-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-425]]

此三差別者， ^4-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-426]]

攝受成熟令入三門，各能生長勝品福故。 ^4-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-427]]

又由二緣差別： ^4-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-428]]

一由意樂，謂慈心俱； ^4-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-429]]

二由正行，謂說證教二行。 ^4-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-430]]

又諸菩薩成就四法能修空性： ^4-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-431]]

一者於內心無動搖、 ^4-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-432]]

二者信解擇力所持、 ^4-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-433]]

三者於一切法如實通達、 ^4-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-434]]

四者解脫一切障。 ^4-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-435]]

如是四法顯修所依及修差別。 ^4-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-436]]

以何為依？ ^4-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-437]]

謂靜慮波羅蜜多。 ^4-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-438]]

云何修差別？ ^4-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-439]]

一由異生道， ^4-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-440]]

謂聞思力所持； ^4-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-441]]

二由學道， ^4-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-442]]

謂達諸法實性； ^4-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-443]]

三由無學道， ^4-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-444]]

謂脫一切障。 ^4-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-445]]

又諸菩薩成就四法， ^4-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-446]]

於諸有情心無罣礙： ^4-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-447]]

一者修慈、 ^4-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-448]]

二者不毀正行、 ^4-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-449]]

三者不分別相、 ^4-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-450]]

四者堪忍劬勞。 ^4-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-451]]

如是四法顯所依及無罣礙心差別。 ^4-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-452]]

誰為所依？ ^4-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-453]]

謂過去生所修慈。 ^4-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-454]]

云何無罣礙心差別？ ^4-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-455]]

謂於住邪行所心無違毀、 ^4-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-456]]

於怨家所不分別怨相， ^4-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-457]]

為利益他精勤無懈。 ^4-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-458]]

又諸菩薩成就四法， ^4-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-459]]

常能攝益諸菩薩眾： ^4-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-460]]

一者不自稱量、 ^4-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-461]]

二者正教誨轉、 ^4-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-462]]

三者柔和易可共住、 ^4-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-463]]

四者精勤承事供養。 ^4-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-464]]

如是四法顯示所依及攝益差別。 ^4-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-465]]

何等為依？ ^4-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-466]]

謂摧伏憍慢。 ^4-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-467]]

云何攝益差別？ ^4-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-468]]

謂後所說三句。 ^4-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-469]]

於劣等勝三種菩薩所，如其次第。 ^4-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-470]]

又諸菩薩成就四法， ^4-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-471]]

能無染心廣開法施： ^4-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-472]]

一者善達障難、 ^4-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-473]]

二者善能除遣愚癡沈沒、 ^4-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-474]]

三者歡喜攝受、 ^4-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-475]]

四者愛樂為依怙法。 ^4-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-476]]

如是四法顯示所依 ^4-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-477]]

及廣開法施差別。 ^4-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-478]]

何等為依？ ^4-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-479]]

謂善通達利養恭敬是障難法。 ^4-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-480]]

云何廣開法施差別？ ^4-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-481]]

謂示現教導讚勵慶喜。 ^4-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-482]]

示現者於愚癡沈沒， ^4-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-483]]

教導讚勵者於著放逸自輕下劣， ^4-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-484]]

慶喜者於正行圓滿， ^4-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-485]]

由性愛樂法故。 ^4-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-486]]

如是前四句中，一一句復引發四句差別，如是等名引發門。 ^4-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-487]]

分別顯示決擇者， ^4-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-488]]

謂於如所說蘊等法中，隨其所應 ^4-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-489]]

作一行順前句順後句， ^4-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-490]]

二句三句四句述可句遮止句等。 ^4-490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-493]]

一行者，即問論法，謂以一法與餘法一一互相問已， ^4-493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-494]]

除此法更以第二法與餘法互相問， ^4-494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-495]]

如是一一問一切法。 ^4-495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-496]]

如有問言：若成就眼處亦色處耶？ ^4-496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-497]]

設成就色處亦眼處耶？ ^4-497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-498]]

此應作順前句答。 若成就眼處亦耳處耶？ ^4-498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-500]]

此應作四句答。 ^4-500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-501]]

如是乃至對意處，如理應說。 ^4-501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-502]]

若成就眼處亦法處耶？ 此亦應作順前句答。 ^4-502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-503]]

若成就色處亦眼處耶？ ^4-503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-504]]

設成就眼處亦色處耶？ 此應作順後句答。 ^4-504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-505]]

若成就色處亦耳處耶？ 此亦應作順後句答。 ^4-505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-506]]

如是乃至對法處，如理應說。 ^4-506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-507]]

若成就耳處亦眼處耶？ 此應作四句答。 ^4-507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-508]]

如是乃至對法處，如理應說。 ^4-508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-509]]

如是一一次第漸減，諸處更互如理應說。 ^4-509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-510]]

順前句者，謂於諸法中隨取二法更互相問， ^4-510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-511]]

依止前法以答所問。 ^4-511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-512]]

如有問言：若智亦所知耶？ 設所知亦智耶？ 此應作順前句答： ^4-512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-513]]

諸智亦所知； ^4-513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-514]]

有所知非智， ^4-514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-515]]

謂餘法。 ^4-515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-516]]

順後句者， ^4-516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-517]]

謂即二法展轉相問，依止後法以答所問。 ^4-517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-518]]

如有問言：若所取亦能取耶？ 設能取亦所取耶？ ^4-518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-519]]

此應作順後句答： ^4-519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-520]]

諸能取亦所取； ^4-520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-521]]

有所取非能取， ^4-521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-522]]

謂色等五境及法處，除相應。 ^4-522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-523]]

二句者，謂於所問應二句答， ^4-523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-524]]

不得有餘。 ^4-524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-525]]

如有依蘊建立、 ^4-525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-526]]

依界建立而發問言： ^4-526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-527]]

若蘊數亦界數耶？ ^4-527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-528]]

設界數亦蘊數耶？ ^4-528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-529]]

此應作二句答： ^4-529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-530]]

或蘊數非界數， ^4-530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-531]]

謂色蘊識蘊。 ^4-531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-532]]

何以故？ ^4-532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-533]]

無有一界全攝色蘊相或全攝識蘊相故。 ^4-533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-534]]

或界數非蘊數， ^4-534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-535]]

謂法界。 ^4-535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-536]]

三句者，謂於所問唯三句答。 ^4-536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-537]]

如有問言：若蘊數亦處數耶？ ^4-537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-538]]

設處數亦蘊數耶。 ^4-538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-539]]

此應三句答： ^4-539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-540]]

或蘊數非處數， ^4-540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-541]]

謂色蘊； ^4-541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-542]]

或處數非蘊數， ^4-542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-543]]

謂法處； ^4-543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-544]]

或蘊數亦處數， ^4-544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-545]]

謂識蘊意處。 ^4-545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-546]]

俱非數者，於蘊處中決定不有。 ^4-546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-547]]

四句者，謂於所問作四句答。 ^4-547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-548]]

如有問言：若成就眼根亦耳根耶？ 設成就耳根亦眼根耶？ ^4-548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-549]]

應四句答。 ^4-549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-550]]

初句，謂聾者眼根已生不捨。 ^4-550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-551]]

第二句，謂盲者耳根已生不捨。 ^4-551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-552]]

第三句，謂眼耳根已生不捨。 ^4-552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-553]]

第四句，除上爾所相。 ^4-553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-554]]

述可句者，謂於所問順爾而答， ^4-554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-555]]

以如是言述可所問。 ^4-555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-556]]

如有問言：諸無常者皆是行耶？ ^4-556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-557]]

設當是行皆無常耶？ ^4-557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-558]]

應述可答：所問如是。 ^4-558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-559]]

遮止句者， ^4-559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-560]]

謂於所問不爾而答， ^4-560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-561]]

以不爾言遮止所問。 ^4-561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-562]]

如有問言：蘊外諸行幾諦攝耶？ ^4-562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-563]]

應遮止答：蘊外無行。 ^4-563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-564]]

等論決擇者， ^4-564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-565]]

謂依八何八若之詞， ^4-565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-566]]

問答決擇一切真偽。 ^4-566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-567]]

八何詞者， ^4-567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-568]]

且如問言： ^4-568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-569]]

何誰無所得？ ^4-569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-570]]

謂已得般若波羅蜜多菩薩摩訶薩。 ^4-570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-571]]

何所無所得？ ^4-571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-572]]

謂所取相能取相。 ^4-572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-573]]

用何無所得？ ^4-573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-574]]

謂用般若波羅蜜多。 ^4-574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-575]]

為何無所得？ ^4-575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-576]]

謂為救脫一切有情令住無上正等菩提。 ^4-576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-577]]

由何無所得？ ^4-577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-578]]

謂由遇佛出世、聽聞正法、如理作意、法隨法行。 ^4-578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-579]]

何之無所得？ ^4-579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-580]]

謂一切法之無所得。 ^4-580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-581]]

於何無所得？ ^4-581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-582]]

謂於勝解行地乃至第十菩薩地。 ^4-582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-583]]

幾何無所得？ ^4-583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-584]]

謂十一種： ^4-584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-585]]

一已生已滅、 ^4-585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-586]]

二未生、 ^4-586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-587]]

三現前、 ^4-587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-588]]

四因力所生、 ^4-588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-589]]

五善友力所生、 ^4-589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-590]]

六一切法無所得、 ^4-590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-591]]

七空性無所得、 ^4-591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-592]]

八有我慢、 ^4-592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-593]]

九無我慢、 ^4-593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-594]]

十未具資糧、 ^4-594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-595]]

十一已具資糧， ^4-595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-596]]

如是十一無所得。 ^4-596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-597]]

隨所有過去未來現在、 ^4-597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-598]]

若內若外、 ^4-598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-599]]

若麁若細、 ^4-599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-600]]

若劣若勝、 ^4-600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-601]]

若遠若近次第應知。 ^4-601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-602]]

如何詞，若詞亦爾， ^4-602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-603]]

謂若能無所得、 ^4-603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-604]]

若所無所得、 ^4-604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-605]]

若用無所得、 ^4-605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-606]]

若為無所得、 ^4-606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-607]]

若由無所得、 ^4-607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-608]]

若彼無所得、 ^4-608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-609]]

若於無所得、 ^4-609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-610]]

若爾所無所得， ^4-610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-611]]

如是一切處盡當知。 ^4-611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-612]]

復有四種等論決擇道理， ^4-612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-614]]

謂能破、能立、能斷、能覺。 ^4-614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-615]]

能破者， ^4-615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-616]]

謂遮破他宗，言彼惡說非為善事。 ^4-616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-617]]

能立者， ^4-617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-618]]

謂建立自宗，言此善說真為善事。 ^4-618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-619]]

能斷者， ^4-619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-620]]

謂能決種種他所生疑。 ^4-620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-621]]

能覺者， ^4-621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-622]]

謂開曉愚情令解妙義。 ^4-622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-623]]

所依等六者，一所依、二依、三攝、四相應、五成就、六雜染。 ^4-623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-624]]

識等六者，一識、二受、三想、四作意、五智、六遍知。 ^4-624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-625]]

清淨等六者，一清淨、二詞、三出離、四甚深、五解脫門、六入一切法。 ^4-625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-626]]

方便等六者，一方便、二通達、三修、四圓證、五究竟、六引發。 ^4-626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-627]]

自性有四種，謂實自性、假自性、世俗自性、勝義自性。 ^4-627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-628]]

因有四種，謂生因、成因、轉因、還因。 ^4-628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-629]]

境界有七種，謂有相境、有分別境、對治境、安住境、增益境、損減境、自在境。 ^4-629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh4054-commentary-on-the-compendium-of-abhidharma.md#^4-630]]

行相有五種，謂分析行相、差別行相、正解行相、觀察行相、作隨作行相。 ^4-630
